### **About House of Thule**

House of Thule is EverQuest's 17th expansion.

- It was announced on August 6, 2010, with an official launch date of October 12, 2010.

- The initial announcement may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=22931) and the expansion release patch message may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=23631).

* * *

#### **New Features**

- +5 Level Increase (to Level 90)
- More than 800 AAs (or unofficially, about 1200 more)
- In-game Housing

  - Check out the Sunrise Hills accessed through the Guild Lobby

- New Housing Trophy System

  - Display your trophies and receive statistical benefits

- Hundreds of new housing items to decorate your house to your own style
- 18 Raids

  - Collect valuable rewards from all new adventures

- 13 Zones

  - Be the first to discover untouched lands of Norrath

- 450+ Spells

  - Exponentially add to your caster's arsenal

- 17 Missions

  - Experience intriguing lore and immersive Missions

- 120+ Quests

  - Challenge your skills and gather useful loot

- Inventory increase

  - Indulge the packrat in you and collect even more game goodies

#### **Zones**

- Check out [this link](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zcont=House+of+Thule) for a list of zones in the House of Thule expansion.

- There are four difficulty Tiers in this expansion:

- Tier 1 (equivalent to [Underfoot's Tier 8](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_Underfoot#Tier_8:))

  - Feerrott, the Dream
  - House of Thule, the Lower Floors

- Tier 2 (a small step above [Underfoot's Tier 9](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_Underfoot#Tier_9:))

  - Erudin Burning
  - The Grounds
  - The Library
  - The Well

- Tier 3

  - House of Thule, the Upper Floors
  - Al'Kabor's Nightmare
  - Miragul's Nightmare
  - Fear Itself

- Tier 4

  - Morell's Castle
  - Sanctum Somnium

- A screenshot of the in-game map of zone connections can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/gallery/ingamemaps/thule.jpg).

#### **Getting There**

- There are three ways to get to the House of Thule expansion:

  - Click on the statue on the banks of low level Feerrott's Greenblood River (ports you to the new Feerrott).
  - Use the Guild Hall portal to port to the new Feerrott.
  - Wizard and Druid port spells to The Grounds.
  - If you click on the Oggok portal in PoK it now asks you if you want to go to Feerrott or Feerrott the Dream. Feerrott the Dream, is the base zone of House of Thule.

#### **Spells**

- Rank I Spells

  - These spells are vendor-bought.

    - Vendors are at location -1375, 665, 2 in the new Feerrott (southern boundary outside the House of Thule zone).

- Rank II Spells

  - These spells result from Nebulous Dream Fragment turn-ins.
  - Fragments are droppable. Spells are TRADEABLE.
  - See [Kaelynn Stearns](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=35718) in the Feerrott (location 520, 2800, 30). You can request a task from her to do your spell turn-in and actually choose which spell you want.

    - [Minor Nebulous Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90072) results in a [Level 86 spell/disc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=5115)
    - [Lesser Nebulous Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=91160) results in a [Level 87 spell/disc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=5116)
    - [Median Neblulous Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90073) results in a [Level 88 spell/disc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=5117)
    - [Greater Nebulous Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90069) results in a [Level 89 spell/disc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=5118)
    - [Glowing Nebulous Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=91152) results in a [Level 90 spell/disc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=5119)

  - Note: Some Rk. II spells may require amiable [Dream Delvers](http://eqbeastiary.allakhazam.com/faction.html?faction=574) faction.

- Rank III Spells

  - These spells result from raid turn-ins.
  - Fragments and spells are TRADEABLE.
  - See [Spensur](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=35726) in the Grounds (location -60, -1685, -100). As with the Rank II spells, you request a task from him and can actually choose which spell you want.

    - [Minor Tangible Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90905) results in a Level 86 spell/disc
    - [Lesser Tangible Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90966) results in a Level 87 spell/disc
    - [Median Tangible Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90967) results in a Level 88 spell/disc
    - [Greater Tangible Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90875) results in a Level 89 spell/disc
    - [Glowing Tangible Dream Fragment](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=90906) results in a Level 90 spell/disc

  - Note: Some Rk. III spells may require amiable [Dream Delvers](http://eqbeastiary.allakhazam.com/faction.html?faction=574) faction.

## Overview

House of Thule progression is set up in the same manner as it was in Underfoot. Actual progression involves only the group missions, so you can complete only those missions as long as you have someone to request them for you.

The first two tiers are open zones:

- Tier 1 :

  - [Feerrott, the Dream](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=747)
  - [House of Thule, Lower Floors](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=748)

- Tier 2 :

  - [Erudin Burning](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=752)
  - [The Grounds](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=749)
  - [The Library](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=751)
  - [The Well](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=750)

Tier 3 zones can be accessed by completing missions in Tiers 1 and 2.

- Tier 3 :

  - [House of Thule, Upper Floors](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=753)
  - [Al'Kabor's Nightmare](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=754)
  - [Miragul's Nightmare](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=755)
  - [Fear Itself](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=756)

Tier 4 zones can be accessed by completing missions in Tier 3.

- Tier 4 :

  - [Morell's Castle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=757)
  - [Sanctum Somnium](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=758)

Most zones have two group missions in them (each of which is required for the "Hero of \_\_\_\_\_" Achievements), however actual progression involves only one mission per zone.

You must be at least Level 75 to participate in House of Thule tasks and missions.

You must be at least Level 87 to request most of the T3 tasks and ALL missions.

You must be at least Level 88 to request all of the T4 tasks and missions.

Listed below here are progression tasks by zone. **Bolded** tasks (each of which is a group mission in an instanced zone) are required for everyone. Unbolded tasks are required only to request the group missions.

* * *

You say, 'Found any dreams? Have you gone mad?'

You say, 'How can I get a spell?'

You have been assigned the task 'Glowing Nebulous Dream Fragment'.
Kaelynn will give you a level 90 ability in return for a Glowing Nebulous Dream Fragment. Be careful not to choose an ability you have already learned!

---

_On turning a fragment, you get your choice out of all your Level 90 spells or tomes. (You can also get your Dream Fragment back by choosing it as a reward.)_

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task begins with Researcher Breoro in the House of Thule's Lower Floors. He's located just inside the front door of the house._
You say, 'Hail, Researcher Breoro'
Researcher Breoro says 'Are you yet another [figment] of my imagination seemingly come to life?'
You say, 'I'm not a figment of your imagination...'
Researcher Breoro says 'No, I can tell that you are not from this accursed place. Thank the Gods. Please forgive me. I have been in this [living nightmare] for far too long. It has become difficult to tell that which is real from that which is not.'
You say, 'What living nightmare?'
Researcher Breoro says 'This place, whatever it is, has trapped me within its dark grasp. You see, I am a researcher of dreams and nightmares. I have long suffered from debilitating nightmares for as long as I can remember. Long ago, I decided to dedicate my life to uncovering their meaning. As my research progressed over several weeks, the [nightmares] I was experiencing seemed to become more and more lifelike.'
You say, 'Nightmares?'
Researcher Breoro says 'The nightmares always involve four horrible beasts. Night after night the nightmares grew in intensity until one night I woke up here, in this accursed place. Now I cannot [leave].'
You say, 'Why can't you leave?'
Researcher Breoro says 'There seems to be some force blocking my escape from this place. I have discovered that the beasts hold some sort of influence over my mind and have brought me here. They are minions of Terris Thule. Terris is up to something, hence this house. I feel her calling to me. What she wishes to gain from this, I do not know. The beasts appear to be her tools for whatever evil plan she is envisioning however. We must stop this. The beasts must be [destroyed].'
You say, 'Destroyed?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Before we can confront the beasts, several potions made from the essences of the creatures that exist here are needed. We will need a [potion of rot], a [potion of the unknown], a [potion of rage], and a [potion of horror].'
You say, 'What about the potion of horror?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Collect the ingredients I need for the Potion of Horror and then bring them back to me. There is no time to waste.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Beast of Horror'.
Researcher Breoro wants you to collect Bone Dust from the bone golems, Shrunken Heads from the bone crafters, Troublesome Snake Skins from snakes, and Essences of Horror from beastlings of horror. These creatures all inhabit the first floor of the House of Thule. Once you have them, deliver them to Researchrer Breoro.

---

Collect 2 Bone Dusts 0/2 (House of Thule)
Collect 2 Shrunken Heads 0/2 (House of Thule)
Collect 2 Troublesome Snake Skins 0/2 (House of Thule)
Collect 1 Essence of Horror 0/1 (House of Thule)
_Items are pre-lootable._
Deliver 2 Bone Dusts to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
Deliver 2 Shrunken Heads to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
Deliver 2 Troublesome Snake Skins to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
Deliver 1 Essence of Horror to Researcher Breoro 0/1 (House of Thule)
Researcher Breoro says 'You are doing well, my friend. The potion of horror must be mixed without error. Even the smallest mistake will ensure that I am lost to madness forever. Let's see. . . a pinch of bone dust is needed. Next the scales from the snake skins should be sloughed off into the mixture. Now, I must pour the mixture into the shrunken heads and add the essence of horror. Finally, the potion must ferment inside the shrunken heads for at least two hours. Make sure to speak with me again once all four of the potions have been brewed.'
_Rewards:_
158 Platinum
Experience (about 1.5 AAs worth or more if you have a low AA count)
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers has been adjusted by 10.
5 Dream Motes
_Heroic characters may receive an aug choice reward. Regular characters do not._
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

Researcher Breoro says 'You have brought the components for the potion of rage? This potion must be made with extreme care. The piles of rust will taste poorly, I am sure, but they are necessary. I shall add them first. Next, the pulsating ectoplasm should be added to the mix. The fragments of stone will help ground me in the physical realm after consuming the potion. Finally, the essence of rage can be added. This potion must sit for at least an hour before I can consume it. It is far too potent at the moment. Make sure to speak with me again once all four of the potions have been brewed.'

You say, 'Hail, Researcher Breoro'
Researcher Breoro says 'Are you yet another [figment] of my imagination seemingly come to life?'
You say, 'I'm not a figment of your imagination...'
Researcher Breoro says 'No, I can tell that you are not from this accursed place. Thank the Gods. Please forgive me. I have been in this [living nightmare] for far too long. It has become difficult to tell that which is real from that which is not.'

You say, 'Destroyed?'

You say, 'What about the potion of rot?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Collect the ingredients I need for the Potion of Rot and then bring them back to me. There is no time to waste.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Beast of Rot'.
Researcher Breoro wants you to collect Rotdog Ribs from rotdogs, Bone Fragments from skeletons, Snake Hearts from snakes, and Essences of Rot from beastlings of rot. These creatures all inhabit the first floor of the House of Thule. Once you have them, deliver them to Researchrer Breoro.

# The Beast of the Unknown

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with Researcher Breoro in the House of Thule's Lower Floors. He's located just inside the front door of the house.
You say, 'Hail, Researcher Breoro'
Researcher Breoro says 'Are you yet another [figment] of my imagination seemingly come to life?'
You say, 'I'm not a figment of your imagination...'
Researcher Breoro says 'No, I can tell that you are not from this accursed place. Thank the Gods. Please forgive me. I have been in this [living nightmare] for far too long. It has become difficult to tell that which is real from that which is not.'
You say, 'What living nightmare?'
Researcher Breoro says 'This place, whatever it is, has trapped me within its dark grasp. You see, I am a researcher of dreams and nightmares. I have long suffered from debilitating nightmares for as long as I can remember. Long ago, I decided to dedicate my life to uncovering their meaning. As my research progressed over several weeks, the [nightmares] I was experiencing seemed to become more and more lifelike.'
You say, 'Nightmares?'
Researcher Breoro says 'The nightmares always involve four horrible beasts. Night after night the nightmares grew in intensity until one night I woke up here, in this accursed place. Now I cannot [leave].'
You say, 'Why can't you leave?'
Researcher Breoro says 'There seems to be some force blocking my escape from this place. I have discovered that the beasts hold some sort of influence over my mind and have brought me here. They are minions of Terris Thule. Terris is up to something, hence this house. I feel her calling to me. What she wishes to gain from this, I do not know. The beasts appear to be her tools for whatever evil plan she is envisioning however. We must stop this. The beasts must be [destroyed].'
You say, 'Destroyed?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Before we can confront the beasts, several potions made from the essences of the creatures that exist here are needed. We will need a [potion of rot], a [potion of the unknown], a [potion of rage], and a [potion of horror].'
You say, 'What about the potion of the unknown?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Collect the ingredients I need for the Potion of the Unknown and then bring them back to me. There is no time to waste.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Beast of the Unknown'.
Researcher Breoro wants you to collect Cube Goos from sleeper cubes, Necromantic Trinkets from bone crafters, Poison-Coated Fangs from fright spinners, and Essences of the Unknown from beastlings of the unknown. These creatures all inhabit the first floor of the House of Thule. Once you have them, deliver them to Researchrer Breoro.
- Collect 2 Cube Goos 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Collect 2 Necromantic Trinkets 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Collect 2 Poison-Coated Fangs 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Collect 1 Essence of the Unknown 0/1 (House of Thule)
- Deliver 2 Cube Goos to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Deliver 2 Necromantic Trinkets to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Deliver 2 Poison-Coated Fangs to Researcher Breoro 0/2 (House of Thule)
- Deliver 1 Essence of the Unknown to Researcher Breoro 0/1 (House of Thule)
Researcher Breoro says 'Thank you, my friend. The cube goo is a powerful sleep-inducing component. I must take care not to add too much, or the potion may not wear off. Next, the poison-coated fangs must be carefuly handled. Each one must be dipped into the potion and then carefully discarded. The necromantic trinkets are next. The trinkets must be ground into a fine powder. If added in the right proportions, it should help protect me from the potion's poisonous effect. Finally, the essence of the unknown can be added. Make sure to speak with me again once all four of the potions have been brewed.'

# Kintorom #1: Interview with a Scholar

**Level:** 86 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 86
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the first in a series of three that begins with Scholar Kintorom at the zone-in of The Library.
You say, 'Hail, Scholar Kintorom'
Scholar Kintorom says 'Oh, hello there. Are you here to do some research? It's been my lifelong dream to find the answers to the origins of Norrath and the Gods we worship. I had researched everywhere in the world, but to no avail. Not too long ago though, I stumbled across this house and its wondrous [library].'
You say, 'What's up with this library?'
Scholar Kintorom says 'There any more books here than I have seen in all the libraries in my lifetime combined! Never in my wildest dreams did I ever expect to find a library such as this. The answers I seek must be within them, so I began to conduct my [research].'
You say, 'Your research?'
Scholar Kintorom says 'Not long after I started, the mysterious wardens here kicked me out. Why would they deny me access to these tomes? I must continue my research! While I cannot get back in to do my research, you may be able to go in my place. Would you be willing to go inside the library and [interview] the other scholars here regarding my research?'
You say, 'Of course I'd be willing to interview some other scholars'
Scholar Kintorom says 'Great! Please come back as soon as you have the results.'
You have been assigned the task 'Interview with a Scholar'.
Scholar Kintorom recently arrived in the library and began researching the origins of the godsand the creation of norrath. Shortly after though, he was kicked outfor unknown reasons. Because he cannot get back inside, he has asked you to enter the library and interview various scholars regarding his research.
Interview a preoccupied scholar 0/1 (The Library)
Interview an absorbed scholar 0/1 (The Library)
Interview an oblivious scholar 0/1 (The Library)
Interview an obsessed scholar 0/1 (The Library)
Interview a fascinated scholar 0/1 (The Library)
These mobs are found a few rooms deep into the zone. You can hail them with invisibility up. The first four scholars you hail will give you responses like this:
an oblivious scholar says 'I'm busy with a project of my own so I can't help you right now.'
an obsessed scholar says 'This is most fascinating.....hmm? Research? I don't have time for that. Go ask someone else.'
The fifth one you hail (doesn't matter which is last) will become aggravated enough with you to attack (and will also aggro nearby mobs).
a fascinated scholar says 'I'm trying to read. Why are you bothering me!'
Defeat the crazed scholar who attacked you 0/1 (The Library)
It hits for a max ~6,000.
Search the scholar for anything of interest 0/1 (The Library)
Loot a "Volume 791" from its corpse.
Bring 'Volume 791' to Scholar Kintorom 0/1 (The Library)
You were helping Scholar Kintorom with his research and delivered an interesting book to him.
Scholar Kintorom says 'This is a most interesting book you have brought me. I'll need some time study it further, but do come back soon. I suspect this may lead to something.'
159 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
Dream Motes (# varies)Rewards:
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

# Rupkerr #1: An Expedition Gone Awry

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the first in a series of three that begins with Rupkerr (click for map image) is found in the safe area in the north-central part of Feerrott, the Dream at approximate location 1175, 1340.
You say, 'Hail, Rupkerr'
Rupkerr says 'It's such a relief to come across a friendly traveler. I was sent here to determine the whereabouts of a missing [expedition crew], and found myself quite lost in this jungle, exposed to all its dangers. My own search has been halted, due to [rumors] of wandering undead.'
You say, 'A missing expedition crew?'
Rupkerr says 'A group of my fellow frogloks ventured out this way ages ago, with the intention of exploring the Feerrott and collecting [information] on how the jungle has changed since our time in Innothule.'
You say, 'What information?'
Rupkerr says 'We haven't had a report back since they first ventured this way; many fear the worst. I'm afraid I can't navigate this terrain well on my own. A helping hand won't go unrewarded. What do you say; will you [help] me find my missing kin?'
You say, 'I will help'
Rupkerr says 'According to my findings, the expedition crew was traveling northeast through the Feerrott. See if you can locate the crew, or recover any information on their whereabouts.'
You have been assigned the task 'An Expedition Gone Awry'.
Find the missing froglok expeditionary force 0/1 (The Feerrott)
In the far northeast part of the zone are the undead ruins. Note: This area is patrolled by numerous restless spirits, so you may need to do some clearing before you're able to proceed.
Search the froglok corpses for proof that you have found the missing expeditionary force 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Loot a Tattered Expedition Journal from a froglok corpse (has a five minute respawn timer). If you have difficulty targetting it, use "/target a_froglok" and then "/open" their corpse.
Defend yourself from the Lizardman Patroller 0/1 (The Feerrott)
A lizardman patroller spawns here, but any patroller in the zone will update this step.
Deliver the expedition journal to Rukperr 0/1 (The Feerrott)
On turn-in:
You have located the missing froglok expedition crew, only to find that they have perished for reasons unknown.
Rupkerr says 'This journal you brought me belonged to Krupp, the expedition leader. You say you found it on his corpse? It's a shame so many of the pages are missing. I'm sure they hold clues as to what caused the expedition crew to perish.'
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.
158 platinum
Experience (about 6% in Level 86)
Faction with Dream Delvers

- Dream Mote [item=90255]

You say, 'Hail, Rupkerr'
Rupkerr says 'This doesn't seem right. While my brethren's mission was of the utmost importance, it wasn't an expedition of force. Who or what could be responsible for their deaths? You say that you were [attacked] by a lizardman when you found the journal?'
You say, 'I was attacked!'
Rupkerr says 'It's certainly strange that the lizardmen were lingering so close to the froglok corpses. If you could recover the [pages], maybe I could make sense of their hasty scribbling.'
You say, 'I'll recover those pages'
Rupkerr says 'If you find the lizardmen, I am sure you will also find the pages. They have multiple camps scattered around the Feerrott. Good luck to you.'
You have been assigned the task 'Uncovering the Truth'.
Find the Missing Journal Pages 0/4 (The Feerrott)
_Loot four Missing Journal Pages (dropped randomly by "a lizardman \_\_\_\_\_" mobs in the zone). Note: These are not pre-lootable._
Reassemble the Journal 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Deliver 1 restored expedition journal to Repkerr 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Rupkerr thumbs through the journal pages with unnerving trepidation. Rupkerr says 'Quite disturbing, indeed. It seems the expedition force stumbled upon an ancient lizardman clan, whose beliefs delve into dark ritual and sacrifice. The bloodstained scrawling is hard to make out, but I believe my brethren have been cursed. By what, I'm afraid that knowledge escapes me. However, if these journals are correct, I'd wager a Tae Ew ritualist would have a grimoire that could identify the curse.'
Recover the ritualist's grimoire 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_You'll find some Tae Ew ritualists near the Cazic Thule ramp area. Kill them until you loot a Tae Ew Ritualist Grimoire. NOTE: This is not pre-lootable._
Return the grimoire to Repkerr 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Thanks to your efforts, Rupkerr now has the ritualist's grimoire in his possession, and can hopefully find a way to remove the froglok's curse.
Rupkerr says 'I'm amazed you that got your hands on this book! Rarely do we have the chance to get such an intimate look at the Tae Ew. I'm sure I'll find what we need in here to lift the curse.'

315 platinum

# Illian #1: Protecting the Weak (Minded)

**Level:** 80 | **Maximum Level:** 130 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 80
**Maximum Level:** 130
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the first of two tasks offered by Dark Alchemist Illian [npc=35881] at approximate location 1200, 145 in the Feerrott. He's on Find (Ctrl+F).
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian appears engrossed in his work and takes a few moments of measuring a concoction before he speaks. 'I'd like to think you're here because you have a true [interest] in what I'm doing, but I highly doubt that.' The Teir`Dal turns to look at the _____ who addressed him, and noting the battle-worn and magic-imbued armament, frowns slightly. 'I see.'
You say, 'I do have a true interest...'
Dark Alchemist Illian hmphs. 'I don't believe you.'  He gently sets the materials down before intently staring back at his inquirer.  'I am Illian and I was, for lack of a better word, "hired" by the local Oggok leadership to try to resolve the [issue] of the [undead rising], bloated, from their watery graves... for vengeance.'  Mockingly, his hands come up like a spread of stiff boogeyman claws, mouthing 'Ooooh', then punctuates the point with a roll of his eyes.
You say, 'What about the undead rising?'
Dark Alchemist Illian gives an extra moment's regard to the inquiry before replying. 'Perhaps you have an interest after all.  The undead zombies who roam the swampy forest of the Feerrott are not natural undead.  In fact, it's quite difficult to find natural undead, but that is beside the point.  No, these men and women were quite [unlucky].'
You say, 'Unlucky?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Well, they certainly weren't "lucky" to get in that state, were they?' He smirks, and looks down to one of his containers, tapping at a vial filled with an orange powder.  'They were a human expedition, a small smattering of tree-elf half-elves thrown in.  A twist of fate led them a bit too far [south] for their own good.''
You say, 'What's wrong with the south?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'South? Well, besides the Temple of Cazic-Thule and the eerie presence at the source of the river? Based on where the explorers are now making themselves at home, it's hypothesized the source of their new existence is a rather interesting tribe of scaly Thulians, the Alliz Tae Ew.  Oh, but not purposefully.  No, that point is quite an [accident].'
You say, 'An accident?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'If it is as we believe, they weren't quite as delicious as the little cannibals had hoped, or just that the explorers were used as simply a ritual gift in reverence to some queen of reptiles or the God of Fear himself.  Either way, in the end, they were dumped in the river rather unceremoniously and left there to literally [rot] by a group known for their shamanism, but not terribly known for their necromancy.'
You say, 'Rot?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Well, they did rot, and are rotting, but those unfortunate humans didn't return to the earth.  No, their dead and decaying flesh seems to be frozen in time now.  Perhaps due to the rituals performed on them, or perhaps due to whatever lies at the end of the river.  Either way, the creatures rise, they're killed, and they rise again hours later.  That leaves us to our present [issue].'
You say, 'What issue?'
Dark Alchemist Illian furrows his brow. 'To be honest, I didn't want to involve outsiders, particularly since I'd have to award you with part of my pay regardless of the outcome, but I suppose I must.  You see, the little wee Ogres are a tad too scared to go outside the city at the moment.  Things are significantly more dangerous out here than in the safe confines of the city... if you can call the stinkpit Oggok that.  And it is in large part due to the undead [threat].'
You say, 'What threat?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Simply put, Ogres are immune to what plagues those undead, but they are not immune to death. The undead are a threat because Ogres aren't smart.  If they were smart, I wouldn't have to be hired to find a [cure] for the zombification.'
You say, 'A cure?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'You're getting ahead of yourself.  That's a bad habit.'  He hmphs again.  'If you want to assist in this situation, I'm going to need your [word].  Not only that you'll do it, but that you'll survive, because I can't afford to pay your family benefits in the case that you're dragged into the river by a giant serpent or chopped to bits by the Tae Ew as the latest banquet meal.'
You say, 'You have my word'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Good, good.' He clears his throat and points directly southward.  'To the river fork that lines the borders of the Alliz Tae Ew's stomping grounds, I need you to survey the area, kill some of the walking undead explorers that litter the waterways, and return to me with some of their bile.'  His gaze turns to whatever expression that gives. 'And no complaints if you want to be paid.  I've work to do and cannot be bothered by whining.  Get to it.'
You have been assigned the task 'Protecting the Weak (Minded)'.
A plague of undeath has been running across the Feerrott, keeping the young Ogres from leaving Oggok to save their lives. While ogres have not been particularly prone to undeath, they are not immune to death at the hand of the walking corpses. Dark Alchemist Illian has been hired by the Ogres of Oggok to investigate the undead and put a permanent stop to them, and has requested your assistance in the matter.
Illian would like for you to investigate the fork in the river where the northern tip of the Tae Ew settlement begins. Slay the zombified fallen travelers and drowned explorers in the area and loot their bile to return to Illian for processing.
1. Explore the fork of the river 0/1 (The Feerrott)
This updates around /way -350,+720.
2. Kill a few zombies and search them for their bile 0/5 (The Feerrott)
The undead you need to kill are found in the Greenblood River that stretches through the zone.
3. Collect a few zombie bile samples 0/3 (The Feerrott)
4. Deliver 3 zombie bile samples to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/3 (The Feerrott)
5. Speak with Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'For a while, I was afraid I'd lost you, or you found something new and shiny to take your interest.' The dark elf peers at the samples supplied, looking over their consistency. 'Thank you. This will do nicely. It will take some time to prepare it.' He pauses a beat and continues, 'You know, I had a [thought] while you were out.'
You say, 'What kind of thought?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'You know, a thought. When something comes to mind, a spark of inspiration, knowledge or facts that come to the forefront.' After a round of sarcastic mockery, he looks you over again and mumbles a quick, 'Nevermind.' Clearing his throat, he continues, 'Beyond the river in the northwest, there are bands of gorillas... families if you will. Some of the adults look rather sickly and while I have not looked into it specifically, there may be a connection between those blighted mature gorillas and the zombification of the humans in the river. Are you up for more [dirty work]?'
You say, 'Dirty work?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Same deal as with the explorers before. Find the blighted ones, put them out of their misery, then bring back their blood.'
6. Explore a gorilla camp far to the north west 0/1 (The Feerrott)
7. Kill a few blighted gorillas and search them for their blood 0/5 (The Feerrott)
8. Collect a few blighted blood samples 0/3 (The Feerrott)
9. Deliver 3 blighted blood samples to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/3 (The Feerrott)
10. Speak with Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian examines the blighted blood samples provided and gives a nod. 'This will certainly do,' he replies, turning back to his workspace. He immediately disregards anyone else around him as he mutters to himself, keeping his focus on his alchemy. After a long several minutes, he produces a small amount of supplies. 'I suppose you're ready for the [next step] in your job?'
You say, 'What next step?'
Dark Alchemist Illian passes the supplies onto you, and points to each in turn. 'First, while you were out the second time, I condensed the reactives in the bile you returned. It is now essentially "concentrated zombie". Don't drink it, don't sniff it, and don't rub your face in it. Not unless you want to be the next test subject, anyway. Next,' and he motions to the dark mass in another vial, 'that is the blood you returned, refined. Same deal; it's not food.' To a larger flask, he comments, 'This is a flask of my special agent. I don't care if you are an alchemist, or know an alchemist, but the elements in that flask are quite confidential. It's also an irritant, so don't get it in your eyes.' A final gesture goes to an empty [canister] with a nozzle attached, but doesn't say anything immediately.
11. Combine the elements received in the canister 0/1 (The Feerrott)
12. Test the cure on an unsuspecting zombie 0/1 (The Feerrott)
13. Kill 1 an empowered fallen explorer 0/1 (The Feerrott)
14. Loot 1 empowered zombie bile sample from an empowered fallen explorer 0/1 (The Feerrott)
15. Deliver 1 sealed alchemic canister to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
16. Deliver 1 empowered zombie bile to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Dark Alchemist Illian says, 'Well, this is unfortunate. I suppose you did what you could, though. I'm going to need some time to look over... these new developments. Here is your pay for your assistance so far, and know that I can use your help later.'
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers

# Illian #2: There's Something in the Water

**Level:** 80 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 80
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the second of two tasks offered by Dark Alchemist Illian [npc=35881] at approximate location 1200, 145 in the Feerrott.
You must have completed his first task, Protecting the Weak (Minded) [quest=5128] in order to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Ah, welcome back. I've looked over the data you've given me, including the final bile sample, and it's quite puzzling indeed. There's certainly [more to investigate] with this phenomenon. Unless, of course, I have your [word] that any attempts on the original solution would go better than the last.'
You say, 'More to investigate?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Before we examine the river more carefully, I'd like for you to investigate the portal to the realm of Fear. Do not wander inside, no, I rather cannot take the time to try to collect your remains out of there should you perish to the denizens within. No, instead, I want you to [collect samples] from the beings just outside, namely the blood of the bloodbone skeletons.'
You say, 'I will collect samples.'
You have been assigned the task 'There's Something In the Water'.
Dark Alchemist Illian turns back to his work, replying with a simple, 'Good. Go now and I'll prepare for the samples.'
- 1. Loot 3 Bloodbone Skeleton Blood Samples from the bloodbone skeletons 0/3 (The Feerrott)
Bloodbone skeletons [npc=35854] are found in the southwestern part of the Feerrott. Loot 3 Bloodbone Skeleton Blood Sample [item=91156].
Note: The skeletons are rootable/snareable and do not summon.
- 2. Deliver 3 Bloodbone Skeleton Blood Samples to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/3 (The Feerrott)
- 3. Speak with Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian dips a finger in the blood you've returned and tastes it, shaking his head slightly. 'It's not the same. Well, that was quite useless... or not. At least we verified what the source is not.' Frowning thoughtfully, he continues, 'Let's move forward, let's look back at the [river].'
You say, 'What about the river?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'There are two places that I'd like for you to take samples from. Up north, around where you had examined the blighted gorillas, I'd like for you to take a sample of the waterfall. The source itself may be what's coming from around that blasted city of Oggok, and the waterfall would be a good sample of that. As for the [second]...'
- 4. Inquire further about Illian's needs 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'What second thing?'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'The second location would be the southern end of the river, to where it disappears beyond the Temple. It's no secret that something is down that direction, but it's unclear just what it may be. I'd much rather you return alive with your sample than dead and I lose the sample, so please gather the second sample near the Temple of Cazic-Thule itself rather than beyond it. Go. Do not pass it, or you don't collect your pay.'
- 5. Gather sample Azia from the waters near the waterfall 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Right-click Water Collection Vessel Azia [item=92476] to get the update.
- 6. Gather sample Beza from the waters at the southern end of the river 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Upon gathering a sample by right-clicking Water Collection Vessel Beza [item=92477] at the southwestern fork of the river, an awakened rotdog [npc=36058] spawns and attacks.
- 7. Defend the sample received 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Kill awakened rotdog. It hits for a max ~5,000; is rootable/snareable; has ~500k hp; and summons.
NOTE: If you die to this mob, or if someone else kills it, there is no known way to respawn it.
- 8. Collect the head of the rotdog as proof 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Loot the Rotdog Head [item=92534].
- 9. Deliver 1 Rotdog Head to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
- 10. Deliver 1 Filled Water Collection Vessel Azia to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
- 11. Deliver 1 Filled Water Collection Vessel Beza to Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Give the Filled Water Collection Vessel Azia [item=92491] and Filled Water Collection Vessel Beza [item=92494] to Dark Alchemist Illian.
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian says 'Dark Alchemist Illian quickly puts down the water samples to look over the head that was returned with more detail. 'This... This is interesting. I'll have to investigate this further. And a strange gate, you say? There's one last thing I ask for you, and that's to examine the gate, then return to me with what you find.''
- 12. Explore the gate discovered 0/1 (The Feerrott)
Zone into the House of Thule via the gate at the south-central zone boundary of the Feerrott.
- 13. Speak with Dark Alchemist Illian 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'Hail, Dark Alchemist Illian'
Dark Alchemist Illian nods slowly. 'I won't ask you to go down there to investigate the area, and you've done what has been asked of you fairly adaquately. If you wish to examine that area further, that is up to you, but at this point it's beyond the scope of my own work.'
Experience (7% regular at level 86)
315 platinum
10 Dream Motes [item=90255]
Faction with Dream DelversSubmitted by: Tarvas of Tribal FuryRewards:
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

Modified: Sat Feb 18 07:48:00 2012 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Ashrin T`Vahn in the Feerrott (location 1020, 845)._
You say, 'Hail, Ashrin T`Vahn'
Ashrin T`Vahn growls as he pulls you into the hut. 'For Thex, you'd think you'd be a little more [incognito] if you're going to just out my name like that, and so loudly. Who in Norrath are you, anyways, to just waltz to my hut and greet me so openly? In better parts of Norrath, I'd have taken you down on the spot.'
You say, 'Incognito?'
Ashrin T`Vahn says 'Are you mad? Do you have to ask?' He sighs softly and rubs at a temple. 'Adventurers like you give me such a headache. Well, since you're no doubt at a loss for things to do to such an extent that you're bothering a random stranger about it, I suppose I'll tell you a little of [what is going on].'
You say, 'What is going on?'
Ashrin T`Vahn says 'Impatient, are we? You know I'm only doing this to get you out of my hair, right? Well, there's the group of lizardmen to the south, the Tae Ew, that are spreading some very nasty rumors as to the politics of Oggok and the Feerrott right now. I've been sent here to [investigate]. . . before you so rudely broke my cover.'
You say, 'Investigate what?'
Ashrin T`Vahn says 'At this point, it's useless to investigate further. The lizardmen should be brought down before they cause an uproar in Oggok itself, and in turn, Neriak. Take down the Tae Ew lizards that roam this swampy forest. Dead men tell no tales. I hope you're [up to it], for all the trouble you've put me through.'
You say, 'I'm up to it'
Ashrin T`Vahn says 'Good. Now get out there. And \_\_\_\_\_? I don't want to hear you back here until the deed is done. Have I made myself clear?'
You have been assigned the task 'Appeasing a Teir'Dal'.
Ashrin T`Vahn is afraid the Tae Ew lizardmen of the Feerrott are getting some wrong ideas about the goings on in the Feerrott. Before they spread their rumors to Oggok, he requests their extermination before his own cover is blown.

---

Silence the Tae Ew by killing several of them 0/16 (The Feerrott)
_The Tae Ew are found throughout the area outside the Temple of Cazic Thule (south-central part of the zone)._
_Note: "a frenzied Tae Ew" does not update this task._
Inform Ashrin that the deed is done 0/1 (The Feerrott)
You say, 'Hail, Ashrin T'Vahn'
Ashrin T'Vahn says, 'Well done, I suppose. I'm glad you acknowledged the threat they could have become. Such pesky troublemakers. I suppose you now want your reward, adventurer, and so you shall have it.' The dark elf sneers as he passes it over.

You say, 'Hail, Researcher Breoro'

Or, just in case, you can retrieve more ingredients for me, so I can brew another [potion of rot], [potion of rage], [potion of horror], or [potion of the unknown].'
You say, 'What task?'
Researcher Breoro says 'Once I drink these potions, you will be able to enter my nightmare and destroy the beasts. Each beast has three protectors that will need to be defeated before the beasts themselves can be confronted. When you are [ready], I will drink the potions.'

You have been assigned the task 'Welcome to My Nightmare'.
Defeat the necromancers guarding the Beasts of Rot, Horror, Rage, and the Unknown. Once you have accomplished this, speak with Researcher Breoro.
You say, 'Let's go'
Researcher Breoro says 'May the Gods be with you.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered House of Thule: Breoro's Nightmare.

---

_The first task steps open are all the "Destroy Keeper..." steps. These Keepers are found in groups of three in the same locations where you'd find the "beastling" mobs in the non-instanced zone._
_Killing each group of Keepers spawns the Beast associated with them. The Beasts spawn right on top of the corpse of the last Keeper to die in each set._
_Keepers hit for a max ~6,000 and are stunnable, rootable/snareable, mezzable, and able to be harmonized._
_The Beasts hit for a max ~8,500, enrage, have more hitpoints, and are immune to crowd control methods. They also each come with an AE debuff (listed below). These debuffs are triggered by using same-resist spells against them (The Beast of Rot's debuff, for example, would be triggered by any disease-based spell used against it)._
_The Researcher at the end hits for a max ~8,000 and also has a lot of hitpoints._

This task is the second in a series of three that begins with Scholar Kintorom at the zone-in of The Library.
You must have completed his prior task, "Interview with a Scholar" before being able to get this one.
You say, 'Hail, Scholar Kintorom'
Scholar Kintorom says 'The book you brought me is amazing! It describes ancient events that I have never seen recorded previously. Unfortunately, the book only covers a small time period. I would theorize this book is part of a larger [set].'
You say, 'A set?'
Scholar Kintorom says 'There must be more of these books in the library. The binding appears to be unique so they should be easy to find. Would you be willing to venture inside once again and [locate] more books from this set?'
You say, 'I'll locate them'
Scholar Kintorom says 'Excellent! I eagerly await your return.'
You have been assigned the task 'History of the World, Part 2'.
Scholar Kintorom is fascinated by the book you brought him and theorizes it to be part of a larger set. He has asked you to enter the library any books which match the appearance of 'Volume 791'.

The enraged historian isn't social with other mobs, so you can be in a safe corner or hall and only deal with the one mob. It hits for a max ~6,000 and drops "Volume 588".

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This group task (3-6 players) begins with Scholar Kintorom at the zone-in of The Library._
_You must have completed the Feerrott and House of Thule group progression missions ("Fall of the Tae Ew" and "Welcome to My Nightmare"), his two solo tasks, and his shared task before being able to request this one._
You say, 'Hail, Scholar Kintorom'
Scholar Kintorom says 'Now that we know the answers are in the Encyclopedia Norrathica, we must travel to the top of the library to [confront] the Head Librarian on its whereabouts. Otherwise you could [interview] scholars about my research, [locate] more books, or help me fulfill my [dream].'
You say, 'Confront?'
Scholar Kintorom says 'I have no doubt they will try to stop us but I'm no good in a fight. I'll need you and your friends to clear the way to the head librarian. Be prepared for anything. Shall we [proceed]?
You have been assigned the task 'The Stuff of Dreams'.
Scholar Kintorom has discovered the existence of the Encyclopedia Norrathica, a book which contains all the information regarding the creation of Norrath and the Gods. He wants to confront the head librarian and claim the book for himself, so he can fulfill his dreams, but needs your help to clear the way to the top of the library.
_Say "proceed" to zone in._

---

**General Information**
_In this instance, you can expect trash mob types to hit for a max ~6,200. Nameds hit for a little more (but no flurry, no rampage, and no enrage). They may not even summon (this is not confirmed). Some nameds have an AE associated with them (if so, they are listed below)._
_Trash mobs are rootable/snareable, stunnable, and mezzable (even with 81-85 Enchanter mezzes)._
_The "Make your way..." steps (which are update locations found gradually further into the library) open up one at a time as you kill the mobs associated with them._
_After triggering one of these steps, the trash mobs begin spawning one at a time. The named is permanently rooted, so its attacks are avoidable until you want to engage it. (There is an achievement for killing the named before the trash.)_
_NOTE: Make sure to engage the named within a minute of killing the last add spawn. If you let too much time pass without doing so, another wave of adds spawns._

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
_You'll see an erudite named Curator Majda. As you approach her, this step updates and the first trash mob spawns. (The rest of the task plays out the same way.)_
Kill 3 a furious sweeper 0/3 (The Library)
Kill 1 Curator Majda 0/1 (The Library)
_These mobs appear to be pure melee._
Curator Majda says 'Sweeper, get them!'
Curator Majda has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Curator Majda's corpse gasps for air, choking out one final breath.

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
Kill 3 a fallen warrior 0/3 (The Library)
_Fallen warriors have spell procs:_
Touch of Flame: Single Target, Fire (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 300
Touch of Frost: Single Target, Cold (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 300
Touch of Rot: Single Target, Disease (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 300
Kill 1 Tome of the Fallen 0/1 (The Library)
_Tome of the Fallen summons and casts:_
Breath of the Fallen: Single Target, (-100)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 8000 per tick
2: Set Healing Effectiveness to -20%
3: Increase corruption counter by 50
Tome of the Fallen surges with increased velocity, preparing for a fight!
Tome of the Fallen has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Tome of the Fallen's corpse falls into a heap of dust and torn pages.

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
Kill 3 an armored watcher 0/3 (The Library)
Kill 1 Ardull the Watcher 0/1 (The Library)
Ardull the Watcher says 'Watcher, I call you to defend the library!'
Ardull the Watcher raises its weapons and rushes into battle!
_Watchers appear to be pure melee, while Ardull the Watcher casts "Focused Rage:"_
Focused Rage PB AE 40', Corruption (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 22000
2: SpinStun
3: Add Effect: Focused Rage
Ardull the Watcher has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Ardull the Watcher's corpse crashes to the ground with unnatural force, shaking the surrounding library walls.

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
Kill 3 a nightmare 0/3 (The Library)
_Note: The nightmares proc on their targets "Shadow Touch":_
Shadow Touch: Single Target, Magic (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1500 per tick _(3 ticks)_
Kill 1 Compendium of Nightmares 0/1 (The Library)
Compendium of Nightmares summons forth a nightmare from its pages.
_It casts "Waking Nightmares":_
Waking Nightmares: Single Target, Magic (0)
1: Increase Spell Haste by 10%
2: Inhibit Melee Attacks by (15%)
3: Ultravision
Compendium of Nightmares has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Compendium of Nightmares's corpse falls into a heap of dust and torn pages.

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
Kill 15 a musty tome 0/15 (The Library)
_Note: The musty tomes come in waves of 3-5 at a time. They hit for a max ~1,300 each and are crowd-controllable._
Kill 1 Archivist Herrdar 0/1 (The Library)
_He's found up the stairs on the next level. He casts "Dust of the Ages:"_
Dust of the Ages: Targeted AE 50', Chromatic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 8000 per tick
2: Unknown #408 (100/80/0)
Archivist Herrdar has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Archivist Herrdar's corpse cries out in pain, before falling eternally still.

---

Make your way toward the top of the library 0/1 (The Library)
_The Head Librarian attacks once you trigger this update._
Kill 1 Head Librarian Matilda 0/1 (The Library)
Head Librarian Matilda raises her voice in warning, 'Those who make noise will be silenced by my hand!'
_Matilda hits for a max ~7,000 and AEs "Forceful Knowledge":_
Forceful Knowledge: PB AE 50', Magic (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 22000
2: Decrease Mana by 3000
3: Decrease Endurance by 2700
_She also spawns time-based adds one at a time. They're called "a dutiful librarian" and will keep spawning until six of them are up. If you kill one, another will take its place. You'll want to kill the Head Librarian while offtanking or mezzing or rooting the adds._
Head Librarian Matilda has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Head Librarian Matilda falls over in defeat.

---

Allow Scholar Kintorom to claim the Encyclopedia Norrathica 0/1 (The Library)
_After the Head Librarian and her adds are dead, approach the Encyclopedia Norrathica. Scholar Kintorom appears and has this exchange..._
Scholar Kintorom says 'Finally I've found it! All the answers I seek will surely be in the Encyclopedia Norrathica. You are mine now! Wait, what's happening? This isn't right! I was so close to fulfilling my dreams! Noooooo.....'
_The Head Librarian then respawns (non-KOS):_
Head Librarian Matilda says 'Because the knowledge contained in this book is forbidden, Kintorom was banished from this place. He will never be permitted to return. Some things are better left in your dreams, adventurers. Take these things and ruminate on what you have witnessed this day. Inform me when you are ready to [leave].'
You helped Scholar Kintorom reach the Encyclopedia Norrathica, though things didn't turn out as expected.
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (The Library)

**Level:** 85

**Group Size:** Solo

**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is one of five solo kill tasks that are offered by a froglok named Ruppoc Rockjumper at the zone-in of the House of Thule (lower floors).
You say, 'Hail, Ruppoc Rockjumper'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'Hey, are you looking for mushrooms and moss to eat too? You're not? Well let me tell you about something I [found] anyway.'
You say, 'Tell me about that something you found...'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'I was walking through the jungle the other day, looking for some delectable moss and mushrooms for dinner, when I came across this gate you passed through. It just appeared one day. I thought that was strange, so I came inside to check it out. It is a treasure trove of delicious but strange-looking moss! There is one [problem] though.'
You say, 'What problem?'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'This place is crawling with nightmarish creatures. I managed to sneak around a bit, gathering a few samples of the delicious moss that grows in here, but the monsters eventually spotted me, and I had to flee back to the gate. I really want more of that moss. Think you could [help] me out?'
You say, 'I'll help you out'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'I can't get any more of that moss with all these critters in here. Do you think you could [thin] their numbers out a bit?'
You say, 'I could thin their numbers'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'You are too kind! What would you like to kill? [rot dogs] and [snakes], [skeletons] and [bone golems], [terror guards], [terror spinners] and [sleeper cubes], or [bone crafters] and [quaking haunts]?'
You say, 'Rot dogs and snakes'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'Rotdogs and snakes? Most of the rotdogs I found in here patrol the front of the mansion. The snakes can be found both inside and outside of the house. Good luck!'
You have been assigned the task 'Thinning Out Their Numbers: Rotdogs and Snakes'.

Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'You cleared out some of the rotdogs and snakes? Thank you. Perhaps now I can go gather some more of that wonderful moss!'

# Thinning Out Their Numbers: Bone Crafters and Quaking Haunts

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is one of five solo kill tasks that are offered by a froglok named Ruppoc Rockjumper at the zone-in of the House of Thule (lower floors).
You say, 'Hail, Ruppoc Rockjumper'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'Hey, are you looking for mushrooms and moss to eat too? You're not? Well let me tell you about something I [found] anyway.'
You say, 'Tell me about that something you found...'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'I was walking through the jungle the other day, looking for some delectable moss and mushrooms for dinner, when I came across this gate you passed through. It just appeared one day. I thought that was strange, so I came inside to check it out. It is a treasure trove of delicious but strange-looking moss! There is one [problem] though.'
You say, 'What problem?'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'This place is crawling with nightmarish creatures. I managed to sneak around a bit, gathering a few samples of the delicious moss that grows in here, but the monsters eventually spotted me, and I had to flee back to the gate. I really want more of that moss. Think you could [help] me out?'
You say, 'I'll help you out'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'I can't get any more of that moss with all these critters in here. Do you think you could [thin] their numbers out a bit?'
You say, 'I could thin their numbers'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'You are too kind! What would you like to kill? [rot dogs] and [snakes], [skeletons] and [bone golems], [terror guards], [terror spinners] and [sleeper cubes], or [bone crafters] and [quaking haunts]?'
You say, 'Bone crafters and quaking haunts'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'I found a particularly good spot to get some of the moss in the house, but a bone crafter spotted me. She wanted to kill me and raise me as an undead creature! Oh, and the quaking haunts, they are just as scary. Both can be found inside of the house.'
You have been assigned the task 'Thinning Out Their Numbers: Bone Crafters and Quaking Haunts'.
Kill 4 bone crafters 0/4 (House of Thule)
Kill 4 haunts 0/4 (House of Thule)
Report to Ruppoc Rockjumper 0/1 (House of Thule)
You say, 'Hail, Ruppoc Rockjumper'
Ruppoc Rockjumper says 'You managed to defeat some of the bone crafters and quaking haunts? You sure are braver than I am! Thanks!'
Experience (about 1 AA worth)

Modified: Fri Sep 13 06:38:21 2024 | | This is a non-progression group task that begins with Rupkerr ( **click for map image**) is found in the safe area in the north-central part of Feerrott, the Dream at approximate location 1175, 1340.
There are no pre-requisites (other than level) for requesting this task.
You say, 'Hail, Rupkerr'
Rupkerr says 'It's such a relief to come across a friendly traveler. I was sent here to determine the whereabouts of a missing [expedition crew], and found myself quite lost in this jungle, exposed to all its dangers. My own search has been halted, due to [rumors] of wandering undead.'
You say, 'What rumors?'
Rupkerr says 'Oh, the rumors have been coming in from all over, as far north as the waterfall, to the southern border of Innothule Swamp. The Feerrott has its fair share of undead, but these recent [sightings] have been far more fearful.'
You say, 'Sightings?'
Rupkerr says 'Normally the undead tend to linger not far from the portal to the Plane of Fear, leaving the rest of the jungle relatively free of skeletons and specters. The recent reports I've heard detail a myriad of fearful [creatures], unlike anything that's ever been seen before in the Feerrott.'
You say, 'What creatures?'
Rupkerr says 'Indescribable creatures, looking as if they crawled straight through the Fear portal itself! Of course, there simply cannot be any truth to these rumors, as it is common knowledge the portal to Fear is a one-way trip. Nonetheless, someone should [investigate] these sightings.'
You say, 'I'll investigate for you'
Rupkerr says 'Very well, \_\_\_\_\_. You're far braver than the adventures that passed this way before you. I'll take extra care to scribe the sighting locations in your journal exactly as they were reported to me. Let me know when you are ready to [begin] your investigation.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Tearable Fear'.
You say, 'I am ready to begin.'
You are teleported into an instanced version of Feerrott, the Dream.

---

1\. Investigate the rumors of undead near Innothule Swamp 0/1 (The Feerrott)
2\. Vanquish the undead from the area 0/4 (The Feerrott)
3\. Investigate the rumors of undead near the Temple of Cazic Thule Swamp 0/1 (The Feerrott)
4\. Vanquish the undead from the area 0/4 (The Feerrott)
5\. Investigate the rumors of undead west of the waterfall 0/1 (The Feerrott)
6\. Vanquish the undead from the area 0/4 (The Feerrott)
7\. Speak to Rupkerr 0/1 (The Feerrott)
He is near the Rathe Mountains zone line.
8\. Speak to Cyndreela 0/1 (The Feerrott)
9\. Collect fear essences from creatures in the area 0/8 (The Feerrott)
_The item you're looking for is "an essence of fear"._
10\. Give the fear essences to Cyndreela 0/8 (The Feerrott)
11\. Speak to Rupkerr 0/1 (The Feerrott)
He is near the Rathe Mountains zone line.
12\. Defeat the portal guardians near Innothule 0/3 (The Feerrott)
_The Innothule portal is by the zoneline at /waypoint -977, -2670, 3_
_When you run past one of the portals, you instantly aggro the first portal guardian. The aggro range is pretty far, so be ready for a fight._
_The second and third guardian spawns are on a timer. They're each mezzable and snareable._
13\. Destroy the Portal 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_Blackbones spawns and rushes you after you kill the last guardian. He casts a large knockback._
14\. Defeat the portal guardians near the Temple of Cazic Thule 0/3 (The Feerrott)
_Temple of CT portal base of the ramp /waypoint -1003, 52, -9_
15\. Destroy the Portal 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_Terror Unleashed spawns and attacks after you kill the last guardian here._
16\. Defeat the portal guardians west of the waterfall 0/3 (The Feerrott)
_Waterfall portal is against the Zone wall nw corner, but near the waterfall._
17\. Destroy the Portal 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_The Fearful Spectre spawns and attacks after you kill the last of this portal's guardians. It has two AEs:_
Dread Pulse: PB AE 100', Magic (-650)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 23000
2: Decrease Movement by 50%
3: Stun (2.00 sec)
Touch of Fear: Single Target, Magic (-650)
1: Fear up to level 0
18\. Defeat Cyndreela 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_Cyndreela doesn't see invis and won't aggro until you pull her. She is undead. She has an AE Soul Devour (hits everyone in range for 5,000 and heals herself for that amount)._
Soul Devour: PB AE 40', Unresistable (0)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 5000
The task locks.
19\. Claim your reward from ornate chest 0/1 (The Feerrott)

---

Experience (about 3% regular exp at Level 86 or 7 AAs worth)

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the first of three tasks in a series that begins with Dtaemi in The Well. He's found at location -515, -780, 16 (in the southern part of the easternmost cavern)._
You say, 'Hail, Dtaemi'
Dtaemi says 'Hello? Please help me. I've been down here a long time. My [mother] must be worried something fierce.'
You say, 'Your mother?'
Dtaemi says 'We came to the garden and I ran off. I knew I wasn't supposed to, but she wouldn't let me do anything. So I snuck off and found the [well].'
You say, 'The well?'
Dtaemi says 'I just wanted to get a closer look. But when I leaned over the edge I fell. I've been here ever since. At first I heard voices from above, but when I yelled back they couldn't hear me. Now I am [stuck] down here.'
You say, 'How did you get stuck?'
Dtaemi says 'I died trying to find a way out. I cannot go on knowing my mother is still looking for me. She needs to know what happened. If you can [find] her, I can finally be at rest.'
You say, 'I'll find her'
Dtaemi says 'Thank you. I don't know where she is now, but we had talked to one scientist type guy who was here looking at the plants. Maybe he knows where she is.'
You have been assigned the task 'Down the Hole'.
Dtaemi needs your help to track down his mother, who has not seen him since he was lost long ago.

---

Speak to Giladan the Botanist 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Giladan the Botanist is found at location -65, -1700, -105 (due east of the zone-in; just east of the central garden and next to Spensur, the Rk. III spell NPC)._
You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'I've got too much going on right now to answer questions. If someone can deal with those rotdogs, I am sure I would have more time for inquiries.'
Kill 5 rotdogs 0/5 (The Grounds)
_Kill any five rotdogs to update this step. Note: Only "a rotdog" will update this task._
Speak to Giladan the Botanist 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'A young boy? I do remember a while back some mother looking for her son. He'd apparently gotten lost but they could not find him. Quite a few searchers fell to the creature here in the garden looking for him. So they eventually had to call off the search. She finally left and went back to speak to Shalma in Feerrott about supplies. But I never saw her again.'
Speak to Shalma in the Feerrott 0/1 (The Feerrott)
_Shalma is found in the Feerrott just north of the zone-in from the House of Thule. She's on the east side of the riverbank. There are undead mobs in the water, so keep a lookout._
You say, 'Hail, Shalma'
Shalma says 'Yes, I remember that woman. She had no means to pay for anything. But besides that, no one was willing to go look for her son since there were too many who had not returned from the last time they searched. I'd look for any signs that she'd been killed here in Feerrott.'
Kill 10 Tae Ew 0/10 (The Feerrott)
_Any "a Tae Ew \_\_\_\_\_" will do._
Speak to Dtaemi 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Dtaemi'
You have found out that Dtaemi's mother most likely died trying to search for him.
Dtaemi says 'But if she has moved on, what is keeping me here? Thank you, but I may need your help again soon.'

Modified: Fri Nov 19 00:07:53 2010 | | _This is the third of three tasks in a series that begins with Dtaemi in The Well. He's found at location -515, -780, 16 (in the southern part of the easternmost cavern)._

---

Kill 3 bone golems 0/3 (The Grounds)
Speak to Ikaena the Bonesmith 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Bone golems are found throughout the northeastern corner of the Grounds. The Bonesmith is found inside the building in the same area._
You say, 'Hail, Ikaena the Bonesmith'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'If Dtaemi will not move on, it is because of the Adversary. It lives to corrupt everything good. It is the main reason nothing ever returns from the well. It is bound to the mouths of insanity that inhabit the well. I'd start there.'
Kill 2 mouths of insanity 0/2 (The Well)
_The mouths of insanity are located all along the tunnel leading up from the pit in the northwest corner of the zone. There's also one in the tunnel south of Retta Kole._
Speak to the Adversary 0/1 (The Well)
_The Adversary spawns on top of the second mouth of insanity you kill. Hail it._
_Note: Your entire group can hail the adversary before it goes active, so everyone can get the update._
The Adversary says 'I knew I would be seeing you soon enough! I will devour your soul after I rend the flesh from your bones as I did to that boy!'
The Adversary says 'You will never leave this place alive!'
Kill the Adversary 0/1 (The Well)
_Its difficulty is on par with trash mobs in the zone. WARNING: If you die during this step, or otherwise cause the Adversary to lose aggro and despawn, you may have to repeat the entire task to respawn him._
The Adversary has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
The Adversary's corpse says 'This cannot be!'
Return to Dtaemi 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Dtaemi'
You have succeeded in freeing Dtaemi from the well so that he may join his mother.
Dtaemi says 'You mean I am finally free? I can be with my mother? Thank you for everything you have done for me. You will not be forgotten.'

262 platinum
Experience (about 3 AAs worth)
Faction with Dream Delvers

Modified: Tue Dec 19 17:37:26 2023 | | _This is a group progression mission (3-6 players) that begins with the Fallen Soul in the Well (located in the northern part of the large western cavern)._
_You must have completed Dtaemi's three solo tasks prior to being able to request this task._
You say, 'Hail, Fallen Soul'
Fallen Soul says 'I have watched you, and others like you come and go. Some trying to help the boy, while others were fated to becoming one of the [lost souls] here.'
You say, 'Lost souls?'
Fallen Soul says 'I was like you once. Long ago, I came to help find the boy that was lost here, but was killed for my efforts. But instead of my soul moving on, I found myself trapped within the [body] of one of the creatures here in the well. At first it was a spider, but once that body perished, I found myself in the body of a scorpion. This has continued for a very long time. To make matters worse, I have noticed that I appear to lose just a little bit more of myself each time my soul is transferred.'
You say, 'What body?'
Fallen Soul says 'I believe that most of the creatures here entomb the soul of someone lost to the dangers of the cave. It seems that the more of us that die around the same time, the longer it takes before we are cast into another body. Why, I do not know... Perhaps, if all of us were [released] around the same time, we'd be freed permanently from this fate.'
You say, 'You think you'd be released?'
Fallen Soul says 'I don't know if that is the case, but it seems just as good an idea as any. I would guess that there appears to be about twenty of us here. The window to release us is fairly short though, so I would hurry. After some time, we seem to lose any control over our physical bodies and will attack almost anything before finally waking to find that we are, yet again, entombed within some creature. Will you [help] me... us! Will you help... Us...?'
You say, 'We will help!'
Fallen Soul says 'Thank you and good luck. Let me know when you are [ready] to proceed.'
You have been assigned the task 'No Freedom in Death'.
You are tasked with freeing the many souls that have been taken and entombed within the bodies of the creatures here in the well. You have only a limited amount of time to release all the souls so that they may be free from the horrible and cruel circle of pain they currently endure.
You must find and kill the creatures in the well in order to free the captured souls within.
You say, 'Ready'
Fallen Soul says 'Be careful and good luck!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Well.

---

Free the lost souls within the corrupted creatures 0/20 (The Well)
_Kill 20 of the corrupted mobs found throughout the zone._
_Note: As of a patch in September 2012, "a lost soul" becomes KOS 35 minutes after its spawn. (These see invis.) The task can be completed within this time frame, but if you take longer, you may have to contend with these spawns in addition to the other parts of the task._
Speak to the Fallen Soul 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Fallen Soul'
Fallen Soul says 'You've done it! But you need to kill the warden. To do this you will need to kill the Lower and Higher Denizens. Then, and only then, will the Warden appear. When you do this, in the presence of the Warden, the souls you have released may attack you.'
_This opens up the next two steps._
Kill the Lower Denizen 0/1 (The Well)
_The Lower Denizen is found in the mud hole. (From the zone-in, take the eastern tunnel.) It hits for a max ~7,500; single-target rampages; and casts "Power of the Low":_
Power of the Low: PB AE 100', Magic (-675)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 20000 per tick _(3 ticks)_
2: Decrease HP when cast by 10000
3: Slow Push (100)
4: Increase Disease Counter by 18
Lower Denizen has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Lower Denizen's corpse explodes in meaty chunks.
Kill the Higher Denizen 0/1 (The Well)
_The Higher Denizen is found in the southern part of the easternmost cavern._
_It hits for a max ~7,500; flurries; and casts "Power of the High":_
Power of the High: PB AE 100', Magic (-675)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 38000
2: Stun (2.00 sec)
Higher Denizen has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Higher Denizen's corpse explodes in meaty chunks.
Kill the Warden of the Well 0/1 (The Well)
_The Warden of the Well spawns in the center of the easternmost cavern. It hits for a max ~8,000; AE rampages; and casts "Breath of Soul Corruption":_
Breath of Soul Corruption: NPC Hatelist 0', Corruption (-25)
1: Illusion: Unknown(608)
2: Unknown #430
3: Unknown #431
4: Decrease Hitpoints by 14000 per tick _(10 ticks)_
5: Decrease Movement by 50%
_Any Lost Souls that happen to be nearby will assist the Warden._
Warden of the Well has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Warden of the Well's corpse explodes in meaty chunks.
Speak to the Fallen Soul 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Fallen Soul'
Fallen Soul says 'We have been freed! Maybe for good this time. You have done more than I could have ever hoped. Here is what little I have to offer. Take it with my thanks. Let me know when you are ready to [go back].'
Claim your reward from the heavy chest 0/1 (The Well)

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | This task is the first in a series of three that begins with Giladan the Botanist [npc=36092] at location -65, -1700, -105 in the Grounds (due east of the zone-in; just east of the central garden and next to Spensur [npc=35726], the Rk. III spell NPC).
You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'These blasted things won't let me get any [work] done! First it's those rude [peponnites], then the arrogant [tree] things, then the buzzing [bixies], then those flea infested [dogs], and finally that awful pond scum [snake] and its offspring! It's no wonder why this once beautiful place is gone in the gutter!'
You say, 'What work?'
Giladan the Botanist says 'This garden used to be such a lovely place, and I've come a long way just to study the [fauna] here.'
You say, 'What fauna?'
Giladan the Botanist says 'I am a botanist, and there are some especially rare plants that grow here. But I've come into a bit of [trouble], so I am unable to do much of anything.'
You say, 'What trouble?'
Giladan the Botanist says 'It is utter chaos here! Well, at least for me! I cannot go anywhere without getting attacked by something or other. And this is why I need your [help].'
You say, 'I will help'
Giladan the Botanist says 'Thank you! You'll need to speak to Ikaena the Bonesmith at the mausoleum. He has information regarding one of the plants I am looking for.'
You have been assigned the task 'Giladan's Assistant'.

---

Speak to Ikaena the Bonesmith 0/1 (The Grounds)
Ikaena the Bonesmith [npc=36088] is found in a building in the northeastern corner of the Grounds. There are numerous bone casters and bone golems around the area, many of which see through invisibility.
You say, 'Hail, Ikaena the Bonesmith'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'Giladan is too scared to come himself? HA! That little gnome should have stayed home then! It is the Antheia Bloom [flower] you want then? '
You say, 'I want the flower...'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'Very well... But you have to do [something] for me first, since you are so... willing to help.'
You say, 'What something is that?'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'One of our golems seems to have wandered away. You must track it down and [destroy] it.'
You say, 'Destroy it?'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'Yes. It has been gone far too long for us to ever hope that it will follow orders. Kill it and bring back its bones so we can rebuild another.'
Kill 1 Lost Golem 0/1 (The Grounds)
You can find Lost Golem [npc=35923] in the western part of the zone's northwestern quadrant. It hits for a max ~6,500.
Lost Golem has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Lost Golem's corpse uses its last bit of strength to stand and fight before dying at your feet.
Loot the Golem Bones 0/1 (The Grounds)
Give the Golem Bones to Ikaena the Bonesmith 0/1 (The Grounds)
Speak with Ikaena the Bonesmith 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Ikaena the Bonesmith'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'You have done what I asked, and I would tell you how to find the Antheia Bloom. But unfortunately, it is no longer here. Destroyed by those foul rotdogs! They eat them, among other things. But even that information may be of use to Giladan. Now go.'
Speak with Giladan the Botanist 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
You've brought Giladan the information he sought.

You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'Ah, you've returned just in time! I believe I know [someone] who can help us.'
