---

Speak to Ghrechla the Patch Warden 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Ghrechla is found in the north-central part of the zone, in the pumpkin patch._
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'Ghrechla has no time for [strangers] now. Leave or suffer your fate at the hands of Ghrechla!'
You say, 'Strangers?'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'You are not stranger to Ghrechla? He does not know you he thinks. He thinks you are a [pretender]!'
You say, 'I'm not a pretender!'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'Giladan? Ghrechla knows of this [Giladan]. He hides from all that is here in the garden. Ghrechla knows everything that goes on in the garden!'
You say, 'Giladan wants to know about an antheia bloom'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'The antheia bloom? There be no antheia bloom here! Not since the [rotdogs] devoured them.'
You say, 'Rotdogs?'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'Ghrechla knows it is futile to look. No hope. No way. Nothing can do. But... Ghrechla knows [Meikona] would know a way, if one exists.'
You say, 'Who's Meikona?'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'The [leader] of the bixies to the south. Not as great as Ghrechla, but they did be the ones that planted the Antheia Bloom long time ago, refuse to re-plant because of the rotdogs!'
You say, 'The leader?'
Ghrechla the Patch Warden says 'Them bixies do not take kindly to strangers. You will no doubt have to fight your way in just to speak with her. Ghrechla has much to do. '
Speak with Meikona the Keeper 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Meikona the Keeper is in a building inside the bixie fort in the south-central part of the Grounds._
You say, 'Meikona the Keeper'
Meikona the Keeper says '(Angry) What gives you the right to [attack] us?!'
You say, 'Attack?'
Meikona the Keeper says 'My workers were only protecting the hive! It is their duty! Many you have killed, and now you seek [audience] with me?!'
You say, 'I do seek an audience with you'
Meikona the Keeper says 'Antheia bloom?! You [kill] my people over a flower?!'
You say, 'Kill?'
Meikona the Keeper says 'Uh... My death would solve nothing... so... I will give you the information you seek, if that means you will [leave].'
You say, 'I'll leave if you tell me'
Meikona the Keeper says 'Very well... The seeds of the Antheia Bloom are very resilient. They do not digest easily, if at all. You will have to take them from the rotdogs directly. There! I gave you what you wanted, whatever good that will do.'
Loot 4 Antheia Bloom Seeds from rotdogs 0/4 (The Grounds)

Give 4 Antheia Bloom Seeds to Giladan the Botanist 0/4 (The Grounds)

You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'You found them where?! I don't envy you at all! Ha, ha, ha! But I do thank you!'

You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'I can't get these blasted things to grow! It appears the very thing that helped them survive the rotdogs is making it [difficult] to germinate!'
You say, 'Difficult?'
Giladan the Botanist says 'I've been doing this a long time, and I've never seen anything like it. Perhaps if we go [back] to the bixies?'
You say, 'Back to the bixies?'
Giladan the Botanist says '(Sigh) What to do, what to do... I got it! The [treants]!'
You say, 'The treants?'
Giladan the Botanist says 'No, I don't want you to talk to them! I need you to get some of their [bark]. And I don't imagine they'll give it to you willingly.'
You say, 'I'll get some of their bark'
Giladan the Botanist says 'Yes! At least three pieces should be good.'
You have been assigned the task 'It Only Gets Worse'.

You say, 'Hail, Gardener Aeriht'
Gardener Aeriht says 'So, you are the one that has been helping that botanist fellow. I have heard of you! Making a mess of things around here! Sure, this place is dangerous, but that is just how I like it! It keeps out the riff raff! Now, you have gone and upset the balance of things around here. But since you like helping so much, perhaps you can [help] me?'
You say, 'What do you want my help for?'
Gardener Aeriht says 'The place is in turmoil! And because of you, they are planning an uprising! That, I cannot have! You must put a [stop] to them before they gain any more momentum.'
You say, 'I will stop them'
Gardener Aeriht says 'There is also something I need from you while you are ending this revolt. I seemed to have lost my book. But with the natives so aggravated, they have torn it into three pieces and each is keeping a piece. You will get these pieces back. That is what you are good for anyway, is it not? Let me know when you are ready, so that we can proceed.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Gardener'.
Blaming you for the current state of unrest here in the garden, Aeriht wants you to end the uprising of certain creatures, while also retrieving his book that is being held by them.
You say, 'Ready!'
Gardener Aeriht says 'Be careful! And do not forget the pieces of the book!'

---

Recon the bixie's forces in the hive 0/1 (The Grounds)
Recon the peponnite's forces in the patch 0/1 (The Grounds)
Recon the treant's forces in the orchard 0/1 (The Grounds)
_The bixies are in the south. The patch is in the north. The treants are in the northwest. These steps update just outside the areas for each of the above._
Report to Aeriht about all three forces 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Gardener Aeriht'
Gardener Aeriht says 'Excellent! I mean... very well. They will need to be dealt with immediately before they can grow in numbers.'
Gardener Aeriht says 'The garden creatures are rising up against me! I must have your assistance!'
Commence attack on the bixie hive 0/8 (The Grounds)
Retrieve a piece of Aeriht's book from the bixie who stole it 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Kill any eight of the bixies and loot part of the book from their corpses. Repeat this process for the peponnites and the treants._
_Target mobs in this task hit for a max ~6,500. All kill targets see invisibility and are in close proximity to each other (you'll have 2-3 per pull without a splitter). Peponnites, bixies, and treants are mezzable. The peponnites can flurry. Some of the Thule Servants are mezzable while some may be immune._
Commence attack on the peponnite's patch 0/8 (The Grounds)
Retrieve a piece of Aeriht's book from the Protector of the Patch who stole it 0/1 (The Grounds)
Commence attack on the treant's orchard 0/8 (The Grounds)
Retrieve a piece of Aeriht's book from the Bulwark of the Orchard who stole it 0/1 (The Grounds)
Deliver the pieces of the book to Aeriht 0/1 (The Grounds)
Gardener Aeriht says 'I see now why the botanist relied on you so much. This is most excellent! I just need one more thing from you. I have just discovered that one of those irritating servants has the binding to my book. You will need to retrieve it.'
Retrieve the binding from the Thule servants 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Numerous Thule Servants can be found in the western part of the zone (the area leading the House of Thule zone-in)._
Deliver the book binding to Aeriht 0/1 (The Grounds)
Gardener Aeriht says 'Finally! I have it! All I have ever been was a stinking gardener! But no more! Now, you will see the power of Aeriht!'
_He respawns as Deceiver Aeriht and attacks immediately along with a few "Aerihts Construct". Deceiver Aeriht hits for a max ~8,500. He is slowable, but not rootable/snareable. The constructs hit for a little less and are rootable/snareable. There is an achievement associated with leaving them alive._
_Note: You can have someone with FD or fade do the turn-in and then single-pull Aeriht from a distance._
Kill Deceiver Aeriht 0/1 (The Grounds)
After this, the task locks.
4 Aeriht's Constructs spawn with Aeriht. They are not mezzable.
Collect your reward from the dirty chest 0/1 (The Grounds)

---

_Task Rewards:_
Experience (about 2 AAs at Level 90)
315 platinum
30 Dream Motes
Faction with Dream Delvers

Ethereal Focus Blade
Nighmarish Page of Empowerment

Thulian Linked Gauntlets
Thulian Linked Legplates

Entered: Thu Oct 28 19:50:15 2010
Modified: Tue Feb 23 01:13:23 2021 | | _This is the first task in a series of five. It begins with Weligon Steelherder in Erudin Burning (located in the second floor of the building next to the dock area)._
You say, 'Hail, Weligon Steelherder'
Weligon Steelherder says 'I wish I could muster the will to give you a hearty greeting, friend, but the shambles our beloved High City has been reduced to finds me quite subdued. The Castle burns, the people are dying, the spirit of Erud weeps. A great apocalypse has come to the city. A wave of [terror] has spread to our city, and it appears unstoppable.'
You say, 'Terror?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Here we sit, exhausted, having been driven from the city by the Heretics and their ilk. Hundreds, perhaps thousands of them fell, and yet they simply kept coming. No matter how many we killed, there were always dozens more. It makes no sense. There cannot have been so many of them lurking in all Norrath, let alone within striking distance of [Erudin].'
You say, 'What's happened to Erudin?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Our proud city, the bastion of civilized thought in a weary world, is all but completely destroyed. Endless teams of heretics have taken the city by storm, overwhelming all of our defenses in hours. There are but [few] of us left - not enough to turn back the murderous surging masses of Heretics come here to destroy, pillage, and terrorize. The castle burns brightly above us. . . and I am at a loss to express my shame and sorrow that such an event could ever have been allowed to happen.'
You say, 'A few?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'The Knights and the sentinels are all but exhausted, three days of valiant counterattacks upon the Heretic burrow entrance have decimated their numbers even further, and sapped their strength. Others have become steeped in horrible, blasphemous visions of the total destruction of the city, and their morale has been broken. I can't expect them to take action again anytime soon. There may be a few things you can [do] to lift all of our spirits, to prove to us that in spite of this nightmarish situation, there must still be hope.'
You say, 'What can I do?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'First. . . you might speak to some of the Deepwater Knights and try to lift their morale with a few words of inspiration. If you would [agree] to do this, I'll be able to suggest other things that might be done to lift us out of this predicament.'
You say, 'I'll agree to do it'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Bless you. It is as if Erud himself sent you to us, to set things right once again.'
You have been assigned the task 'Heretics Among Us'.
Weligon Steelherder needs your help with some vital duties on behalf of the Deepwater Knights and the defenders of Erudin in their darkest hour.

---

Offer words of inspiration and encouragement to any of the Deepwater knights 0/5 (Erudin)
_Hail any Deepwater Knight NPC for these updates. You'll see responses like this:_
a Deepwater Knight says 'I cannot fathom how the city will ever be the same after the events of recent days, nor do any of the Deepwater Knights understand how we will live with ourselves now. The one time in recent memory we were called upon to defend the High City, and we failed utterly. Yes, I accept your words of inspiration and encouragement, but we need more than that now.'
a Deepwater Knight says 'I am doing better after the earlier routing of our entire force, thank you. There is no time for remorse or sympathies. Only a victory against the Heretics and the recapture of our city will dispell the lingering terror that I now feel.'
a Deepwater Knights initiate says 'The nightmarish visions began just after the attack, enough to burn my very soul. Thank you for trying to lift my spirits, it is appreciated.'
Ask Weligon Steelherder what more can be done to help 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Weligon Steelherder'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Thank you for inspiring the Knights. The relentless dread, terror, and horror has made their duties nearly unbearable. Would that we could understand where the Heretics of Paineel suddenly came into so much strength that they could overwhelm the High City. . . would that we could awaken from this endless nightmare. The next thing you can do is clear the port of Heretic infiltrators. I don't know how they made it here with the sea burning, but it should be enough to kill five of them. If you should find any infiltrator sappers or pyromancers, bring me back their heads. We will need to make a special example out of those in particular, as they are the ones best equipped to destroy the rest of Erudin.'
Kill 5 of the Heretic infiltrators near the docks 0/5 (Erudin Burning)
Retrieve the head of an Infiltrator Sapper 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Retrieve the head of an Infiltrator Pyromancer 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_As you may imagine, the above two mobs drop their heads rather commonly. They, along with the Heretic infiltrators, are found all around the dock area._
Give the head of an Infiltrator Sapper to Weligon Steelherder 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Give the head of an Infiltrator Pyromancer to Weligon Steelherder 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Speak with Weligon Steelherder 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
( **Closing?**)
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
13 Dream Motes

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the second solo task in a series of five. It begins with Weligon Steelherder in Erudin Burning (located in the second floor of the building next to the dock area)._
_You must have completed the previous task, "Heretics Among Us" prior to being able to request this one._
You say, 'Hail, Weligon Steelherder'
Weligon Steelherder says 'I wish I could muster the will to give you a hearty greeting, friend, but the shambles our beloved High City has been reduced to finds me quite subdued. The Castle burns, the people are dying, the spirit of Erud weeps. A great apocalypse has come to the city. A wave of [terror] has spread to our city, and it appears unstoppable.'
You say, 'Terror?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Here we sit, exhausted, having been driven from the city by the Heretics and their ilk. Hundreds, perhaps thousands of them fell, and yet they simply kept coming. No matter how many we killed, there were always dozens more. It makes no sense. There cannot have been so many of them lurking in all Norrath, let alone within striking distance of [Erudin].'
You say, 'Erudin?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Our proud city, the bastion of civilized thought in a weary world, is all but completely destroyed. Endless teams of heretics have taken the city by storm, overwhelming all of our defenses in hours. There are but [few] of us left - not enough to turn back the murderous surging masses of Heretics come here to destroy, pillage, and terrorize. The castle burns brightly above us. . . and I am at a loss to express my shame and sorrow that such an event could ever have been allowed to happen.'
You say, 'Few?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Yes, a few of the survivors of the city have barricaded ourselves here and in the Meditation Chamber. A few of the Deepwater Knights, some of the city sentinels, anyone else who managed to take up arms and follow us here without having their throats cut by the marauders. In Erud's name, I swear we shall earn vengeance upon these jackals who have destroyed the city. The heretics may have succeeded in their goals and made a mockery of all that Erud stood for, but I assure you we will not let any of them rest. Whatever their dark plans may be, we shall find ways to [retaliate] against them. On the other hand, if you're interested in helping Arbin Derrex to push back into the city, I can send you to help [him].'
You say, 'How can I help him?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'General [Derrex] is helping gather a force to retake the city from the Heretics, but this will take time and there are a number of jobs to do before our forces can re-enter the fight. If you can provide [assistance], I'll tell you where he is.'
You say, 'What do you know about this General Derrex?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'I don't know [him] very well, he arrived only a few weeks ago. I will say he seems to have done much to shore up the situation and prevent the rout of the Deepwater Knights from unraveling the city in its entirety. What little of the city we still hold, we can thank Derrex for standing firm in the face of complete chaos.'
You say, 'How can I be of assistance?'
Weligon Steelherder says 'Arbin Derrex is organizing the defense of the Meditation Chamber. Go south and. . . well, you shouldn't have any hard time finding it, you might have been here many times before. I think the general will be needing help finding out what the condition is in the city, and how many have died.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Fallen Citadel'.

---

Speak with General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_The general is located at about -340, 310 (in the area south of the dock area, along with several other quest NPCs)._
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Would that we could have met in happier times, friend. As you are no doubt aware by now, the Heretics have hurled an unstoppable army upon the High City and driven out all they could not get their wretched hands on and kill. Though I expect that all our friends and allies in the city proper are now dead, I will entrust you to take this casualty list and learn whether Belario, Telld'mare, and Eniela are alive or dead. . . or worse. I will also expect you to help us determine the status of the Temple of Divine Light and the City Armory. Go through the tunnel the enemy's skeletons have dug in the wall.'
Determine the condition of the Temple of Divine Light 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Further south is a tunnel leading into what was once the main area of Erudin. The ruins of the Temple of Divine Light are just outside the tunnel's exit._
Determine the condition of the City Armory 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_This is the building to the east of the Temple._
Find Sentinel Belario 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Find Gorion Telldmare 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Find Translocator Eniela 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_The above three NPCs are in close proximity to one another. Gorion and the Sentinel are on the ground while the Translocator is on the balcony outside the Temple of Divine Light._
Speak with Translocator Eniela 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Translocator Eniela'
Translocator Eniela says 'Do not try to move me, adventurer. I am done to death, and expect my spirit to depart shortly. The attack came with such force, from every angle, there was no time at all to react. Look at what they have done to the city. It is tragic, is it not? The Library stands in ruins, desecrated by the heretic mobs. This was their great aim, to destroy Norrath's greatest repository of learning and lore. Ages of information have been lost. I am. . . thankful I will not live to see what becomes of our city now.'
Kill 3 skeleton warriors 0/3 (Erudin Burning)
Speak with General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'It is sad that Translocator Eniela lingered for awhile, only to die in front of you. So terrible and tragic. Though it is unlikely that we shall all see the morrow, we will at least go down in a terrible froth of blood, and force the Heretics to pay dearly for our lives, and the lives of people like Eniela. At least you have successfully completed your task. You are fairly efficient and responsible, the way I like my subordinates.'
_Rewards:_
138 platinum
Experience (about 2.5% regular experience in Level 89)
Faction with Dream Delvers
10 Dream Motes

Modified: Wed Sep 27 20:53:54 2017 | | _This is the third solo task in a series of five. It begins with General Arbin Derrex in Erudin Burning. He is located at about -340, 310 (in the area south of the dock area, along with several other quest NPCs)._
_You must have completed the previous two tasks, "Heretics Among Us" and "The Fallen Citadel" prior to being able to request this one._
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'People may question my methods, but I get results. I'm fighting terror with terror, and I relish driving back this Heretic onslaught. We shall make them pay for every drop of blood they spilled in the High City. I intend to set you high among my followers when all is done here, if you continue to perform good service to me. This nightmare you see around you is only the beginning of a new era of the city and its people. We shall prevail and build a new tomorrow. All I ask is your continued [help].'
You say, 'How can I help?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'You can start by looking into [rumors] that Deepwater Knights may have fallen from their oaths inside the ruins of the city.'
You say, 'Rumors?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'All I ask is that the defenders of the city do the jobs they have been commissioned for. Years go by without incident, and then these black days of Heretical invasion arrive only to find most of the city's defenders unable to find their nerve. They say some of the Deepwater Knights dropped their weapons and cower in the ruins of beloved Erudin, even as Heretics and cultists run rampant. Then there is the matter of the [cultist] who has been captured and taken to the Port Authority building.'
You say, 'A cultist?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Yes, the guildmasters there keep sending me requests for further instructions. I tire of the distractions, so would you please ask Ghanlin what to [do] about the fallen Knights and the captured cultist? Perhaps the Guildmasters have some advice.'
You say, 'I'll ask what to do'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Thank you for your help.'
You have been assigned the task 'Erudin's Apocalypse'.

---

Speak with Ghanlin Skyphire 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_The first two guildmasters you'll have to hail are found in the same vicinity as the general._
You say, 'Hail, Ghanlin Skyphire'
Ghanlin Skyphire says 'Hello. I have heard of this talk of Deepwater Knights turning against their oath, and letting panic get the better of them. That should never have happened. The Knights are trained extensively to never lose their nerve. They are to fight to the death or escape if the situation is hopeless. I don't understand it. Perhaps Rjarn has heard something I haven't.'
Speak with Lanken Rjarn 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Lanken Rjarn'
Lanken Rjarn says 'It is unacceptable that a Deepwater Knight should fail in his or her duty. There is no excuse for it, and I honestly can't say that we could ever look on a dishonored Knight again. I don't know how this could happen. . . oh, what a horrendous piece of news! I refuse to believe this has happened. As for the cultist captive, I have no idea what that girl thinks she knows, but it will take someone with greater patience than I to get it out of her. Jaevins may have some further advice for you.'
Speak with Markus Jaevins 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Markus is found in the building next to the dock (approximately 110, 330, -1)._
You say, 'Hail, Markus Jaevins'
Markus Jaevins says 'The captive is in the cellar, under guard. I doubt she'll tell you much. She's quite mad. She may tell you something, since you're new here, but I doubt it. There isn't a way to know until you try. Ever interrogated a lunatic Terris Thule worshipper before? You'll have a field day, friend. I've used all my tricks, but thus far she won't say anything useful.'
Interrogate the cultist captive 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Interrogate the cultist captive again 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Interrogate the cultist captive again 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
a cultist captive says 'Erudin burns. Hundreds of confused Erudites are dead. Dread gives way to delicious terror! Oh, to see Terris in person! To watch as she brings the Endless Night to this forlorn and stodgy place! Nothing you say or do will swerve the city's new destiny!'
a cultist captive says 'Trifle with me not. Feel the flowing terror and embrace it! My Mistress, the goddess of Terror herself, comes to this place soon. She will quench you of your calm, anon. . . then Norrath shall celebrate her reign over Erudin, the Feast of Endless Dread will come to the Old Library!'
a cultist captive says 'When the Mistress returns, there will be suffering and death the likes of which Norrath has never seen! When she comes, she will eat the words of the defeated spirit of Erud and regurgitate them as a curse that will remake the city into a fortress of Terror. . . from the fallen Knights in her grasp will she take their broken oath and break your last defenses!'
Speak with Markus Jaevins 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Markus Jaevins'
Markus Jaevins says 'Gods' blood, if what she says is true, she will use the magic phrases in the Deepwater Knights' oath against us! I've heard there might be a way to reassemble them into a potent curse. . . As far as the fallen Deepwater Knights themselves are concerned, there has never been such a thing as a 'fallen Deepwater Knight' and we surely aren't breaking that tradition at a time like this, if you catch my meaning. Find them and kill them before the enemy can extract the text of their oaths from their minds. Spare me from having to send another Knight to do it, and do it quickly before the enemy becomes wise to us.'
Kill 3 of the fallen knights 0/3 (Erudin Burning)
_Fallen deepwater knights are found most commonly in the southwestern part of the zone._
Speak with General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'So you have dealt with the fallen Knights with decisive force, eh? Not what I would have done, but I suppose you had your reasons. Very well, the perimeter remains secure, for now. From now on, let's try to remember to check with me before carrying out one of the Guildmaster's orders.'

158 platinum
Experience

# Erudin Burning #4: The Destroyers' Den

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is the fourth solo task in a series of five. It begins with General Arbin Derrex in Erudin Burning. He is located at about -340, 310 (in the area south of the dock area, along with several other quest NPCs).
You must have completed the previous three tasks, "Heretics Among Us", "The Fallen Citadel", and "Erudin's Apocalypse" prior to being able to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'People may question my methods, but I get results. I'm fighting terror with terror, and I relish driving back this Heretic onslaught. We shall make them pay for every drop of blood they spilled in the High City. I intend to set you high among my followers when all is done here, if you continue to perform good service to me. This nightmare you see around you is only the beginning of a new era of the city and its people. We shall prevail and build a new tomorrow. All I ask is your continued [help].'
You say, 'How can I help more?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'You can start by looking into [rumors] that Deepwater Knights may have fallen from their oaths inside the ruins of the city, or you may take the [fight] to the enemy with an attack on the Heretic leaders.'
You say, 'I'll fight the enemy!'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Quite simply, you will confer with Guildmaster Skyphire on the destruction of remaining threats inside our perimeter, and then you will enter the city and kill the two highest-ranking Heretics. Does that sound [appealing] to you?'
You say, 'Very appealing!'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Very well. Ghanlin awaits your visit.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Destroyers' Den'.
Speak with Ghanlin Skyphire 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Ghanlin Skyphire says 'I'm glad General Derrex is finally ready to start the counter-attack. I've spoken with our people holding the south end of the chamber, and there are still undead heretics holding this end of the tunnel. With the teleportation gem fouled, we'll have to enter the tunnel ourselves. We'll have to spare all of our people for the main attack, whenever it is to come. We'll need you to clear away a few of the tunnelers at the mouth of the tunnel. When you're done with that, seek out the two enemy leaders at the former headquarters of the Deepwater Knights. Bring their heads back to the General.'
Kill 3 of the skeleton tunnelers 0/3 (Erudin Burning)
You'll find these skeletons in and outside of the tunnel leading to the main city.
Go to the former Deepwater Knights' Headquarters 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
This step updates around the building in the southeastern corner of the zone.
Retrieve the head of a Heretic commander 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Retrieve the head of a Heretic hierophant 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
The above two mob types are found in the headquarters building.
Give the head of a Heretic commander to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'So, this is a heretic commander! He has seen better days, no? Years ago I rescued three Erudite sages from the confines of Paineel, all of them due to be tortured by guttersnipes like this. With him dead, the Heretic invaders will certainly be more easily dealt with.'
Give the head of a Heretic hierophant to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'Yes. . . I recognize this one's face, I vaguely remember her from the battlefields of Kithicor Forest. One of the lesser necromantic apprentices, how many mentors must she have had to kill to gain access to such magic? At least we won't have to worry about this one any more.'
Speak with General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'With the main leaders of the Heretics dead, we shouldn't have much trouble recapturing the High City. . . That's assuming these Heretics don't bring in more reinforcements, of course!'
158 platinum
Experience (approximately 2.1 AAXP)

Entered: Thu Oct 28 20:06:38 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the final task in a series of five. It begins with the Spirit of Erud in Erudin Burning. He is located at about -340, 310 (in the area south of the dock area, along with several other quest NPCs)._
_You must have completed the previous four tasks, "Heretics Among Us", "The Fallen Citadel", "Erudin's Apocalypse", and "The Destroyers' Den" prior to being able to request this one._
You say, 'Hail, Spirit of Erud'
Spirit of Erud says 'Hail, traveler. Perhaps you have come to gawk at the fallen demi-god, the man who thought he could persuade mortals to embrace a better existence? I am nothing, I have failed. The Heretics, always so certain of themselves and their answers for everything, have utterly destroyed my city and all that I sought to establish for the betterment of not just the Erudites but all the people of Norrath. All is lost. Given that the city is being destroyed there is but one thing that would set my soul at ease in this dark hour: knowing that the five foundational tomes of Erudite civilization are intact and in safekeeping. Will you [undertake] the task of locating and returning these sacred tomes of my city, before the Heretics destroy them?'
You say, 'I'll undertake this task'
Spirit of Erud says 'I am most pleased. Seek them out in the Library of Erudin, and bring them to Arbin Derrex. He will see to their safekeeping and restoration.'
You have been assigned the task 'Raiders of the Sacred Tomes'.

---

Kill 5 of the Heretic pillagers 0/5 (Erudin Burning)
Loot the Tome of Eternal Love 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Loot the Tome of Martial Necessity 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Loot the Tome of Intellectual Grace 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Loot the Tome of Spiritual Improvement 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Loot the Catechism of Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Heretic pillagers spawn in and around the perimeter of the library (the building in the center area of the southern part of the zone). The tomes may only be dropped by certain spawns._
_There are two pillagers just west of the library entrance, two on the first floor, and one on the second floor._
Tome of Eternal Love _(can also drop from a Heretic ravager)_
Tome of Martial Necessity _(from the second floor pillager)_
Tome of Intellectual Grace _(one of the pillagers outside the west entrance of the library)_
Tome of Spiritual Improvement _(bottom floor of the library, single spawn on southeast side)_
Catechism of Erud _(from the pillager in the southwestern corner of the library's bottom floor)_
Deliver the Tome of Eternal Love to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'Excellent, you've found the Tome of Eternal Love. It is the ancient guide given by Erud to his people, for the betterment of one's most intimate relationships. I shall see that this tome is secured at once.'
Deliver the Tome of Martial Necessity to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'The Tome of Martial Necessity, my favorite of the ancient Tomes. Erud was wise enough to recognize that any society, no matter how advanced or liberal in its intellectual posture, must still take care to defend itself with force as the situation warrants. It is good that we have retrieved this Tome.'
Deliver the Tome of Intellectual Grace to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'Yes, it is very good to see that the Tome of Intellectual Grace has not been rendered to ashes. Erud had much to say about the proper way to order one's mind in this sacred volume. This tome shall be placed under guard with the Deepwater Knights.'
Deliver the Tome of Spiritual Improvement to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'You have found the Tome of Spiritual Improvement. Certainly, a controversial work, but one that seems to explain the relationship between mortals and magic to a degree. We are lucky it survives.'
Deliver the Catechism of Erud to General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
General Arbin Derrex says 'How fortunate that the Catechism of Erud is now back in our possession. Within these pages, Erud describes how to behave in order to live up to his idea of a true Erudite citizen. Had this original Tome been lost, Erudite civilization might have become easily fragmented and vulnerable to cultural schisms.'
Speak with General Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, to General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'The survivors of Erudin are lucky indeed that you took it upon yourself to rescue these five tomes from the marauding Heretics. If Erud's spirit were watching over us, I'd say he would be very pleased. You have brought back the essence of our civilization. There is no appropriate way to thank you for saving these precious works, but perhaps these coins and items will suffice.'
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience

Modified: Tue Dec 19 11:53:14 2023 | | _This is a group progression mission that begins with General Arbin Derrex in Erudin Burning. He is located at about -340, 310 (in the area south of the dock area, along with several other quest NPCs)._
_You must have completed the Feerrott and House of Thule group progression missions ("Fall of the Tae Ew" and "Welcome to My Nightmare") as well as the five Partisan tasks for Erudin Burning before being able to request this one._
_Say "willing" to get the task. Say "leave" to zone into the instance._
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Your dedication is admirable, and most certainly appreciated. We still have a long way to go before our city is reclaimed from the clutches of Heretics. You can continue to help by looking into [rumors] that Deepwater Knights may have fallen from their oaths inside the ruins of the city, or you may take the [fight] to the enemy with an attack on the Heretic leaders. Or perhaps someone with your skill is looking for a more [challenging] task?'
You say, 'What challenging task?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Brave souls like you give hope for us during this dark nightmare. Unfortunately, the worst is far from over. It is truly a shame that we don't have more like you within our ranks. Keen spirit and an appetite for battle are traits that could turn the tide for our [troops].'
You say, 'What about your troops?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Our offensive numbers have dwindled, but the Spirit of Erud insists on sending more into the fray to recover whatever knowledge lies buried in the burning rubble. I warned that it was a senseless thing to do. We lost our footing here long ago, and attempts to salvage [keepsakes] from the burning city have only resulted in more death and despair.'
You say, 'Keepsakes?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Too many have strong sentimental attachments to the city. Do not hear me wrong, I too loved Erudin at its prime, but it is time we abandon our emotions and start thinking strategically. I have urged the Spirit of Erud to withdraw forces and destroy what little is left of Erudin. Knowledge lost is far better than knowledge in the hands of heretics, don't you [agree]?'
You say, 'I agree'
General Arbin Derrex says 'The Spirit of Erud refuses to listen to my reasoning. Instead, he allowed our esteemed guild masters to accompany Deepwater Knights into the heart of battle. As is the case with most expedition crews, no one has heard from them since. It is my understanding that if word were to spread that our [pillars] have fallen, the defending forces may lose what little hope they have left.'
You say, 'Pillars?'
General Arbin Derrex says 'It is possible that their disappearance will serve as a wake up call for the Spirit of Erud. If you are [willing], head into the burning city and discover what has become of the missing Guild Masters. Even if your findings are as grim as I suspect, report to the Spirit of Erud. Perhaps he will then awake from this haze of floundering hope that has him so blind to the truth.'
You say, 'I'm willing'
General Arbin Derrex says 'I'm glad you chose to aid me once again. You should [leave] at once, as we have no time to spare.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Illuminating Truth'.
Something is amiss in the burning city of Erudin. Most of the defensive forces are holed up near the docks, but a few guildmasters accompanied Deepwater Knights into the fray and have not been heard from since. Help uncover the mystery that is veiled over the city of Erudin.
_Task map points for "fallen.txt" map file:_
P -265.0000, 950.0000, 45.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Markus
P 60.0000, 1265.0000, 46.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Lanken
P -30.0000, 1285.0000, 56.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Ghanlin
P -190.0000, 1200.0000, 37.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Wood
P 25.0000, 1420.0000, 28.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Oil
P -60.0000, 1265.0000, 28.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, Tome

---

Discover the fate of Erudin's guildmasters 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Markus Jaevins: -950, 265, 45 (balcony outside the Temple of Divine Light)
Lanken Rjarn: -1265, -60, 46 (second floor of the former Deepwater Headquarters)
Ghanlin Skyphire: -1285, 30, 56 (second floor of the library)
_Note: While they all scowl, ready to attack, they are non-KOS._
_Hail each of the guildmasters:_
Markus Jaevins groans incoherently, 'My mind, it's not my own. I fear what I am becoming. Please, end my misery, I beg you! The anguish... it's... it's too much. Bring this living nightmare to an end. My only salvation will be a swift death.'
Lanken Rjarn blinks at you stoically, with a slight twitch in his left eye. 'Who are you and why are you here? She does not require your assistance. All is well and in order. Return to the docks at once.'
Ghanlin Skyfire gasps for air as he pleads, 'Silence! She will hear you. She hears everything; every fear she adds to her growing nightmare. Help me... please... I cannot bear the dark whispers gnawing at my mind!'
Report to the Spirit of Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Spirit of Erud'
Spirit of Erud says 'So the guild masters live! Something embedded within my soul told me they had not perished, though your report clearly shows things are not well. General Arbin Derrex is so eager to demolish what is left of our city, yet his hasty actions would have meant our guild masters' demise. It is good you found them; now something must be done to [help] them.'
You say, 'How can we help?'
Spirit of Erud says 'My instincts are keener than they have ever been. Waves of memories so clear rush over me, that it's hard not to feel like I've experienced all of this before. I see visions of torches... yes, it's clear, torches that reveal the true nature of a being! One of the tomes you recovered, the Ritual Guide of the Five Flames, it holds the key to crafting these torches. Return to Arbin Derrex at once, and instruct him to turn over the Ritual Guide.'
Obtain the Ritual Guide of the Five Flames from Arbin Derrex 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_The general is in the opposite end of the room from the zone-in (west of the tunnel entrance)._
You say, 'Hail, General Arbin Derrex'
General Arbin Derrex says 'Another fool without the sense to leave a burning city. I don't have all day, I'll try to make this quick for you. . .'
_The general attacks, and so do the two mobs near him. They aren't linked to each other, so the general can be pulled single. He hits for a max ~6,000._
General Arbin Derrex has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
General Arbin Derrex's corpse looks surprised and keels over dead.
_Loot the item from his corpse._
Give the Ritual Guide of the Five Flames to the Spirit of Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Spirit of Erud says 'I should have known Arbin was traitorous. Everything about him seems so transparent now. Why did I not remember his twisted ways sooner? Enough contemplation, the time to act is upon us. Venture forth into the burning city and bring me back a charred piece of wood, a sample of veracious oil, and a page torn from the Tome of Acumen. With any luck, they can still be found within what is left of Erudin. The items appear to be mundane, so I'm convinced they've been left untouched by the heretics.'
Find and deliver a charred piece of wood to Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Find and deliver a sample of veracious oil to Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Find and deliver a page from the Tome of Acumen to Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Task Window Says:_ The Spirit of Erud needs item to craft a torch of truth. A charred piece of wood can be found in the burning rubble. A sample of veracious oil can be collected near the storage barrels within the remains of Blue Hawk's. The Tome of Acumen should be located in the library.
_The three items in question are groundspawns in the southern portion of the zone._
Wood: -1200, 190, 37 (in the burning rubble just northwest of the library)
Oil: -1420, -25, 28 (inside the building in the southeast)
Tome: -1265, 60, 28 (first floor of the library in the burning pile of books)
Speak to the Spirit of Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Spirit of Erud'
Spirit of Erud says 'I used the items you brought me to create a torch of truth. How my hands knew what to craft, is a mystery to me. All I can say is that it feels as if they've done this a hundred times before... but I digress. Let the light of the torch shine before each guild master, revealing the truth of their existence, however terrifying it may be.'
_You receive a Torch of Truth._
Light the torch before Ghanlin Skyphire 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Equip the torch while standing next to Ghanlin. This spawns a shade which attacks._
a Shade of Nightmare appears from the void, engulfing you in nightmarish shadows.
Defeat the Shade of Nightmare 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_It hits for a max ~8,000 and casts "Veil of Darkness":_
Veil of Darkness: Single Target, Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 8000 per tick
2: Blindness (1)
3: Silence
a Shade of Nightmare has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a Shade of Nightmare's corpse dissolves into a thick black mist and is carried away by the chilled night air.
Ghanlin Skyfire says 'Many thanks, friend. You have freed my mind from her dark influence.'
Light the torch before Markus Jaevins 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Equip the torch while standing next to Markus. This spawns a shade which attacks._
a Shade of Torment lets out an anguished shriek as it rushes toward you!
Defeat the Shade of Torment 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_It hits for a max ~8,000 and casts the spell "Night Terrors":_
Night Terrors: Single Target, Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 9000 per tick
2: Charm up to level 110
a Shade of Torment has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a Shade of Torment's corpse dissolves into a thick black mist and is carried away by the chilled night air.
Markus Jaevins says 'Many thanks, friend. You have freed my mind from her dark influence.'
Light the torch before Lanken Rjarn 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Equip the torch while standing next to Lanken. This spawns a shade which attacks._
a Shade of Horror appears before you, sending a chill through the air.
Defeat the Shade of Horror 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_It hits for a max ~8,000 and casts "Scream of Horror":_
Scream of Horror: Single Target, Magic (-650)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 12000
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 4000 per tick
3: Stun (2.00 sec)
a Shade of Horror has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a Shade of Horror's corpse dissolves into a thick black mist and is carried away by the chilled night air.
Lanken Rjarn says 'Many thanks, friend. You have freed my mind from her dark influence.'
Speak to the Spirit of Erud 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Spirit of Erud'
Spirit of Erud says 'My assumptions were correct; the guild masters were being influenced by nightmares. It's all beginning to come back to me now. I've lived out this torment countless times before. There is only one who is capable of weaving such an intricate and deceptive nightmare, and I know just how to reveal her. I remember hiding a crate of torches in the library. I must have known I'd be back here again... Meet me in front of the library, and together we shall end this horrific nightmare once and for all!'
Gather on the roof of the library 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_As you step onto the roof of the library, Terris Thule spawns and has this exchange:_
Spirit of Erud says 'You have been revealed, Terris Thule. No longer shall you linger in the darkness of this lurid nightmare. Come forth, and writhe in the light of truth!'
Terris Thule says 'Oh Erud, how your tireless efforts amuse me! Do you not remember the last time we met here? The last time you attempted to break free of this magnificent dream I have crafted for you? What a terrible memory you have! Fret not; plaything. Your mind shall once again be cloaked in nightmares!'
_She strikes down the Spirit of Erud and turns her attention to you._
Terris Thule senses an outsider presence and raises a boney finger in your direction, 'You will meet the same fate as the foolish Erud, and succumb to my nightmare!'
Defeat Terris Thule 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_NOTE: The task locks when Terris Thule dies._
_She hits for a max ~8,500 and casts the spells "Enduring Nightmares", "Eternal Slumber" (note: both these are permanent effects), and "Nightmare's Shroud":_
Enduring Nightmares: PB AE 200', Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 5000 per tick
2: Increase Curse Counter by 30
Eternal Slumber: Single Target, Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 7000 per tick
2: Increase Curse Counter by 30
Nightmare's Shroud: Self 0', Unresistable (-650)
1: Increase Absorb Damage by 100000
2: Increase Absorb Magic Damage by 100000
_As you take her down to 0%, you see this unfold:_
Terris Thule says 'Evade my nightmares for now, but you're no closer to finding Morell. Soon you will witness the full power of dreams!'
Terris Thule has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Terris Thule crashes to the ground, while shrieks of anguish echo through the burning city.
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Although the Spirit of Erud has no recollection of recent events, thanks to your efforts, one of the many nightmares Terris Thule weaves has been dissolved.
Spirit of Erud clutches his head in anguish and groans, 'Wh... where am I? What am I doing here? And why does my head hurt...?'

You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'These blasted things won't let me get any [work] done! First it's those rude [peponnites], then the arrogant [tree] things, then the buzzing [bixies], then those flea infested [dogs], and finally that awful pond scum [snake] and its offspring! You say, 'Peponnites?'

Modified: Sun Apr 7 14:12:14 2024 | | _This solo task begins with Grithlan Vraniq in the Grounds. He is located at -185, -1935, -223._
You say, 'Hail, Grithlan Vraniq'
Grithlan Vraniq says 'Thank the gods! I've been waiting for what seems like an eternity for someone who can [help] me!'
You say, 'Help you how?'
Grithlan Vraniq says 'I've been trapped in this damnable place for many seasons now searching for my [Deirdre]. She'd come to see the garden, but unfortunately, I could not come with her as I was away at the time. But when I returned, I found that she had not.'
You say, 'Deirdre?'
Grithlan Vraniq says 'I know she traveled through many places to get here, so I started here. But I've not been able to leave this place! If you could [assist] me, I'd be eternally grateful!'
You say, 'I'll assist you'
You have been assigned the task 'The Last One'.
Grithlan is looking for his lost love Deidre. Help him find out what her fate was and help him find his way back to her.

---

1\. Speak with Orloh in the Feerrott 0/1 (Feerrott, the Dream)
_You can find Orloh at location -255, 535, -10._
You say, 'Hail, Orloh'
Orloh says 'What you [want]? No time for chit chat. No time at all. Time!'
You say, 'I want to know about Grithlan'
Orloh says 'Grithlan? I know no [Grithlan]! Nonsense! You bug me with nonsense! Nonsense!'
You say, 'You know Grithlan...'
Orloh says 'I told you...Oh yes... [Remember] him I do. Remember.'
You say, 'What do you remember?'
Orloh says 'Not my business. No business of mine. And no [business] of yours too, if you know what is good for you. No business.'
You say, 'What business?'
Orloh says 'As I told him, the garden is no place to visit. He did not listen, and seen him no more have I. Him or his girl. His [girl].'
You say, 'His girl?'
Orloh says 'So he got you looking for [her] eh? Sucker born... blah, blah, blah. Sucker!'
You say, 'Who is 'her'?'
Orloh says 'Tell you same thing I told him. Same thing. She came here looking to get passage to the garden. But she talked to Weaver.'
You say, 'Weaver?'
Orloh says 'How should I know where he is? Do I look like his keeper? No I do not. Do not! [Shalma] is who you need to speak with. Speak with.'
You say, 'Shalma?'
Orloh says 'Shalma is partnered with Weaver! She is around here somewhere. Perhaps near the temple. Always poking her nose into things that don't concern her. Concern her!'
2\. Find and speak with Shalma 0/1 (Feerrott, the Dream)
_Shalma can be found in the Feerrott at location -1110, 630._
You say, 'Hail, Shalma'
Shalma says 'Greetings stranger. What can Shalma [do] for you?'
You say, 'Here's what you can do...'
Shalma says 'Weaver is not available right now, but I am sure Shalma can [help] you.'
You say, 'Help me...'
Shalma says 'You are wasting my time, something I value. As I told you Weaver is not [here]!'
You say, 'He's not here?'
Shalma says 'Fine! Enough! He went to the Underfoot. Had some business in Foundation. '
3\. Kill the bodyguards that protect Weaver 0/5 (The Foundation)
_When you zone into the Foundation, hug the right wall take the tunnel. You'll arrive at the Weaver camp._
_The bodyguards are KOS, but don't aggro. You can talk to Weaver first if you want, or kill the five bodyguards and then talk._
_They are snarable and don't summon._
4\. Speak to Weaver about Grithlan 0/1 (The Foundation)
You say, 'Hail, Weaver'
Weaver says 'Shalma needs to learn to keep her mouth shut! What do you [want]?'
You say, 'I want to know about Grithlan'
Weaver says 'Grithlan??? That fool? I told him his search was futile. The garden is not the place in which one would want to be lost. Many travelers have been lost there. Especially because of what they do to [people] that do go there.'
You say, 'People?'
Weaver says 'They change them! How do you think they get the many servants that roam that place? That was most likely her fate at the hands of the garden.'
5\. Tell Grithlan what you have found out 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Grithlan Vraniq'
Grithlan Vraniq says 'Weaver did tell me that. But I refuse to believe that until I see it with my own eyes! You must find her! If she made it here, I am sure there are others who have seen her. But they will not talk to me. I am ignored like I am not even here!'
6\. Speak to Ikaena about Deirdre 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Ikaena is found in a building in the northeastern corner of the Grounds. There are numerous bone casters and bone golems around the area, many of which see invisibility._
You say, 'Hail, Ikaena the Bonesmith'
Ikaena the Bonesmith says 'I have not seen anyone like you describe. But it is true that she may have fallen to the hands of the servants.'
7\. Speak to Meikona about Deirdre 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Meikona the Keeper is in a building inside the bixie fort in the south-central part of the Grounds._
You say, 'Hail, Meikona the Keeper'
Meikona the Keeper says 'Can you not just leave us be?! I have not seen who you are looking for!'
8\. Speak to Giladan about Deirdre 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Giladan the Botanist'
Giladan the Botanist says 'Hmm. . . very interesting indeed. I do recall seeing a young woman some time ago. But it was just once, and I never saw her again. But she did ask for directions to the maze.'
9\. Search the maze for Deirdre 0/1 (The Grounds)
_Find Deirdre in the hedge maze in the north-central part of the zone._
You say, 'Hail, Deirdre'
Deirdre says 'You have freed me. For that I am in your debt. Please tell Grithlan that I will see him again, where we can spend eternity together.'
10\. Kill 4 servants of Thule 0/4 (The Grounds)
11\. Return to Grithlan Vraniq 0/1 (The Grounds)
You say, 'Hail, Grithlan Vraniq'
You have found out the fate of Deirdre and helped Grithlan so that they can be together again.
Grithlan Vraniq says, 'You found her?! My dear Deirdre? She has gone to a better [place]. Of that, I am certain.'
You say, 'You're dead, brother. You and her are in the same place.'
Grithlan Vraniq says, 'I am what? Dead? How can that be? I remember... I remember... I fell to the serpent of the pond. I remember now. That is why I could not leave. That is why no one would listen. It all makes [sense].'
You say, 'Now that this all makes sense... What will you do?'
Grithlan Vraniq says, 'I can now be at peace. I can move on and be with Deirdre forever. Thank you. You have done so much for me. I must go now. My Deirdre will be waiting.'

---

_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience (about 2.5 AAs worth)
Faction with Dream Delvers has been adjusted by 10
13 Dream Motes

Entered: Tue Nov 2 01:44:39 2010
Modified: Thu Oct 20 06:59:25 2011 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Retta Kole in The Well. She's located at -430, -260, 40 (in the southern part of the first open cavern)._
You say, 'Hail, Retta Kole'
Retta Kole says 'You there! Where are we? I knew I should not have left the [camp] site. They'll dock my pay if I don't show up by dawn!'
You say, 'Camp site?'
Retta Kole says 'My employer and my friends are there. I'm a sellsword; a mercenary. And my team is going to be missing me if I don't show up. I must have been exploring the area and hit my head, since I have a really awful headache. But more importantly, I can't even [leave].'
You say, 'Why can't you leave?'
Retta Kole says 'Look, I fear nothing on the battlefield. Retta Kole is no coward. However, deep water makes my skin crawl. I can't explain it. And those nayats here, and in the water outside this tunnel. They cling to you and keep you down! I can't go out there. You have no idea how embarrassing this is to admit, but I just can't do it. Please, destroy the nayats. You'll have my eternal gratitude. In the meantime, I have to conjure the courage to swim in that water.'
You have been assigned the task 'Aquaphobia'.
Retta has asked you to destroy the nayats infesting the waters and caverns. While capable of fighting herself, she fears the deep water, and refuses to confront them in this situation. Kill all the nayats and return to Retta with the news.

---

Destroy the young nayats 0/3 (The Well)
Destroy the grown nayats 0/3 (The Well)
_Nayats are found throughout the tunnels and caverns in the zone._
Speak with Retta Kole 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Retta Kole'
The jelly creatures called nayets have been destroyed. Retta the mercenary can now escape, assuming she can conquer her fear of swimming in deep water.
_Rewards:_
Experience
Minor Faction with Dream Delvers

Modified: Tue Jul 7 00:37:08 2015 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Dahlmar Raltend in The Well. He's an Erudite located at 15, -335, -35 (next to the mud pool at the end of the tunnel east from the zone-in)._
You say, 'Hail, Dahlmar Raltend'
Dahlmar Raltend says 'Where am I? How did we get here? Those horrid mud creatures are closing in, and I can't find an exit! I can't remember how I got here, and I didn't even bring a [map].'
You say, 'You didn't bring a map?'
Dahlmar Raltend says 'I'm a cartographer, so I always have my maps on me. Still, would it even help me? Especially with all of this [mud] here. I'm going to lose my mind if I can't get out of here!'
You say, 'What mud?'
Dahlmar Raltend says 'This mud. It reminds me of the quicksand that almost killed me when I was a child. I nearly drowned in it! I can't shake the horrible memories. Please, you have to help me. I'm sure I can help us get out of here, but you'll need to destroy these mud creatures first. I'm no fighter!'
You have been assigned the task 'Drowned in Mud'.
You've been asked to destroy the mud creatures that are terrorizing Dahlmar. They seem to be emerging from the slippery mud pool room, but might be elsewhere as well.

---

Destroy mud golems 0/3 (The Well)
Destroy sludge golems 0/3 (The Well)
_Golems can be found in the mud pool room as well as the tunnel leading out of the area (there's a small pit in the bottom of the mud pool that leads to the tunnel out)._
Speak with Dahlmar Raltend 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Dahlmar Raltend'
Your task 'Drowned in Mud' has been updated.
You've cleared out the mud golems and given Dahlmar some peace of mind. There are bound to be more emerging from the muck, however.
Dahlmar Raltend says 'Thank you so much. Maybe I can find a way out of here now!'
_Rewards:_
Experience
Minor Faction with Dream Delvers (+5)

Modified: Mon Dec 13 02:43:55 2010 | | _This shared task (1-6 players) is the first of two that begins with Traelphor in The Well. He's located at -310, -665, 30 ((n the tunnel that leads out of the easternmost cavern -- its entrance is a false wall)._
You say, 'Hail, Traelphor'
Traelphor says 'Is it [time]? I am here. Always here.'
You say, 'It's time'
Traelphor says 'But I am not ready! I am not complete! I cannot leave. No life! No life will I have until I am [whole]!'
You say, 'Whole?'
Traelphor says 'A sly one you are! You take my [soul] and leave me here to rot, and now you pretend to not remember! What manner of game is this?'
You say, 'Your soul?'
Traelphor says 'Is that it then? You wish me to relive my agony? My despair? I have not forgotten! I will never forget! And to make matters worse, you hide the location of my very being from anyone who might have helped. Now no one will [help]!'
You say, 'I can help'
Traelphor says 'Surely you will deceiver! Teller of lies! You would do best to stick with mocking your lackey Plomaedis in the garden. I am sure he will do whatever it takes to please your wickedness. '
You have been assigned the task 'All Is Not Forgotten Part 1'.
Traelphor is still unconvinced that you are trying to help him. You must find out what you can from Plomaedis about what you need to do to help Traelphor.
For someone who has so little time for anything, Plomaedis sure talks a lot. You may only need to listen to him to get the clue you need.

Modified: Tue Jan 4 21:48:42 2011 | | _This shared task (1-6 players) is the second of two that begins with Traelphor in The Well. He's located at -310, -665, 30 ((n the tunnel that leads out of the easternmost cavern -- its entrance is a false wall)._
_You must have completed his first task, "All Is Not Forgotten Part 1" before you can request this one._
You say, 'Hail, Traelphor'
Traelphor says 'I am surprised you have returned this [time]. No one has made much of effort to help me in the past. And those who have tried have never returned. But I see that the [scrolls] you have found are indeed the clues to name of the one responsible for my present condition.'
You say, 'The scrolls are clues?'
Traelphor says 'It appears that the answers to the scrolls are the key to deciphering the [final] scroll. The writings within it will no doubt appear to be gibberish. But by applying the key properly, you should be able to read it.'
You say, 'Where's the final one?'
Traelphor says 'You will need to find the final scroll here in the well. I have been pondering this. And I think it is the [Maorda] that has it. '
You say, 'What is the Maorda?'
Traelphor says 'A vile creature in the employ of the one responsible. It occasionally appears to feast on creatures here in the well. It seems to have a particular taste for spiders. I am sure if you bait him with the dead bodies of the spiders, it will come to feast.'
You have been assigned the task 'All Is Not Forgotten Part 2'.

---

Kill 10 spiders 0/10 (The Well)
Speak to Traelphor 0/1 (The Well)
_NOTE: Hailing Traelphor triggers the spawn of Maorda. You may want to clear nearby trash mobs before doing this as Maorda flees at low health. He doesn't cast any spells._
Kill Maorda 0/1 (The Well)
_Maorda is slowable and snareable, and not much harder than other trash mobs in the zone._
Loot the Final Scroll from Maorda 0/1 (The Well)
Tell Traelphor the name you deciphered from the Final Scroll 0/1 (The Well)
_Translation of the final scroll's text:_
He lives to deceive, for he is the deceiver. He is Raejic, the devourer of souls.
_Say to Traelphor, "Raejic"._
Kill Raejic 0/1 (The Well)
_Raejic is the same as Maorda: Slowable and snareable, and not much harder than other trash mobs in the zone. He doesn't cast any spells._
_The task auto-completes as Raejic dies._

You say, 'Hail, Curator Risald'
Curator Risald says 'You do not appear to be a dreamer. You should not be in this place. However, since you are here, perhaps I can make use of you. I have a [problem] that must be addressed.'
You say, 'What problem?'
Curator Risald says 'An inventory was recently conducted and we found several books have been removed from the library. Obviously we need these books to be returned and that's where you come in. Would you be willing to [search] the house and surrounding grounds for the missing books?'
You say, 'I'll search for them'
Curator Risald says 'How fortunate. Some of these have been missing for quite some time, while others have vanished only recently. One such book, Fallen Gods, was most recently seen in the hands of Athmos the Poet. I look forward to the return of the missing volumes.'
You have been assigned the task 'Book Hunt'.
Curator Risald has asked you to locate several books that have gone missing from the library. He has advised you to search the first floor of the house and the surrounding grounds.
Curator Risald mentioned that Fallen Gods was last seen in the hands of Athmos the Bard.

# You Spoony Bard!

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with Athmos the Bard at the zone-in of the Grounds. The book reward ("Fallen Gods") is used in the "Book Hunt" task from the Library.
Note: You need to have the task "Book Hunt" active in your quest journal from the Library in order to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Athmos the Bard'
Athmos the Bard says 'Ooo, you startled me! I was busy composing some new lyrics based on a [book] I read recently. Was there something you wanted?'
You say, 'What book?'
Athmos the Bard says 'The book? It was called [Fallen Gods]. I had recently come across it in a fantastic library inside the house and decided to take it with me. '
You say, 'Fallen Gods? I need that book!'
Athmos the Bard says 'Well, I don't have the book anymore, you see. While I was wandering the gardens, I had several unfortunate encounters with the beasts therein and [dropped everything] I was carrying. I barely escaped with my life!'
You say, 'You dropped everything?'
Athmos the Bard says 'Yes, including the book. Say, if you were to go out into the garden and [find my belongings], I would be happy to give you the book.'
You say, 'I can find your belongings...'
Athmos the Bard says 'What a fortuitous encounter! I'll wait here while you go looking.'
You have been assigned the task 'You Spoony Bard!'.
Athmos the Bard wants you recover his various belongings from monsters in the Garden. If you are successful, he will give you the book 'Fallen Gods' as a reward.

Recover Athmos' Rapier from the bixies 0/1 (The Grounds)
The lute is dropped by bone golems. Capes are dropped by rotdogs. Satchels are dropped by mulch cubes. Rapiers are dropped by bixies.

You say, 'Hail, Varesa K`Zen'
Varesa K`Zen says 'I can feel my power slipping away. Why can't I remember all my spells? This is my worst [nightmare] come to life.'
You say, 'Nightmare?'

Varesa K`Zen says 'Have you not looked around? Those armed and armored thugs patrolling around like this is a barracks. The custodians who attack anyone who passes by. Modified: Sat Sep 28 07:21:33 2024 | | _This is a non-progression group mission (3-6 players) that begins with Tarlyn Caelith in the Library. He is found at location 55, 285, -3 (in the hallway west of the room with the various scholars)._
_There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task._
_Note: This is a Tier 4 difficulty task, meaning mob difficulty will be along the same lines as Morell's Castle and Sanctum Somnium._
You say, 'Hail, Tarlyn Caelith'
Tarlyn Caelith says 'Please tell me you are not here to try to kill me. I'm getting quite sick of running around in this library. At least I'm having better luck than that Kintorom fellow. Still, even though I'm [close], I still need more time!'
You say, 'How close?'
Tarlyn Caelith says 'Very close! To finding the answers. About what is [wrong]. I'm not like the others. I know something is wrong here. Though I distinctly remember finding this library, I believe my memory, and almost everything about this place is again, for lack of a better word, [wrong].'
You say, 'Wrong?'
Tarlyn Caelith says 'Yes. In fact, I think you know more about this than anyone else here. Still, I'm afraid my moment of clarity won't last too long. I'll need to act fast. There is a spellbook here in this library. I saw it, I almost had it, but one of those strange librarians took it and almost killed me. We must [acquire] it!'
You say, 'Yes, we must acquire it...'
Tarlyn Caelith says 'I'm glad to see you agree. Let me know when you are [prepared]. I warn you, this will become violent, and I am not going to be of much use to you in a fight. However, I will be able to use that book once we find it.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Moment of Clarity'.
You say, 'We're prepared!'
Tarlyn Caelith says 'Excellent. This way. We'll return to the first floor and start there.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Library.

---

_Summary: You'll have to find Unusual Tomes, Old Spell Codices, and Worn Books (groundspawns you'll find as you make your way upstairs); and kill listless librarians, weary assistants, and finally, Sage Weldan._
_Mobs in this zone are mostly yellow /cons to Level 90 and mostly melee._

_Sage Weldan's DPS output is only slightly higher than that of the trash mobs in the zone. He has ~1.1 million hitpoints and casts the spells "Delusion Visage I" and "Shadow Lore":_
Delusion Visage I: PB AE 30', Chromatic (-700)
1: Decrease Movement by 80%
Shadow Lore: PB AE 200', Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 11900

Tarlyn Caelith says 'I knew you could do it! This is just the book I was looking for. I hope Sage Weldan didn't give you too much trouble? This place is mad. Thank you friend. With your help, I might be able to end this nightmare. At least, I hope so. I'll need some time to study. Don't let me keep you!'
Take your reward from the chest 0/1 (The Library)
With your aid, Tarlyn Caelith obtained the book of magic he was looking for. However, it is unclear if he will remember what has transpired, or be lucid enough to decipher the text to assist some of the trapped dreamers. You had best continue your efforts in other areas while he studies the text.

_Note: Unlike the other group mission for the Well, this task is of Tier 3 difficulty._
You say, 'Hail, Groundskeeper Seamus'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'What are you doing snooping about in the garden? Your fingernails are far too clean to be a groundskeeper! If you had any sense about you, stranger, you'd scurry [back] to wherever it is that you came from.'
You say, 'Back?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'Yes, go back. Run home, shoo! Do I need to spell it out for you? This place is much too dangerous for someone who looks as delicate as you. If you are so eager to meet your demise, then please, by all means, stick around. Just heed one bit of advice... Show extra caution around the [well]'s edge; it's a long way down.'
You say, 'The well?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'This well dug itself for the most part, starting as a circular patch of land that radiated life. The soil was so moist and light that it appeared to float above the earth, until one day the fertile mound collapsed upon itself, creating what seemed to be an endless [hole].'
You say, 'What's this about an endless hole?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'While the core of the hole continued to erode deeper into the earth, the area near its surface flourished, thanks to the unusual [water] from the natural well. The beautiful life that blossomed was the stuff of dreams. So much so, that those who came to linger near the fragrant flowers that encircled the well often fell into a euphoric slumber.'
You say, 'Unusual water?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'Of course like most wells, the garden's life source couldn't sustain itself forever. It wasn't long before the well ran [dry], and the land surrounding it cried out in hunger. While most would assume water to be nourishing, what was secreted from the well was anything but.'
You say, 'Dry?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'Like any good predator, the well used its enchanted water to lure life to its mouth. Those that lingered around the well were too concerned with bliss to notice its insatiable [hunger]. Completely dependant on the well, many sought to expand it, sending diggers into the hole in an effort to strike that precious vein of water again.'
You say, 'Hunger?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'Of course the diggers haven't had any luck, and the well remains dry. They are too blinded by their quest for bliss to understand the true essence of the well. They should be happy that someone as knowledgeable as me knows what the well needs; what it hungers for. As long as I am here to [feed] the well, a trickle of water still remains.'
You say, 'What do you feed it?'
Groundskeeper Seamus says 'Do you really want to know what I feed the well? Come close and I will whisper it to you. After all, it's a little known secret... that the well feeds off of [souls]!'
You have been assigned the task 'What Lies Below'.
You say, 'Souls...?'
Groundskeeper Seamus gives you a hostile shove, offsetting your balance. 'What souls you ask? Why the souls of unsuspecting travelers like you!' The trail of Groundskeeper Seamus' laughter can be heard as you tumble down the well.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Well.

---

_All mobs in the zone are immune to mez and root/snare. Scorpions and nameds see invisibility (there's also a mix of live and undead)._
Discover the source of the cries 0/1 (The Well)
Continue to investigate the source of the cries 0/1 (The Well)
The cries fill with agony as they grow louder, 'Is anyone still alive out there? Help me... please!'
_These steps update as you proceed through the tunnel to the south of the zone-in._
Speak to Edgar Steelmor 0/1 (The Well)
_In the first cavern, you'll find Edgar Steelmor who's surrounded by several scorpions._
You say, 'Hail, Edgar Steelmor'
Edgar Steelmor strains to talk, but through the pain his voice is hardly louder than a whisper. 'I...I haven't much time. The...scorpion's sting, it's... it's making my limbs numb. Antidote... in my pack, but it was lost in the mud. Please ...help. I will tell you everything.'
Find Edgar's backpack and return it to him 0/1 (The Well)
_Edgar's Backpack can be found in the mud area. It's in the room with the mud pool (same room as the Lower Denizen in the "No Freedom in Death" task. Pick it up and deliver it to Edgar._
Speak to Edgar Steelmor 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Edgar Steelmor'
Edgar Steelmor says 'You're a sight for my weary eyes. It's been longer than I can remember since I've seen a face so vibrant and full of life. I came down here with my digging crew ages ago; our foreman was determined to see the well restored. As countless days went on, our strained efforts proved fruitless. Many diggers began to grow pale. Some thought it was due to a loss of spirit, but I wasn't as convinced. The well seemed to be sucking the life right out of them! So I went to [Foreman] Dreck with my concerns.'
You say, 'Foreman Dreck?'
Edgar Steelmor says 'Blinded by his desperation to find water, Dreck ignored my pleads to return to the surface. Instead, he banished me from camp, tossing me into the pit here with spiders, scorpions, and a myriad of other creatures who'd love to have me for dinner. I've been searching for a way [out] ever since. Death seems a better fate than spending an eternity as a soulless digger, picking away at the well's walls.'
You say, 'A way out?'
Edgar Steelmor says 'I've located a weak spot on the wall. It's a little out of my reach, but I imagine with all the loose rubble around here I can create a something sturdy enough to climb on. My supply is limited, and I'll need your help collecting more loose stones. It's not an easy task, but it's our best bet at getting out of here alive.'
Collect 8 piles of rubble and deliver them to Edgar 0/1 (The Well)
_Piles of Rubble can be found as groundspawns throughout the area. They appear as rocks on the ground. Collect eight of them and hand them to Edgar._
Speak to Edgar Steelmor 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Edgar Steelmor'
Edgar Steelmor says 'This should be plenty, but I fear upon further inspection that the wall can't be breeched with my basic mining tools. It's going to take a large blast to create a hole large enough for us to escape. I know of only one person with those kinds of explosives... Foreman Dreck. He won't give up his dynamite without a fight.'
Defeat Foreman Dreck 0/1 (The Well)
_Foreman Dreck spawns a distance away. He hits for a max ~6,500 and has about 840,000 hitpoints._
Foreman Dreck has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Foreman Dreck's corpse collapses.
Loot a Bundle of Dynamite 0/1 (The Well)
Speak to Edgar Steelmor 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Edgar Steelmor'
Edgar Steelmor says 'I take it you didn't have any trouble snagging the dynamite... I stacked the rubble you brought me into a fairly sturdy pile. If you climb to the top you should be close enough to the weak spot in the wall to light the dynamite and blast a hole.'
Use the dynamite near the weak part of the wall 0/1 (The Well)
_Right-click the dynamite in the area southwest of Edgar (towards the wall). This will open up a tunnel._
Search the tunnel for a way out 0/1 (The Well)
An icy chill blows through the tunnel as the resting place of Zurlak the Soul Leech is disturbed.
Zurlak the Soul Leech scurries toward you, ready to kill.
Defeat Zurlak the Soul Leech 0/1 (The Well)
_Task Window Says:_ You have uncovered the creature that's sucking life from within the well. Destroy it before it consumes you!
_At the end of the tunnel is an insanity mob. It hits for a max ~8,000; has about 1 million hitpoints; and casts the spell "Soul Siphon":_
Soul Siphon: PB AE 200', Disease (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 3000 per tick
2: Decrease Mana by 2000 per tick
3: Improved Spell Effect (Barren Soul)
4: Increase Disease Counter by 30
Zurlak the Soul Leech has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Zurlak the Soul Leech's corpse collapses.
Speak to Edgar Steelmor 0/1 (The Well)
You say, 'Hail, Edgar Steelmor'
Edgar Steelmor says 'Hey, you're still here! It's a good thing, too. You wouldn't believe what I found! When you set off those explosives, beneath all the rubble I uncovered this ornate chest. Funny enough, there was a gate potion inside! I didn't bother with the rest of the treasure though. Just let me keep the potion, and you can have what's left.'
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (The Well)

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Exterminator Cedrin in Erudin Burning. He is located at -400, 330 (in the same area as the General and the Spirit of Erud -- the area south of the dock)._
You say, 'Hail, Exterminator Cedrin'
Exterminator Cedrin says 'Rats, rats, [rats]... Never before recently did we have such a problem with the filthy pests.'
You say, 'Where did all these rats come from?'
Exterminator Cedrin says 'The heretics brought them our way, only to have it intensified by the rate of our fallen brethren filling our streets and our docks. We simply cannot cleanse the area fast enough. Nor can I [dispose] of the rodents on my own.'
You say, 'Dispose of them?'
Exterminator Cedrin says 'I had been tasked with helping the cleansing of our area as our more able bodied knights fight the frontlines. It was hoped that in disposing of the rodents and helping give our brethren a proper burial that the pestilence would fall back from us. The task is too much for this old man to bear. If it's not too much trouble, would you be able to [assist]?'
You say, 'I will of course assist you'
Exterminator Cedrin says 'A handful of rats is all I ask, and proof of such in the form of intact tails. I promise that I will give credit where credit is due when you return. May Prexus' cleansing waters wash away the disease that surrounds us.'
You have been assigned the task 'Extermination'.
Erudin's docks have become overrun with an infestation of rats with the arrival of the Heretics and the rampant death and disease that covers the once glorified city. Exterminator Cedrin has requested your assistance with this task of cleaning the area of the pests, as well as proof of your deed.

Exterminator Cedrin gives a nod as he gathers the tails received and burn them with a swift incantation, reducing them to dust. 'We owe you many thanks, friend.'
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.
