Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a non-progression group mission (3-6 players) that begins with Tensia Hauden in Erudin Burning. She is found at location 80, 300, 0 (inside the port authority building next to the dock)._
_There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task._
You say, 'Hail, Tensia Hauden'
Tensia Hauden says 'There is no time for pleasantries. Too many of the citizens of Erudin are missing, and I fear there may be the ring of [truth] to what the captive cultist had to say about their fate.'
You say, 'A ring of truth?'
Tensia Hauden says 'I couldn't help but overhear, as the troops were doing their interrogation, she said something about them 'ending up in the Vestibule with the other doomed prisoners'. I assume she means the vestibule by the Toxxulia Forest entrance of the city. If there are prisoners there, we must try to free them, don't you agree? This mission will require boldness and excellent timing. Will you [volunteer]?'
You say, 'I'll volunteer'
Tensia Hauden says 'It will not be easy, of course. This location may or may not be guarded, barricaded, or warded against our approach. At a time like this, we must at least make an effort. We cannot afford to leave anyone behind now. I do not relish sending people to chase down rumors, but the knowledge that there may be innocent souls left behind - abandoned to torture at the hands of the heretics or worse - will gnaw at us if we fail to act. I don't mean to usurp the authority of General Derrex, or the guildmasters, but someone must be spared from the defense of the remainder of the city to [do] this task.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Pit of All Fears'
Too many of the civilians of Erudin are still missing since the heretics invaded the city. Tensia Hauden has asked you to follow up on rumors that the heretics are using the former vestibule by the Toxxulia Forest entrance to the city as a temporary dungeon. If there is any truth to this, you are to storm the holding pen and rescue as many of the prisoners as possible.
You say, 'I'll do it'
Tensia Hauden says 'It warms my heart to see some adventurers willing to follow the example of Erud, even in these trying times. Make your way through the city, to the Toxxulia Forest exit. I've already sent Trevis Malfin ahead to look for signs of prisoners, but he can do little by himself. I don't know the condition of the teleport gems that take you into the lower part of the vestibule, perhaps Trevis can help you gain access.'

---

Explore the makeshift dungeon at the southern edge of Erudin Fallen 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Trevis Malfin spawns in the southernmost part of the zone. Tell him "ready" to zone into the instance._
You say, 'Hail, Trevis Malfin'
Trevis Malfin says 'Good to see you. The makeshift prison is below us. The heretics down there guarding it are gone for awhile, but they may be back any second. If they come back, I'll have to hide. Let's get you down there. Tell me when you're [ready] to go.'
You say, 'Ready!'
Trevis Malfin says 'Very well, be careful down there, everyone seems to be in a trance of some sort.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Pit of All Fears.
Hail Yoren Sylgar 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
_Once in the instance, hail Yoren Sylgar to spawn the first set of mobs to be killed._
You say, 'Hail, Yoren Sylgar'
Yoren Sylgar says 'It has been a hellish existence down here. The heretics literally threw us down here. I don't know how long we've been here, it can't have been more than a couple days and yet it's felt like an eternity; I've watched these people starve and die before my very eyes, begging and pleading for someone to do something. Nothing we tried ever seemed to work! Over there, Altherod and a few of the others made some sort of pact against the rest of us, since then we've been dying faster. It seems like one of my group has disappeared every time I doze off; as a result I am afraid to sleep. We must get out of here, yet it is hopeless.'
_Hailing him triggers the spawn of the first set of mobs to kill (12 prisoners + Altherod the Ghoul)._
_Task Window Says:_ It appears the rescue effort will be hampered by an emergent need to defend yourselves against the prisoners, some of whom have survived better than others down here. Gather evidence that at least one of the prisoners has resorted to cannibalism.
Kill six starving prisoners 0/6 (Erudin Burning)
a starving prisoner says 'Another fool without the sense to leave a burning city. I don't have all day, I'll try to make this quick for you. . .'
a starving prisoner has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a starving prisoner's corpse looks surprised and keels over dead.
Kill six skeletal prisoners 0/6 (Erudin Burning)
a skeletal prisoner looks at you through eyeless sockets, and charges forward to attack!
a skeletal prisoner has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a skeletal prisoner's corpse falls apart under the shattering blows.
Kill Altherod the Ghoul 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Altherod the Ghoul says 'Fighting me will only result in pointless death. You should have knelt before the new masters of Erudin!'
Altherod the Ghoul has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Altherod the Ghoul's corpse screams and falls.
Loot a partially-eaten hand from Alterod the Ghoul 0/1 (Erudin Burning)

Yoren Sylgar says 'So, Altherod and his cronies were. . . devouring the bodies of the victims? How disgusting, and for an Erudite to stoop so low, illustrates the danger we are in. Madness is taking every one of us. Fear drives the madness; even now the gnawing dread and terror of dying horribly in this place tear at my sanity. Oh, it seems the Heretics have gotten wise to your adventure. . .'
_Turning in the hand triggers the spawn of a second set of mobs to kill (5 heretics + Anchar Synov)._

a heretic assassin's corpse says 'Terris! I have failed thee!'
Kill Anchar Synov 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Anchar Synov says 'Another fool without the sense to leave a burning city. I don't have all day, I'll try to make this quick for you. . .'
Anchar Synov has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Anchar Synov's corpse looks surprised and keels over dead.
Loot a strange-smelling spider egg sac from Anchar Synov 0/1 (Erudin Burning)

---

Deliver a strange-smelling spider egg sac to Yoran Sylgar 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Yoren Sylgar says 'Ah, Anchar apparently didn't have time to destroy the spider egg sac he confiscated from me. Anchar Synov, witless heretic thug... now a witless DEAD heretic thug, ha-ha, hah! What do I want with these spider eggs? Well, for one thing, their smell will protect me from the one who laid them. I have been down here so long, I deserve to live at least a little while longer. Longer than you. Remember, by coming here, you asked for this. You asked for this!'
_This turn-in triggers the spawn of Hulkenfang at the portal stone._
Kill Hulkenfang 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Hulkenfang hisses and rushes into combat.
_Hulkfenfang hits for a max ~8,600 and casts "Corrosive Webbing":_
Corrosive Webbing: PB AE 100', Corruption (-200)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 2500 per tick
2: Decrease AC by 127
3: Increase corruption counter by 18
_He spawns adds called "a young terrorspinner" (also hit for a max ~8,600; immune to mez/snare) which do NOT despawn upon Hulkenfang's death._
Hulkenfang has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Hulkenfang's corpse leaves a small pool of blood and poison where it falls.
Kill Yoren Sylgar 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Yoren Sylgar says 'Another fool without the sense to leave a burning city. I don't have all day, I'll try to make this quick for you. . .'
_Yoren Sylgar hits for a max ~8,600 and casts nothing._
Yoren Sylgar has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Yoren Sylgar's corpse looks surprised and keels over dead.
Hail Seveth Dinallin 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
You say, 'Hail, Seveth Dinallin'
Seveth Dinallin says 'I am pleased you survived Hulkenfang's onslaught. I wish Yoren could have held out awhile longer before descending into madness. Our nightmares take shape in this place. Is the city around us real? Are we all just losing our minds simultaneously? Or is there some power behind it all? At any rate, I am too far gone to deserve a rescue. Coming here, you have only fallen deeper into the web of nightmares that surrounds us. Take this for your efforts and escape while you can.'
Claim your reward from the blackened chest 0/1 (Erudin Burning)
Though the effort to rescue the captured citizens of Erudin was largely futile, it did provide some interesting insights into the situation now facing the surviving defenders. Something dark and mysterious is altering the perceptions of the inhabitants, a phenomena which defies all reckoning.

Modified: Sat Oct 22 20:55:41 2011 | | _This task is the first in a series of five that begins with Abbit Scribbletch, a gnome found in the first room on the left immediately on entering the northeastern wing of the House of Thule's Upper Floors._
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'I cannot believe this has [happened]. I should know better than to trust a source with whom I do not normally deal with. But I was so close!'
You say, 'What happened?'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'I am a collector, of many things, but books are my main passion. I was so close, or so I thought, to finding the lost memoirs of the [Morzad] leader Argan. '
You say, 'Who are the Morzad?'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'It makes sense that you've never heard of the Morzad. They were an underground group of explorers and archeologists who sought powerful relics in order to gain more power for themselves. No one really knew to what end. The Morzad were thought to have vanished after [Argan] was killed by Cazic Thule himself.'
You say, 'Who's Argan?'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'I was getting to that! Argan made note of every relic the Morzad ever found and kept in a book along with its location. The book has come to be known as Argan's Memoir. This is why I am here. I thought I had good information that the book was here, but as soon as I arrived, I have been plagued by attackers. I am a collector, not a combatant! And now I am stuck here it seems. Will you [help] me?'
You say, 'I'll help you'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'Excellent! I was to meet someone named Keega here. You must find him and talk to him in my stead. But you must hurry. I am sure he has been waiting for me, but I have been unable to move. '
You have been assigned the task 'Morzad Chronicle - Argan's Memoir'.
Abbit Scribbletch is stuck here while looking for Argan's Memoir. He has asked for your help in meeting the one who knows where the book can be found.

---

Speak to Keega 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
_Keega is an Erudite found in the first hall of the northwestern wing._
You say, 'Hail, Keega'
Keega says 'You are not [Abbit]!'
You say, 'Abbit sent me...'
Keega says 'Very well... The book he seeks is being held by an imp who calls himself Khalis. He is especially annoying. But it shouldn't take much to get the book from him. He seems to not know, or care, what it is.'
Kill 2 protective imps 0/2 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
_To get to Khalis and his protective imps, keep going through the northwestern wing until you come to the rotdog room. From there, go south and through that hall to Khalis' room._
_Note: The protective imps used to be triggered spawns by hailing Keega. A patch changed them to regular spawns, so they'll always be up next to Khalis (unless someone's very recently killed them)._
_Khalis is not aggro, but the imps are as well as a number of undead in the room. They each hit for a max ~6,500._
a protective imp says 'I will pry those pretty little eyes from your skull and feast like a king upon your every fleshy nerve!'
a protective imp has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a protective imp's corpse says 'Oops! Back to The Faceless I go. I'll be back, and your eyes will be mine!'
Speak to Khalis 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, Khalis'
Khalis says 'What have we here? How do I know? Quiet, let me talk! What book? You know what book! Ah, that book! I will give you the book. But first, you must answer something for me. What can burn but is not fire, and has a way of destroying rationality? '
Answer Khalis' riddle 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hate'
Khalis says 'Correct! I don't get it? Hush! It was too easy! You have answered correctly, so here is the book.'
_You receive Argan's Memoir._
Deliver Argan's Memoir to Abbit Scribbletch 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)

Your task 'Morzad Chronicle - Argan's Memoir' has been updated.
Abbit Scribbletch says 'I cannot believe you retrieved it, and so easily! Thank you.'
You have found the book that in which Abbit was looking.

# Morzad Chronicle - Never Trust a Morzad (#3)

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Time:** 15 minutes
**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the third in a series of five that begins with Abbit Scribbletch, a gnome found in the first room on the left immediately on entering the northeastern wing of the House of Thule's Upper Floors.
You must have completed his first two tasks prior to being able to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'Good, you have returned. I know of someone that may be able to help. Goes by the name [Laerdin], and he was on the floor below us. '
You say, 'Who is Laerdin?'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'He is a very timid, yet very smart man. He has a peculiar way of speaking, but should have something for us if you listen. Would you go [speak] with him?'
You say, 'I'll speak with him'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'Let me know what he says, because I am at a loss I am afraid. '
You have been assigned the task 'Morzad Chronicle - Never Trust a Morzad'.
Abbit is no closer to finding the real book than he was before. Maybe this new contact of his will help shed some light on what is going on here.
Speak to Laerdin Helleh 0/1 (House of Thule, Lower Floors)
Laerdin Helleh is a Halfling found in the northwestern hall of the House of Thule's Lower Floors (same hall as The Library entrance).
You say, 'Hail, Laerdin Helleh'
Laerdin Helleh says 'What you say hello? Reason I know your visit. Already the answer is known to Abbit. If what has transpired is thought of, realize the location of the book he will. Ask the basic question to yourself... Who is responsible for the book of fakery? Then, and only then, will you realize the location of the real book.'
Speak to Abbit Scribbletch 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'He said what? What does that mean? WAIT! OF COURSE! The filthy demon that gave you the fake book. It has to have the real one!'
Speak to Khalis 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, Khalis'
Khalis says 'It was not I! What do I care about a book. Some human gave me a taste of flesh for me to hand the book to someone who asked. It was my idea to add the riddle. A little bit of fun, you understand. I was to get more of the tasty flesh but he has not returned. I saw him head to the southeastern part of the wing... Gall, Gala, Gallo, Galo, something like that.'
Find and speak to Galo Maric 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Galo Maric is found in one of the side rooms deep within the southeastern wing of the zone. Go past the sleeping Cazic Thule and into the halls with the centipedes and scorpions. If standing in the same room as Morzad Earthcaller, Galo is through the door on the east side of that room. There are a few mobs around him you may want to clear before proceeding with this step.
You say, 'Hail, Galo Maric'
Galo Maric says 'You should have stayed out of this! '
Galo respawns as Angry Galo and attacks. He hits for a max ~6,500.
Defeat Galo Maric 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Your task 'Morzad Chronicle - Never Trust a Morzad' has been updated.
Angry Galo Maric has been slain by _____!
Speak to Galo Maric 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, Galo Maric'
Galo Maric says 'You are still a fool! That crazy demon has been using you against us. Appropriate, since we have been using you against him! You tell Abbit. Tell him that he will never get the book.'
Return to Abbit Scribbletch 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'This is getting crazy! Let me sort this out. We will talk in a bit. Thank you for your help so far.'
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.

# Morzad Chronicle - The Morzad Confrontation (#5)

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 125

**Group Size:** Solo

This task is the fifth in a series of five that begins with Abbit Scribbletch, a gnome found in the first room on the left immediately on entering the northeastern wing of the House of Thule's Upper Floors.
You must have completed his first four tasks prior to being able to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'Will this never end?! It appears that I am being held here. The Morzad are trying to keep me from leaving. They are using a powerful holding [spell] that is making it impossible for me to leave. '
You say, 'A spell?'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'Yes. There is a Morzad caster in each of the four wings who must be killed. They are essentially acting as generators for the spell. As long as they live, I am stuck. Will you [kill] them?'
You say, 'I'll kill them'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'The sooner the better. Be careful.'
You have been assigned the task 'Morzad Chronicle - The Morzad Confrontation'.
Now that Abbit has Argan's Memoir, the Morzad are not allowing him to leave. Powerful casters are holding him here. Abbit has asked you to deal with them.
Kill the Morzad Firecaster 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Kill the Morzad Watercaster 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Kill the Morzad Earthcaster 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Kill the Morzad Aircaster 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
The Firecaster spawns in the northwestern wing (same room as Khalis).
The Watercaster spawns in the northeastern wing (same room as the Agent of Morzad in the second task).
The Earthcaster spawns in the southeastern wing (outside the room with Galo Maric).
The Aircaster spawns in the southwestern-most room of the southwestern wing.
These mobs, by themselves, are simple enough. However, they are each found in areas with many other mobs around them. Note: They are not social with the general population of the zone, so you can pull them through other mobs without adds.
Speak to Abbit Scribbletch 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Task Window Says: Tell Abbit that the spellcasters have been defeated and that the way should be open for him.
You say, 'Hail, Abbit Scribbletch'
Abbit Scribbletch says 'I can feel my power beginning to return! Thank you. But, unfortunately, I cannot have any witnesses to the fact that I have the book.'
Abbit respawns as "Deceiver Abbit" and attacks. He hits for a max ~7,200 and has a fair amount of hitpoints.
Defeat Abbit Scribbletch 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Task Window Says: Having no further use for you, Abbit has decided that you must die! You must defeat him.
Your task 'Morzad Chronicle - The Morzad Confrontation' has been updated.
Deceiver Abbit has been slain by _____!

# Redemption

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This group task (3-6 players) begins with Lilth near the western zone-in of the House of Thule's Upper Floors.
You must have completed Abbit's entire task arc (five tasks) before being able to request this mission.
Note: The Well mission "What Lies Below" used to be required in order to request this task. A patch on December 8, 2010, eliminated this requirement.
You say, 'Hail, Lilth'
Lilth says 'I have been watching you for some time. You have caused a fair amount of mayhem on the lower floor of the house. You are so foolish. You don't even realize what your destruction can do. Your [kind] is on track to be responsible for the annihilation of all dreams.'
You say, 'What do you mean, my kind?'
Lilth says 'You and your adventuring ilk. You come here uninvited and cause as much damage as you can in the hopes of finding a gold trinket or two. You do not know the [importance] of this house.'
You say, 'What importance?'
Lilth says 'This house is a nexus of multiple dream and nightmare realms. Destroying it would destroy dreams entirely. I would hope you would not want to be responsible for such a horrible act. It is not too late though. There is still time for you to [redeem] yourself.'
You say, 'How can I redeem myself?'
Lilth says 'There are some small groups of adventurers somewhere in the house who threaten the delicate balance this house creates between the dream and nightmare realms. They must be stopped. Should they succeed, the lives of all mortals will no longer be worth living. What is life without dreams? [Stop] them, and the house shall reward your efforts.'
You say, 'I'll stop them...'
Lilth says 'Very good. I knew you would listen to reason. When you are ready to start, just let me [know] and I will send you on your way.'
You have been assigned the task 'Redemption'.
Lilth has informed you that your adventures in the House of Thule are threatening to destroy both the dream and nightmare realms. She asks you to redeem yourself by killing a group of adventurers that are causing havoc in the house.
Vlania and Grubog are found in the northwestern wing in the rotdog room.
Frugvi and Balni are found in the southwestern wing in the room with the ice water. Note: You will receive the achievement "An Icy Death" for killing these two in the water.
Jalpo and Hiliss are found in the northeastern wing outside the Morzad Agent's room, below the staircase to Morell's Castle.
Each named hits for a max ~12,000 and has about 1.2 million hitpoints. They are not KOS and are not social with other mob types in the zone. They are immune to stun, mez, and root/snare. Their aggro isn't linked, so they can be split from one another (they do, however, have large assist ranges).
NOTE: The task locks upon the death of the sixth named you kill.
Find Grubog Skullsmasher on the second floor of the House of Thule and destroy him 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Grubog Skullsmasher says 'Me bash you in face!'
Grubog Skullsmasher has been slain by _____!

Entered: Tue Nov 9 22:07:02 2010
Modified: Tue Nov 9 22:12:36 2010 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Andre MacDonnal in the House of Thule's Upper Floors (located at the eastern zone-in)._
You say, 'Hail, Andre MacDonnal'
Andre MacDonnal sleepily looks up. 'Hail and well met, \_\_\_\_\_. I've been trying to get back to the waking world through sleep, but it doesn't seem to help. At least I believe I hit the [first step] by being aware that I'm dreaming, wouldn't you say?'
You say, 'What first step?'
Andre MacDonnal nods. 'You know, I'd get out of here before you're stuck, too. I'm pretty sure the denizens about would be detrimental to you in the waking world should they find you here. Death here may very well be permanant. I almost wonder if some of the [denizens] here may be the result of that exact thing.'
You say, 'What denizens?'
Andre MacDonnal says 'Well, many of the undead share similarities with the spirit world, so I'm unsure if they were trapped here through death, or if they are too stuck in a dream state. In that respect, would you mind doing [something] for me?'
You say, 'What something would that be?'
Andre MacDonnal says 'Would you [engage] some of the undead in battle and report to me on their behavior? I am curious as to what their true manifestation is, and any behavioral analysis you can give will help this old shaman.'
You say, 'I'll engage them...'
Andre MacDonnal nods approvingly, a smile flashing. 'Many thanks, \_\_\_\_\_. Report back to me soon, and good luck.'
You have been assigned the task 'Spirit Bound'.
The shaman Andre MacDonnal is aware he's in a dream state that he's attempting to shake. However, in the meantime, he's not quite sure the state of the spiritual manifestations on the second floor. Curious, he's asked for information on how they behave in battle and death, seeking your assistance.

You say, 'Hail, Andre MacDonnal'
Andre MacDonnal looks up in worry. 'You mean to say that they appear to have become trapped through their dreams and nightmares and that is why they wander as spirits so? This is troubling and makes our predicament even more precarious. ...Thank you for looking into it.'

# I Was Crazy Once

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is a solo kill task that begins with a listless madwoman in the House of Thule's Upper Floors (located in the first hallway of the southeastern wing).
You say, 'Hail, a listless madwoman'
a listless madwoman looks crazily back at you, some sort of twisted smile upon her face.  'Ha ha ha!  The blood!  The [blood] is in my head.  If I cut myself, I bleed it out!'
You say, 'Blood?'
a listless madwoman says 'The blood is in the house.  Isn't it pretty?  Preeeetty.  Almost like a [rotdog], wouldn't you think?  So pretty...'
You say, 'A rotdog?'
a listless madwoman says 'It is in their heads, it's in your head, the blood.  [Bring] me it.  Bring me lots?  I'm not [crazy].  Craaazy blood.'
You say, 'You're crazy'
a listless madwoman another grin exposes her sharpened teeth. 'What if it is you who is crazy?  Awake, asleep, bleeding with your brains.  How are you here?  You are the madman.  You have to [experience madness].  Experience it deep.
You say, 'I'll experience madness...'
a listless madwoman says 'Find what you think is crazy, you crazy!  Find it, and it may not be so crazy, crazy, until you find yourself some happy madness.  Pretty in my head.'
You have been assigned the task 'I Was Crazy Once'.
While difficult to understand what exactly she wants, it's gathered that a listless madwoman actually wants you to experience madness by finding others in the second floor of the house that are mad themselves. Other madmen, flying monkeys, and raging beasts should suffice.
Kill a few flying monkeys 0/6 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Kill a few raging beasts 0/3 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
Kill a gibbering madman 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
The above mobs are found in the northeastern wing of the zone (flying monkeys are found around the bridge and outside Morell's Castle while raging beasts and a gibbering madman spawn in the room south of the winding staircase that leads to Morell's Castle). Note: There is also a room with gibbering madmen in the furthest room in the southeastern wing.
Let the madwoman know you found madness 0/1 (House of Thule, Upper Floors)
You say, 'Hail, a listless madwoman'
A listless madwoman exclaims, 'Futility! Oh, you'll never know now.  Or you'll know soon.'  However, she thanks you and goes back to herself.
Experience (around 1 AA worth at Level 90)

Entered: Wed Nov 10 06:49:32 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task is the first in a series of three that will grant you access to the different islands in Al'Kabor's Nightmare (as well as lead into the group progression mission for the zone)._
_This task begins with Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip who's located at the zone-in._
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip sighs and kicks at the ground. 'Nothing matters anymore. I should have been grateful for what I had. If I had known this would happen, I wouldn't have wasted my life with stealing stuff. Now, there isn't anything to do but wait to [die], or chat up goblins!' Dreezil rolls his eyes. 'So, did you want something, or were you just [passing] through?'
You say, 'Die?'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip scoffs at your sudden interest, 'Why does a goblin like you care, anyway? Rivervale is in ruins, the Misty Thicket is no more. It's the end of the world! We're out of [supplies], most of the halflings have turned to backstabbing and thievery, and the Runnyeye clan has got us all dancing on the edge of a blade.
You say, 'Supplies?'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip says 'There are stores of food near the wall on the opposite side of this 'island,' but we can't even get to them thanks to the giant animals and the more hostile goblins. You're welcome to [try], but you're wasting your time!'
You say, 'I will try...'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip says 'Good luck then. I guess I won't be seeing you again. If you can knock some sense into some of those halflings that are causing even more problems before you get yourself killed, I'd appreciate it.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Rivervale Ruins'.
You've been asked to stop the Klaknak beetles and halfling bandits from causing trouble and stealing all the food and medicine.

---

Kill klaknak beetles 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Subdue halfling bandits 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)

106, 946
Talk to Dreezil 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip appears shocked and quite grateful. 'I didn't think you'd come back. Thank you. You know, I think you are right. We might be in a bad way, but we aren't all dead yet. Maybe there is hope. Could you talk to Dabner? Maybe you can help him, too.'
Convince Dabner not to give up 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Dabner Drednever is found a little ways east of the wall inside some ruins at 110, 360, -30._
You say, 'Hail, Dabner Drednever'
Dabner Drednever looks up as you inform him of your success in driving back the goblins and securing food and medicine. 'Really? You've done a lot to help us. I may never get over what has happened here with Al'Kabor's magic, but I owe it to my friends that are still alive that I push on. You have my deepest appreciation.'
Return to Dreezil 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip'
Dreezil Stickens Pocketdip manages a slight smile. 'I think you've reminded Dabner and I what courage and hope is all about. I won't wallow in the past any longer. I'll do what I can to make something better of this mess! Listen, if you by any chance get to what remains of Everfrost, I've heard Dagda Icefury needs some help. I have a little magical trinket here that might be able to get you there. If you do go, be sure to dress warmly. It's still cold as ever!'
You've helped give the halflings a second chance and given Dreezil and Dabner some peace of mind. You can now go to the wastelands of Everfrost whenever you wish.
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.
_Rewards:_
315 platinum
Faction with Dream Delvers

30 Dream Motes
_Note: You also gain access to the second island in the zone (Everfrost). To get there, say "Everfrost" to Dreezil._

You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says haggardly, 'Things are bad. I won't deny that. But I won't stop fighting until I'm [dead]. I'll protect everyone until I draw my last breathe! You there. Are you here to lend a hand, or are you just [passing] through?

You have been assigned the task 'Wastelands of Everfrost'.
The remaining barbarians on Everfrost are out of food, weakening their efforts to defend against the orcs and goblins that swarm the landscape. Dagda needs your help to gather some mammoth meat to feed her people, and drive off the snow orcs and ice goblins that threaten the barbarians.

You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says 'Excellent work, my friend. You've brought us enough food for awhile. And your heroism in defeating those orcs and goblins will not be forgotten. Could you spread the good news to my people? I'll wait here for your return.'
Convince the barbarian refugees not to give up 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_You can simply hail the refugee that stands nearby._
You say, 'Hail, a barbarian refugee'
a barbarian refugee says 'We've heard of what you've done for us! You have our thanks. We won't ever surrender. If we are to die, we will take the lot of them with us!'
Speak to Dagda 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says 'You've given us the respite we needed, and you have our gratitude. We'll continue the fight here. The world may be shattered, but nothing shall shatter our spirit! I will die protecting my friends if need be. \_\_\_\_\_, I know you are needed elsewhere. I can arrange a guide to take you over the remains of this world and transport you to Faydark, if you so desire. We will be fine here.'
You've given Dagda and her people the reprieve they need. Their struggles will no longer be in vain. You can now travel to the remains of Faydark.
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Faction with Dream Delvers
Experience
20 Dream Motes
_Note: You also gain access to the third island in the zone (Faydark). To get there, say "Faydark" to Dagda or Dreezil._

Entered: Wed Nov 10 06:49:40 2010
Modified: Wed Nov 10 07:15:12 2010 | | _This solo task is the third in a series of three that will grant you access to the different islands in Al'Kabor's Nightmare (as well as lead into the group progression mission for the zone)._
_This task begins with Sionachie Heartsinger who's located at the port-up to the Faydark._
You say, 'Hail, Sionachie Heartsinger'
Sionachie Heartsinger seems to look right through you as she speaks, 'Thubr is gone, and now Firiona Vie is [unwell]. I never thought I'd be here to see the world end like this. Al'Kabor, what happened?' With a few heavy blinks, Sionachie refocuses her attention. 'Pardon me, are you here to help? Or are you just [passing] through?
You say, 'Unwell?'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'The situation could not be worse. I cannot understand Firiona's actions, and the efforts of what remains of Felwithe and Kelethin are not stopping the Crushbone orcs.' Sionachie peers at you with silent understanding. 'You are not what you seem. Others may be fooled by this clever ruse, but I sense you are not my enemy. If you are offering your [help], we could use it.''
You say, 'I will help'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'Thank you. I've been warned by the Felwithe guard to stay away from Firiona. They claim she has no wish to see me. Their actions are desperate and corrupt. If they are allowed to continue on without any accountability for their actions, who knows what kind of irreparable damage they may do. I need you to lessen the Felwithe guard numbers, and retrieve any military documents you may find while doing so. Do you think you can handle this?'
You have been assigned the task 'Faydark's Shadow'.
Sionachie Heartsinger has asked you to speak to Firiona Vie. Firiona has refused to meet with Sionachie and has asked she be denied access to Felwithe. Sionachie has asked for your help while she takes care of the Crushbone orc assaults. Investigate the ruins of Felwithe and Kelethin and look for any documents that might shed light on Firiona's plans.
The documents have likely been littered around Al'Kabor's Nightmare, in the ruins of Kelethin and Felwithe, or they may still be in the hand of those that guard what's left of Felwithe.

---

Investigate the ruins of Felwithe 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Investigate the ruins of Kelethin 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_These steps update as you approach the ruins of Felwithe and, to the west, the ruins of Kelethin._
Collect Felwithe Military Documents and deliver them to Sionachie 0/5 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Military documents drop from the high elves in the area and are also groundspawns. Known locations (in the ruins behind the main area of Felwithe):_
_In-game /loc:_
-265, -5080, -334
-330, -5070, -333
-550, -5120, -333
-445, -5120, -335
-300, -5220, -333
_For alkabormare.txt map file:_
P 5080.0000, 265.0000, -334.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, document
P 5070.0000, 330.0000, -333.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, document
P 5120.0000, 550.0000, -333.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, document
P 5120.0000, 445.0000, -335.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, document
P 5220.0000, 300.0000, -333.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, document
Eliminate members of the Felwithe guard 0/4 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Kill four of the high elves in and around Felwithe._
Speak with Sionachie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Sionachie Heartsinger'
Sionachie Heartsinger looks over the documents, frowning. 'I see. Firiona is even worse off than I thought. Apparently those who hold the reigns of power in Felwithe are using her as nothing more than a figurehead. Could you do me a favor? I have a potion here that may help her regain part of her memory. When you ready, please take it and give it to her."
Give the Memory Tonic to Firiona Vie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Firiona Vie is found inside a building just inside the front gate of Felwithe at location -400, -5000. You may need to clear a mob or two to get to her._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie says 'Oh, hello there. I don't think I've seen you before. Can you tell me where I might find my friends? I have a feeling I should be doing something useful, but I've been having trouble remembering lately.'
_On handing her the potion:_
Firiona Vie looks slightly more lucid after consuming the potion. 'Sionachie, of course. Yes, I remember her. Tell her she can come speak to me. We have much to do. Something about Al'Kabor. Do I know him?' Firiona pauses. While the magical tonic helped, she is still in no condition to do much of anything.
Speak with Sionachie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Sionachie Heartsinger'
Sionachie Heartsinger listens as you describe what happened. 'So that is that,' she says with a sigh. 'Well, I appreciate what you've done. Her slight improvement may be all I need to start making a real difference here. Maybe Felwithe will listen to me now. I am grateful for your help. If you would like to go, I can give you access to the Dreadlands where Al'Kabor is held captive. You need but ask."
Firiona appears to have lost her grip on sanity, though you've given her enough clarity to recognize her friend. She is now a figurehead for what remains of the elven fighting force, but nothing more. Sionachie will try to help her friend. You've done all you can here. You can now go to the Dreadlands where Al'Kabor resides.
Your faction standing with Dream Delvers got better.
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Faction with Dream Delvers
Experience
20 Dream Motes
_Note: You also gain access to the final island in the zone (Dreadlands). To get there, say "Dreadlands" to Sionachie, Dagda, or Dreezil. You can travel to any island by speaking to one of these NPCs (or to Al'Kabor on the Dreadlands island). You can also say "leave" to zone back out into the Upper Floors of the House of Thule._

---

_Completion of this task allows you to request the group progression mission for Al'Kabor's Nightmare (from Elard Velsorn outside the zone-in in the House of Thule's Upper Floors)._
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This shared task (1-6 players) begins with Gimble Shamrook in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. He's found in a ruined hut a little ways east of the wall (in the same spot as Dabner Drednever from the "The Rivervale Ruins" task)._
You say, 'Hail, Gimble Shamrook'
Gimble Shamrook looks you over with curious suspicion before he speaks, 'I have a quick and keen eye, you know, and I can tell with one glance that you don't belong here. If you're attempting to retrieve [relics], then you'd better be warned that I am one step ahead of you!'
You say, 'What relics?'
Gimble Shamrook says 'I can sense your shenanigans, oh yes. I see that you're trying to steal my brilliant idea! While these islands may be a figment of someone's imagination, they still contain all the same [treasures] the original lands hold.'
You say, 'Treasures?'
Gimble Shamrook says 'Oh, such wondrous treasures! Sure, they may be rusty and ruined, but if I shine 'em up a bit, no one will ever know the difference. The simpletons of Norrath will pay me a hefty sum for my faux relics! I just need to find a way to travel to the other [islands]...'
You say, 'The other islands?'
Gimble Shamrook says 'There are several other islands in this space, and I'm positive there must be a way to travel between them. Since we both seem to have the same goal, we might as well work together. If you can help me [recover] these relics, I'll make sure it's worth your effort.'
You say, 'I'll help you recover them'
Gimble Shamrook says 'Splendid! I'll give you whatever information I have pertaining to the relic's whereabouts. Happy treasure hunting!'
You have been assigned the task 'Ruined Relic Recovery'.
You have partnered up with Gimble Shamrook to recover relics from the surrounding islands in order to earn a hefty profit!

---

_The four "Speak to..." and four "Deliver..." steps are open from the start of the task, so you can handle the islands in any order you'd like._
Speak to Snugum Witgone 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Snugum Witgone is found within the wall on the Rivervale isle._
You say, 'Hail, Snugum Witgone'
Snugum Witgone clears his throat before responding, 'You're here for the beads? Boss must have sent you. Boss knows he can count on me! I hid the beads in the most secret of hiding spots. No one will ever [find] them, not even me!'
You say, 'How can I find them?'
Snugum Witgone says 'You use a magnetic metal detector to find the beads, but that fat-fingered Hilnie stole it! You'll take it from her and find the beads? And not tell Boss that I hid them so good?'
Recover the Magnetic Metal Detector from Hilnie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Hilnie spawns in the north-central halfling bandit camp. Her difficulty is on par with the rest of the mobs on the island (hits for a max ~7,000)._
Hilnie has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Hilnie's corpse crumples to the floor.
Find the spot where the beads are buried 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_The beads are buried in the southeastern part of the island at approximately -270, -400, -40._
Use the Magnetic Metal Detector to retrieve the warbeads 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
The metal detector makes a loud clanking noise as it pulls Ruined Runnyeye Warbeads from the ground.
Speak to Helda Varesh 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Helda Varesh is found in a building in the northeastern part of the Everfrost Wastelands._
You say, 'Hail, Helda Varesh'
Helda Varesh says 'Greetings, \_\_\_\_\_. You're looking for the cloak of an ice bear? What an odd request. It's so old and tattered. If you insist, I can help restore it to a suitable condition, but I'll need a pelt from the elusive ice bear. I can help you lure it out of hiding with my delectable goblin goulash, but you'll need to collect some tender goblin meat for me first.'
Loot Tender Goblin Meat from ice goblins 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_This is a common drop from ice goblins on the isle._
Give the Tender Goblin Meat to Helda Varesh 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Helda Varesh says 'Doesn't it smell delicious? Head right outside of this building, and use the goulash to attract an ice bear.'
Use the goulash to lure an ice bear and take its pelt 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Right-click the Goblin Goulash while standing in front of Helda's building to spawn "an ice bear" (on par with difficulty of the mobs on the isle)._
A ferocious roar rumbles behind Helda's ice hut.
an ice bear has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
an ice bear's corpse falls to the ground with a loud crash.
Give the Pristine Ice Bear Pelt to Helda Varesh 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Helda Varesh says 'This should do it. The cloak won't provide much protection, but it's yours nonetheless.'
Speak to Ogrol 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Ogrol spawns in a set of abandonned huts in the central part of the Faydark island._
You say, 'Hail, Ogrol'
Ogrol says 'You want a robe? What for? You're not going to wear it, are you? Never mind, I don't want to know... I have no use for a robe, but since you seem to want it so badly, I'm sure you're willing to trade far more than what it's worth! Bring me a fragment from the Staff of Discord, and the robe is yours to do whatever you want with.'
Kill the Priest of Discord and loot a fragment of his staff 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_You'll find the Priest of Discord a little ways west of Ogrol's location, beneath the ruins of Kelethin. He has two guards with him (they are mezzable), but can be split away from them. Fight difficulty is on par with the rest of the isle's mob population._
Priest of Discord has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Priest of Discord's corpse lets out a gasp before falling silent.
Give the Staff Fragment to Ogrol 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Ogrol says 'The ruined robe is all yours. Just don't try squeezing it on over that fat head of yours, you hear?'
Speak to Zisak 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Zisak spawns in the northeastern pyramid on the Dreadlands isle._
You say, 'Hail, Zisak'
Zisak says 'All of our weapons have deteriorated, much like our home here. I will do my best to repair the hilt of the wraith bone hammer if you bring me a large chunk of bone from one of the plaguebone skeletons that linger around here.'
Loot a Large Chunk of Bone 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_This item drops from plaguebones on the Dreadlands isle._
Give the Large Chunk of Bone to Zisak 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Zisak says 'I do not believe it will hold up in battle, but regardless, here is the hammer you requested.'
Deliver Ruined Runnyeye Warbeads to Gimble 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Deliver a Ruined Cloak of the Ice Bear to Gimble 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Deliver a Ruined Robe of Discord to Gimble 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Deliver a Ruined Wraith Bone Hammer to Gimble 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Speak to Gimble Shamrook 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Gimble Shamrook'
Gimble Shamrook says 'Brilliant job, \_\_\_\_\_! These relics should make me filthy rich! Oh... um, I mean they'll make us rich, of course.'
You collected numerous ruined relics for Gimble Shamrook and received what you hope is half of the profits...

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a solo task that begins with Serrlis in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. He's found in the southeastern pyramid on the Dreadlands isle._
You say, 'Hail, Serllis'
Serllis says 'If you have come to raid our house, I'm afraid you've picked a poor target. Any items of value were ruined when Al'Kabor destroyed our home. Resources are scarce, and what little we have is used to nourish those who are [ill].'
You say, 'How did they become ill?'
Serllis says 'The lands weren't the only thing devastated when Al'Kabor lost his grip, many iksar have suffered as well. Of the few that remain, several have fallen gravely ill. Viskal, our apothecary, has been working diligently to find a [cure], but hasn't had much luck.'
You say, 'Find a cure?'
Serllis says 'I'm curious as to why you have such an interest? Do you intend to [help] us, rather than harm us? Viskal hasn't been heard from in days. He's been conducting his research in the temple just west of here.'
You say, 'I intend to help...'
Serllis says 'Could you check-in with Viskal and see what progress he has made?'
You have been assigned the task 'A Struggle to Survive'.
Serllis is surprised you are interested in the iksar who have fallen ill, but humbly accepts your help anyway.

---

Find Viskal the Apothecary 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Viskal is found in the next pyramid over. He's not in very good shape._
Kill the iksar torturers 0/2 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Kill Interrogator Silth 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_These three mobs surround Viskal's corpse. The torturers each hit for a max ~3,100 while Silth hits for a max ~6,500._
Interrogator Silth has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Interrogator Silth's corpse falls to the ground with a loud crash.
Loot Viskal's Research 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Deliver Viskal's Research to Serllis 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Serllis says 'It's a tragedy that Al'Kabor's errors have caused my fellow iksar to turn on each other. Our survival comes at a great cost. Although we have suffered a great loss with the passing of Viskal, his death will not be in vain. It appears that he concluded his research, and found a possible cure, but I'll need a few ingredients before I'm able to craft the antidote.'
Collect Nettlevine 0/4 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_These are ground spawns within the spire compound where plantation grows._
Loot Drachnid Venom 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_This is a common drop from drachnids._
Give the Nettlevine to Serllis 0/4 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Give the Drachnid Venom to Serllis 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Serllis says 'Your help so far has been invaluable, but it seems the last ingredient I need to concoct a cure will be difficult to obtain. The apothecary's research calls for yeti blood, but the only known supply lies in the hands of the iksars that murdered Viskal. I know they will not hear my appeals, but they might be willing to barter with you.'
Speak to Zisak 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Zisak is in the northeastern-most structure._
You say, 'Hail, Zisak'
Zisak says 'Who has sent you? It is Serllis, is it not? Why should I help her when she so blatantly steals from our meager food supply! My people suffer, too, from sickness and starvation. Serllis will never have her cure if our rations keep disappearing.'
Speak to Serllis 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Serllis'
Serllis says 'So Zisak blames us for his dwindling food supply? That is absurd! My people are too sick and weak to steal anything. Someone else must be behind this heartless theft. Is it too much to ask for you to sneak into the food supply room to the northwest and find out who is responsible? Maybe if we can correct the situation for Zisak, he'll be willing to part with the yeti blood.'
Head to the store room to investigate 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_This is the northwestern-most structure. You'll be jumped by 2x "a drolvarg thief" which hit for a max ~3,300 and have relatively few hitpoints._
Kill the drolvarg thieves 0/2 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
a drolvarg thief has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a drolvarg thief's corpse crumples to the floor.
Loot a Meager Ration 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Deliver the Meager Rations to Zisak 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Zisak says 'Serllis' kindness shall be repaid with kindness. Please see to it that she receives this vial of yeti blood, along with my gratitude.'
Deliver the Vial of Yeti Blood to Serllis 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Serllis says 'Many thanks, friend. You bring us hope; and perhaps a cure for the many that have fallen ill.'
Thanks to your efforts, an antidote can be created to cure the many iksar who have fallen ill in the Dreadland ruins.

_You must have completed the Al'Kabor tasks "The Rivervale Ruins", "Wastelands of Everfrost", and "Faydark's Shadow" in order to request this task._
_Note: The Well mission "What Lies Below" also used to be required. However, a patch on December 8, 2010, eliminated this requirement._
You say, 'Hail, Elard Velsorn'
Elard Velsorn says 'You traverse these halls with ease and purpose, unlike many of the denizens I see wandering about mindlessly. Tell me, does the house not hold [power] over you?'
You say, 'Power over me?'
Elard Velsorn says 'It seems some of the most powerful [beings] have been held captive by their dreams while here in this house. It's a curious thing, to be certain.'
You say, 'What beings?'
Elard Velsorn says 'I came here, trailing the infamous wizard, Al`Kabor. I've always had my suspicions about his abilities, but the fact that the house chose to ensnare him in [sleep] must mean there is more to him than he lets on.'
You say, 'His sleep?'
Elard Velsorn says 'He rests, rather tumultuously. I'm immensely curious what plagues his mind. Those who are able to [enter] his dreams might gain insight into the true nature of Al`kabor and see a side that he seems to keep hidden from the waking world.'
You say, 'I'll enter the dreams...'
Elard Velsorn says '[Proceed] with caution and a keen eye, \_\_\_\_\_.'
You have been assigned the task 'Catastrophic Power'.
You have entered the dreams of the infamous wizard, Al'Kabor, and have been tasked with discovering what troubles him in his sleep.
_Say "proceed" to zone into the instance._

---

_Mobs in this task hit for a max ~7,000 and are all mezzable. All but the ice giants are stunnable. None are linked to each other, so they can all be split. None see invisibility, so you can set up wherever you'd like._
Investigate the source of the screams 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Oh, please! Will someone help get these critters away from me?!
Shoo, pests! Shoo!
_A little ways east from the zone-in, you'll find Dreezil in a ruined hut with three angry snakes nearby._
Defend Dreezil from the angry snakes 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Defend Dreezil from the angry beetles 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Note: Once the snakes are dead, someone has to move to Dreezil's location in order to activate the beetles. The same goes for the wasps in the next step._
Defend Dreezil from the angry wasps 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Speak to Dreezil 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip'
Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip says 'Well hello there, \_\_\_\_\_. Welcome to the end of the world, and many thanks for saving my hide. I'm not sure how long I would have made it if you hadn't of shown up. Of course I've always said that as long as you're alive there's no point in giving up. You can stop [fighting back] once you're dead.'
You say, 'Fighting back?'
Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip says 'Ah, well, not much that I can see to do to make things better. I mean, heck, everything just blew up.' The halfling's voice changes slightly for a moment, 'I'm too ignorant and stupid to know when I'm in trouble over my diminutive head.' Then he continues in his normal voice, not missing a beat or appearing to notice what just happened. 'I suppose there's enough food for now, though the deputies are having trouble. I just wish I knew [what happened].'
You say, 'What happened?'
Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip says 'I really don't know. I can't even remember anything before this happened. Whatever it was must have caused me to black out, and I woke up locked up in the wall guard tower. All the guards I talked to remember, or, well, don't remember it the same way. It's like the whole world shut off and restarted with a huge [explosion].'
You say, 'An explosion?'
Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip says 'Shattered the world, I figure. The real problem is that the only place that I can see that might have something tasty to drink is floating just out of reach. I suppose we could try [jumpin'] over to the next hunk of rock. Looks a lot like Everfrost to me. But then I'm not a fan of water, especially the frozen kind.'
You say, 'Jumpin'?'
Dreezil Stickens, Pocketdip says 'I was just kidding! Don't do that, you'd never make it. I know a trick, though. If you really need to get over there, all you have to do is let me know and I'll help you get to the chunk of all that's left of [Everfrost]. But I can't help feeling that there's something missing, something I need to do, or need you to do.'
_Say "Everfrost" to him to port over._
Seek out Dagda Icefury on the Everfrost island 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Dagda Icefury is found outside a building in the northeastern part of the island._
You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says 'Hello. Move aside, I need a clear field of vision. It's ridiculous that all these icy jerks survived the end of the world and so few of my kin did. I can't hold this place forever. Sooner or later I'll fall, and so will my [friends]. I can't allow that.'
You say, 'Your friends?'
Dagda Icefury says 'Ognit appears to have been injured in the... the whatever it was. Dabner thinks that he's physically fine, but that he's just being stubborn and refusing to regain consciousness. That seems crazy to me, but then Dabner's the healer, not me. I'm the [killer].'
You say, 'The killer?'
Dagda Icefury says 'I guess it takes the end of everything to force me to recognize who I really am. I've spent my life killing people to protect people that I care about. How righteous and proud I was, thinking that my strength made me and mine worthy and other people not.' As she speaks her eyes glint for a moment and a shadow seems to pass across her face. 'All I have left now are these two. Trouble makers for sure, but friends nonetheless. If we had [Ognit] we might be able to survive the next assault. There can't be an infinite number of those Marr-cursed giants.'
You say, 'Ognit?'
Dagda Icefury says 'Aye, Ognit. He doesn't look like much, but he's a very powerful wizard. But then, perhaps you could help. It seems to me that you have more reason to help me than to help the wretched ice giants. Stay here and help me defend my friends. Obviously I can't offer you any reward, other than my assistance in defeating them. After all, we're some of the last people alive, and those giants would kill you as readily as they would kill me and my friends. Will you [stay] and help defend them?'
You say, 'I'll stay'
Dagda Icefury says 'Thank you. I doubt it will be long before they decide to attack us, so prepare yourselves. I'll stay here and guard Dabner and Ognit, if you can fend off the ice giants.'
_The nearby ice giants (previously unaggro) all converge on the tower. There are six of them. They're mezzable, but not stunnable, and can be split from one another._
Defend Dagda and her friends from the ice giants 0/6 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Speak to Dagda 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says 'Thank you for your help. I don't know if you understand how important it is for me to have friends here at the end of all things. It's almost like I'd be hollow without them. Perhaps now with the giants dead we can think about helping those left. I've seen movement on the green chunk of land, but can't tell who or what it was. It certainly looks like [Kelethin] from here, though.'
You say, 'Kelethin?'
Dagda Icefury says 'I never had the sharpest eyes, but I'm fairly certain the rubble to the east is from Kelethin. I can't leave here until Ognit is better, but maybe Dabner can get you there to help out any survivors.'
Speak to Dabner 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Dabner and Ognit are inside the building._
You say, 'Hail, Dabner Drednever'
Dabner Drednever says 'Hello. I'm sorry for being rude earlier, but you've saved Dagda's life, and I appreciate that. Certainly she would have gotten herself killed if you weren't here. She was talking about hiding us someplace and going out to distract the ice giants. Some fool idea about making a defensive stand at one of the edges and making it look like we fell off before she dies fighting them. Foolish. I think that girl would be happiest if she could die saving someone, the only trouble is that she's too skilled to die and too worried that we wouldn't be able to get along without her. And we probably wouldn't. No doubt [Ognit] here would have met his fate a long time ago.'
You say, 'Ognit?'
Dabner Drednever says 'The great Ognit Eznertob. Bah. He thinks himself the greatest wizard ever, and the greatest tinker. But he's blown up more copper contraptions than I could count. Oh, no doubt he's powerful... why you should have seen the contraption he created once while trying to make a heater for these barbarians! No doubt if Dagda hadn't smashed the thing it would have killed half the population of Halas. But he's no Solist, no Porlo. He's certainly not as powerful as... Odd, I... Well, anyway, he's my best friend, so that's enough for me. If only he'd [listen] to me.'
You say, 'He won't listen to you?'
Dabner Drednever says 'He never listens to me, but now he really needs to. He's in there trying to will himself dead. I don't know why, but I'm sure that's what's going on. It's almost as if... No, it can't be.' Ognit turns to Dabner with a thoughtful expression on his face. 'You always did have a magnificently powerful ability to overestimate yourself.''
Dabner Drednever shouts, 'You are not responsible for this! Ognit, you fool, you DID NOT blow up the world! This is not your fault!'
Ognit Eznertob stirs a bit and begins to murmur, 'Exploded. Everything exploded. Must have been me, only me.' Then in a stronger, oddly accented voice, 'I've always been foolish and free with my power. My irresponsibility was dangerous and stupid.' Immediately after speaking, Ognit falls still once again.
Dabner Drednever says 'He obviously isn't going to listen, but I'll get through eventually. Let me see if I can get you to [Kelethin].'
Your task 'Catastrophic Power' has been updated.
You see, 'Get me to Kelethin...'
Dabner Drednever says 'Good luck, and thank you.'
Seek out Firiona Vie on the Faydark island 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Firiona Vie is found in the southeastern part of the island, overlooking the Dreadlands._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie says 'Hello, \_\_\_\_\_. I am glad to find others alive in this horrible world. As I stare out over the edge of existence I wonder [how] it is that this came to be. I doubt that I'll ever know, and I'm afraid all hope has been lost.'
You say, 'How?'
Firiona Vie says 'The only person that might know is Sionachie. She's very smart. I'm certain she could help us if only there weren't so many nasty Orcs trying to kill us.'
Speak to Sionachie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Sionachie is found within the ruins of Felwithe (in a tunnel just inside the front gate)._
You say, 'Hail, Sionachie Heartsinger'
Sionachie Heartsinger smiles wanly, 'Hello,' she shakes her head, 'it's hard to believe that we few are the last people alive. I mean, sure, it seems that someone would probably be here if we were not, but why us? I can't help but think, why am I alive? [What does it mean]?
You say, 'What does it mean?'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'I really don't know. But if Dreezil, Dagda and the others are still alive, along with the four of us here, that must mean something. Sure, we're all seasoned adventurers, but there were many seasoned adventurers.' Sionachie sighs deeply. 'I'd set off and seek answers, but I cannot leave Felwithe in such a state. [Orcs] have invaded her walls.''
You say, 'Orcs?'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'I suppose they struggle like us, lone survivors living in ruins, fighting for the last square mile of land. Firiona has given up the fight, but I will not let what's left of Felwithe fall. Together we can vanquish the Orcs, and find answers to the questions that plague my mind.'
Clear Felwithe's ruins of the orc invaders 0/6 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Orc invaders can be found wandering throughout Felwithe's ruins._
Return to Sionachie 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Sionachie Heartsinger'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'The ruins of Felwithe have been reclaimed; you have my thanks, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm afraid my [questions] still linger.'
You say, 'Questions?'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'Questions, always questions. Rarely answers. I suppose if I can live long enough I might figure it out. There's something nagging in the back of my head trying to tell me the answer, but I'm apparently not ready to hear it.' She tilts her head, 'You've been to several of the remaining chunks of the world. Perhaps if I sent you to the chunk that appears to be the remnants of the Dreadlands you will be able to find the [answer].'
You say, 'What's the answer?'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'I can't help but think that there is one. Dreadlands is certainly a place of power. I will send you there if you wish. I'm not at all certain how knowing why the world ended will help me accept it, but I'm sure it will. I can send you to the [Dreadlands] chunk whenever you are ready.'
You say, 'Send me to the Dreadlands'
Sionachie Heartsinger says 'Stay safe, \_\_\_\_\_. I will meet you in what remains of the Dreadlands as soon as I can, and together, we will uncover the truth.'
Confront Al'Kabor in the Dreadlands isle 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_NOTE: Sionachie despawns once you hail Al`Kabor (no one else will be able to port up to the Dreadlands isle)._
You say, 'Hail, Al`Kabor'
Al`Kabor says 'Who are you? You don't belong here. You should be dead like the rest of them. Go away. The world is over. You're dead. The world is dead. And I have finally achieved eternal life. The irony is not lost on me. So, what happens now? Do my 'companions' have anything else to say to me, or shall we just stare at each other for a while longer?'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'I see now, I finally understand. You think you blew up the world. You think that your great power is responsible for this. In your arrogance you've created this fantasy... no, this nightmare. The all-powerful Al'Kabor, learner of secrets and the greatest living wizard, and you needed your companions to show you the truth, to save you from your own fears.'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'It was Dreezil that always gave us hope, and hope is what you needed. You needed him to tell you that even though you made a terrible mistake, somehow it would be fine. You needed him to tell you that things would get better.'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'In Ognit and Dabner you saw a reflection of what you believed you had done. Those two were always getting in trouble, and yet they usually made it through unscathed. You used to call them fools, but never yourself. You thought that if those fools could recover from their mistakes, then so could you.'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'Dagda. Strong and faithful Dagda. She never gave up. She never let her friends down and was always our protector. But that wasn't what you needed from her, was it? No, you never needed help or protection. From her you needed the undying faith and love she had for those she called friends. You needed someone to love you despite what you'd done.'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'Firiona had nothing for you, did she? She was ever out of reach of your understanding. Her motivations never made sense to you, since nobility didn't exist in any part of you. So why Felwithe? Why Kelethin? Why were they not destroyed along with the rest of the world?'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'Of course, I see it now! It was me. You thought of me as a fellow seeker of knowledge. I was the only one that had a chance to understand something that you couldn't grasp. And of course the thing you could never understand was yourself. After all, how can you have a true nightmare without coming to understand some horrible truth about yourself?'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'Here's the truth of it. You've spent your life reaching for a goal that you believe should be in your reach and you've been frustrated. Others have done it, others with lesser power perhaps. But those were people that never had what you had. Each of them was willing to cast aside everything for their goal, but you can't. You have friends. Not just that, but you value them, despite your self-delusions to the contrary.'
Sionachie Heartsinger shouts 'Well, you've done it now. In this place you have allowed your friends to perish. Not by your actions but by your inaction. So now you have nothing. Nothing to gain and nothing to lose. Only now can you achieve your heart's desire. Now you may live forever. Into eternity you go, taking nothing with you.'
Al`Kabor shouts 'Aaaaarrrr!' The once living Erudite uncurls from the racking pain of his transformation and looks about. 'So, I am completely alone, am I? Of course, you are correct. And if you are correct, then you can't really be here either, Sionachie.''
Al`Kabor shouts 'Clever child, Sionachie. Of course, she was only clever because she was a product of my imagination, a manifestation of my power in her image. She claims that this is a dream or nightmare. I know this to be the truth. For decades I have withheld my true power. I've rightfully feared what might happen if I used all of my might, focused myself on one end. All of this came about because I grew weary of waiting for the perfect time and place to enact my plan to become immortal.'
Al`Kabor shouts 'And so I have destroyed Norrath. It seems that only you and I remain. Perhaps now I can teach a few of you my true magics? The petty magic that I have taught to others over the years pales in comparison to my real strength!'
Al`Kabor shouts 'Sionachie was terribly wrong. A nightmare this may be, but this is also reality. I have destroyed the world so that I might live forever. I wish it were not so, but it is. The proof lies all around you. The sadness in this all is that now that I've finally become immortal, I wish that I could die.'
Al`Kabor writhes with anguish as his gaze falls upon you.
_Al'Kabor attacks. He hits for a max (???) and casts a few AEs:_
Abolishing Flames: PB AE 500', Fire (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 9000 per tick
2: Increase Curse Counter by 50
Bone Chill: Single Target, Cold (-700)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 37000
2: Illusion: Skeleton
Defeat Al'Kabor 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Note: The task locks upon Al'Kabor's death._
Al`Kabor has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Al`Kabor's corpse falls to the ground, sending a surge of magical energy through the air.
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You have defeated the arrogant wizard, Al`Kabor, and proven to all that he is not quite as powerful as he boasts.

Modified: Thu Nov 18 18:00:52 2010 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Deputy Asler in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. He's located on the wall right at the zone-in (just climb up the log)._
You say, 'Hail, Deputy Asler'
Deputy Asler says 'Defense! Get on [defense]! We must protect what's left of the wall!'
You say, 'Defense against what?'
Deputy Asler says 'There's enough encroaching on our area. We must [protect] ourselves.'
You say, 'I'll protect you!'
Deputy Asler says 'Oh, please let you not be a trick of Bristlebane. Come back with your report when you've slaughtered the lot of them. I may be the only Deputy left in defense, but that's still an order!'
You have been assigned the task 'Defend What?'.
Deputy Asler is the only remaining Rivervale deputy guarding the wall, and as one of the first adventurers he's seen for days, he has ordered you to protect the wall from intruders. Intruders to which direction is unknown, but there seems to be a lot enemies around nonetheless.

---

Kill any Runnyeye fighters, raiders, or guards nearby 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Kill any giant rats nearby 0/3 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
Kill any klaknak beetles nearby 0/4 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_NOTE: The giant rats and klaknak beetles don't summon. However, the Runnyeye goblins do._
Report back to Deputy Asler 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
_Rewards:_
Experience
Minor Faction with Dream Delvers

Modified: Sun Oct 14 03:01:51 2012 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Chorra Clearsky in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. She's located in the northeast area of the Rivervale isle (close to the island's end at 450, -390)._
You say, 'Hail, Chorra Clearsky'
Chorra Clearsky says 'Greetings, adventurer! It's quite nice seeing a fresh face around here. It gives hope to our dire situation. Did you come for the [hunt]?'
You say, 'What hunt?'
Chorra Clearsky says 'Well, at the moment we're forced to make due with what we have right now for meat and supplies. There's a number of animals about that we can make do with. Unfortunately, we're down to, well... me... for hunting. It's so hard to [keep up]!'
You say, 'Keep up?'
Chorra Clearsky says 'You wouldn't mind helping, would you? All you have to do is the killing, and I can manage the skinning and gathering the essential supplies from the bodies. What do ya say? You'll [do it]?'
You say, 'I'll do it'
Chorra Clearsky says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_! You are so great. Thank you, thank you. Here's a list of the animals we require supplies from. I'll await your return.'
You have been assigned the task 'Aiding the Hunt'.
A young and oddly cheerful halfling Chorra Clearsky has requested assistance for hunting meat and supplies, particularly for the dirty part of the killing itself. She's supplied the list of creatures to go for, which has been scribbled down in your journal.

Return to Chorra 0/1 (Al'Kabor's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Chorra Clearsky'
Chorra Clearsky looks out over the field at the bodies before her, and slips her skinning knife out of its sheath. 'Well, lookie there! It looks like it's time to get to work. I appreciate the help, friend. Return to me later and I may have more for you to do.'

Modified: Fri Nov 12 07:38:21 2010 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Tresla McKindle in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. She's located in the building in the northeast corner of the Everfrost isle (485, -2700, -225)._
_Note: You must have completed the task "The Rivervale Ruins" in order to access the Everfrost island._
You say, 'Hail, Tresla McKindle'
Tresla McKindle says 'You don't look like you're in very good shape. But then again, I suppose we don't look so hot, either. There's so few of us left since... it happened. What is worse, what has survived outside these walls is strong; we barely can keep up with food and warmth. Since you're here, since you have survived outside in the remnants of Everfrost, could you do us a [favor]?'
You say, 'A favor?'
Tresla McKindle says 'A favor is all I can ask at the moment. No major quest of glory or honor, full of riches and powerful artifacts. Gone are those days... No, we just need some [pelts] to fashion armor, clothing, and bedding so that perhaps we can survive another night. We don't have much to offer in return...'
You say, 'I'll get some pelts'
Tresla McKindle says 'You're very gracious, friend. I await your return.'
You have been assigned the task 'EverWarmth'.
The Wayfarer Tresla McKindle is holed up in one of the remaining buildings of the Everfrost ruins, and has requested a favor of you to grab Thick Everfrost Pelts from the various animals of the tundra. Polar bears, mammoths, and snow wolves appear to have the proper thickness required.

This is a solo kill task that begins with Seria Woodwind in Al'Kabor's Nightmare. She's located just north of the port-up area on Faydark island (-200, -4550, -322).
Note: You must have completed the tasks "The Rivervale Ruins" and "Wastelands of Everfrost" in order to access the Faydark island.
You say, 'Hail, Seria Woodwind'
Seria Woodwind says 'Hail, friend.  Once I might have offered young Koada'Dal in the ways of the Koada'Vie, but there is too much lost.  We have [little to defend] left of our city, and even Firiona seems to have lost all hope.'
You say, 'Little to defend?'
Seria Woodwind says 'It's in ruins, leaving morale far more destroyed than our city.  If we can [sway another threat] from attacking us in our fragile state, we may have a chance to recover.'
You say, 'Sway another threat?'
Seria Woodwind says 'Forgive me for my hesitation, _____.  I'd like to think you can, but if you're [willing to try], I suppose you shall give it a shot.'
You say, 'I'm willing to try'
Seria Woodwind says 'Very well.  Our long time enemies have grown in power since the earth was sundered.  The decaying skeletons that you would think would fall apart at a breath on their fragile bones have become formidable.  And to make it worse, the minions of Crushbone are just as strengthened.  It has been suicide to many of our forces to try to kill them.  I can only wish you luck taking them down.'
You have been assigned the task 'For the Sake of Life'.
Seria Woodwind, the traditional trainer of the Koada`Vie of Felwithe seems particularly sensitive over the state of Felwithe. With her esteem crushed, she's requested that you try to take down some of Felwithe's traditional foes.

You have been assigned the task 'The Sword of Shadows'.
You have found a mysterious sword that seems to whisper thoughts into your mind. What is its purpose? Perhaps only time will tell.

---

1\. The sword thirsts for shadow. Defeat sorrowful shades to sate its desires 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
2\. The sword thirsts for shadow. Defeat stalking shadows to sate its desires 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
3\. The sword thirsts for shadow. Defeat pools of darkness to sate its desires 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
4\. The sword calls for its former master. Talk to Xilixizan in Miragul's Nightmare 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_Xilixizan is a mephit found at location 445, 685 (in the large clearing that is northwest of the sword's location)._
You say, 'Hail, Xilixizan'
Xilixizan says 'I was hoping this day would never come. You found the sword. Lest you wish to be overcome by shadow by its curse, you must listen to what I have to say. The sword must be destroyed. After the sword consumed me and made me shadow, I had nothing but time to find a way to permanently destroy the sword. Through my endless hours of research, I believe I have discovered the means to destroy the sword. The sword shuns light and the the only means to obtain such powerful items of light is from the forgotten guardians that guard the palace to the north. Gather these globes of pure light from the forgotten guardians and return to me. It is the only means to destroy the sword.'
5\. Collect a globe of pure light from the forgotten guardians 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_This is a common drop from "a forgotten guardian" in the castle of Miragul's Nightmare. NOTE: The step will only update if dropped by a forgotten guardian. The ancient guardians drop the globe as well, but won't update the step._
6\. Return to Xilixizan and speak with him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xilixizan'
Xilixizan says 'You collected the orbs? That is excellent news. The next step is to take the orbs of light along with the sword and place them in a chest. The chest is infused with a spell to destroy the sword. If all works as I hope it does, the sword will be destroyed and you will be safe from its curse. Look for the chest in the nearby ruins.'
7\. Place the globe of pure light into the battered chest 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
8\. Place the mysterious sword into the battered chest 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_A battered chest spawns at about 445, 440 (in the ruins east of Xilixizan's location)._
9\. The sword has been destroyed, but it has spawned a shadowed beast. Destroy it! 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_A shadowed beast spawns as soon as you get the above updates. It attacks, hits for a max ~6,500 and is stunnable. If you got to this point in the task, you should handle this mob with ease._
a shadowed beast has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a shadowed beast's corpse is no longer a problem.
10\. Return to Xilixizan and inform him the sword has been destroyed 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xilixizan'
Xilixizan says 'I saw the flash of light from here when the sword was destroyed. I am pleased that you will not end up a servant of shadow as I have become. Although it is too late for me, I am glad you were able to save yourself. Please take this as a token of my gratitude. I will rest a bit easier knowing the sword will no longer trouble anyone.'
