# Prisoner of the Portal

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 95 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 95
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is one of five pre-requisite tasks leading up to the Miragul's Nightmare mission, "Miragul's Vendetta". There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task.
This is a solo task that begins with a glittering portal in Miragul's Nightmare. It's located in the castle of Miragul's Nightmare (center room of the bottom floor). Beware there is one roaming mob that paths through the room and a mob on either side of the portal (though slightly out of aggro range).
A faint voice from beyond the glittering portal calls out to you for help.
You say, 'Hail, a glittering portal'
A faint voice calls to you from beyond the portal, 'Has someone heard my pleas at last? Do not be frightened. I am neither golem nor mephit, but a helpless [prisoner] of this portal!'
You say, 'Prisoner?'
The voice continues, 'When the nightmare began my father, Dridus, thought he would spare my life by sending me through the portal, but his actions have only forced me to be an [unwilling] witness to the extinction of everything I loved. The brilliance that once filled these halls has been reduced to a dull and colorless haze.'
You say, 'An unwilling witness?'
The voice responds, 'Devoid of vibrancy or not, I still yearn for a [life] beyond this portal. To feel the cool air rush over my skin or delight in the fragrance of wild flowers, are unimaginable pleasures.'
You say, 'What life?'
The voice explains, 'My father, in all his well-meaning ignorance, would never have condemned me to this fate without being certain there was a way to reverse it. Lacking a physical form certainly hinders me from discovering how, though. You, on the other hand, you could [help] me; you have the kiss of life shimmering on your skin, and a sense of altruism in your heart, I can sense it.'
You say, 'How can I help?'
The girl from beyond the portal says, 'My father's quarters are not far from here. He always kept his work closely guarded. Whatever knowledge he gathered about the portal would be within an arm's reach of where he slept.'
You have been assigned the task 'Prisoner of the Portal'.
A faint voice from beyond the glittering portal has asked for your help in breaking free.
- 1. Locate Dridus' quarters 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
- 2. Recover Dridus' journal 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
The journal is a groundspawn in a northwestern room upstairs in the castle at location 1135, 710, 138. You can pick it up safely with invisibility (provided no named is up).
- 3. Deliver Dridus' journal to the girl beyond the portal 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You hear the faint rustle of pages turning, as the girl beyond the portal hums with quiet understanding.
The girl beyond the portal is the portal itself. You trade the book to the portal and hail it for this step.
- 4. Speak to the girl beyond the portal 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, a glittering portal'
a glittering portal shimmers as the girl on the other side speaks, 'It's here, the information I need to set myself free. Take careful note, _____. The delicate balance of force within the portal must remain constant, or it will become unstable. There will need to be equal amounts of energy on both sides before I can pass through safely. Vibrant life is in limited supply, but I'm sure some of the creatures you encounter will have a hint of vigor left within them.'
- 5. Loot chromatic essences from ravens 0/2 (Miragul's Nightmare)
This is a common drop from any raven.
- 6. Pass the chromatic essences through the portal 0/2 (Miragul's Nightmare)
- 7. Speak to the girl beyond the portal 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, a glittering portal'
a glittering portal shimmers as the girl on the other side speaks, 'According to my father's notes, the portal draws its power from the blackened runes found outside of these halls. Destroying the runes should weaken the portal enough for me to pass through safely.'
- 8. Destroy the blackened runes 0/2 (Miragul's Nightmare)
There are two blackened runes you must destroy: One is located at the very southern part of the zone. The other is located in the first large clearing south of the castle (near the quest NPC Xilixizan).
The runes are surrounded by mobs you'll most likely want to clear away. The runes fight back (though don't hit very hard) and summon once damaged. In addition, shortly after attacking a rune, "a runic sentinel" spawns and attacks you (hits for a max ~7,500).
a blackened rune has been slain by _____!
a blackened rune's corpse is nothing but a pile of rubble.
- 9. Return to the portal 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Warning: Don't go into the room with the portal until you're ready for a fight. Stepping into the room will trigger the following:
- 10. Release the girl 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Evelynn appears from beyond the portal, peering around at her new surroundings. 'I can't believe it. I'm finally free!' As the newly freed girl exits the portal, her color begins to fade.
Evelynn doubles over with pain, as a confused and colorless expression spreads across her face. 'What.. what is happening to me?!'
Evelynn stares down at her distorted body in horror as the vibrance that brightened her existance is consumed by the colorlessness within Miragul's Nightmare. 'No, nooo! This can't be real. Please, make it stop!'
Evelynn attacks, hitting for a max ~7,800.
- 11. Put Evelynn out of her misery 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Evelynn has been slain by _____!
Evelynn's corpse falls silently to the ground.
You helped Evelynn travel through the portal, only to watch her succumb to the darkness that infects all within Miragul's Nightmare.
315 platinum
Experience (1.5 to 2 AAs worth at Level 90)

Modified: Mon Feb 14 19:11:29 2011 | | _This is one of five pre-requisite tasks leading up to this zone's mission, "Miragul's Vendetta". There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task._
_This is a shared task (1-6 players) that begins with Hiljorin in Miragul's Nightmare. He's located in the castle area (center room of the bottom floor). Beware there is one roaming mob that paths through this room._
You say, 'Hail, Hiljorin'
Hiljorin says 'You. Can you help a poor soul who has payed his dues a thousand-fold? I have [suffered] for so long but with your help that can end.'
You say, 'Suffered?'
Hiljorin says 'Miragul has trapped me in his nightmare as punishment for betraying him. You see, I was once a trusted servant of his. I was there the night he performed the ritual to become a lich. My despicable [acts] led to the deaths of many innocents to fuel the dark magics needed to complete Miragul's transformation.'
You say, 'What acts?'
Hiljorin says 'Much blood was spilled by my hand, until I came to my senses and realized what I was doing was against all of nature. I had enough. I left the ritual. I was hunted for many years by Miragul's abominations for my treachery. They eventually caught up with me and now I suffer as an undead shadow in Miragul's nightmare. I have found a [way out] however.'
You say, 'A way out?'
Hiljorin says 'I am not a simpleton. Even in undeath my mind is still intact, even if my body is not. Miragul keeps me trapped here, and undoubtedly others with the use of magical orbs. If you can [assist] me, I know of a way to possibly destroy them though.'
You say, 'I will assist you'
Hiljorin says 'Go forth and collect globs of shadow essence from the pools of darkness or other shadowy creatures that inhabit this area. I will explain more when you return them to me.'
You have been assigned the task 'Please Release Me'.
Hiljorin has betrayed Miragul and has been trapped in Miragul's Nightmare as a punishment. He believes he knows of a way to free himself however, and it is up to you to help him.
Destroy living pools or other shadowed creatures and loot globs of shadow essence. You are not sure what Hiljorin wishes to do with such an awful substance, but he should hopefully have more answers once you return them to him.

Here, take this ectoplasm and use it on six orbs. Use caution; I know not the effects this may have on them. Return to me when you are done.'

# Such a Prankster

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This is one of five pre-requisite tasks leading up to this zone's mission, "Miragul's Vendetta". There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task.
This is a shared task (1-6 players) that begins with a mephit named Mylzital in Miragul's Nightmare. He's located at about 400, 445, 45 (in the northeastern ruins in the large clearing that's south of the castle entrance).
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'My brother, Xylzital is such a pain! He keeps pranking me and I am sick of it! Hmm. . . I know! You could help me prank him! There is only one tiny [problem] though.'
You say, 'A problem?'
Mylzital says 'The invisible men known as unseen foes have taken my tomes of summoning. I wanted to use the tomes to try to scare Xylzital. Do you think you could get the tomes [back] for me? We could prank him really good if you could do that for me.'
You say, 'I'll get them back'
Mylzital says 'You will get my tomes? Yes! The first tome I need is my tome of spider summoning. If you find it, bring it back to me and we will make Xylzital sorry he ever tried to play a trick on me!'
You have been assigned the task 'Such a Prankster'.
Mylzital is fed up with his brother, Xylzital always playing pranks on him. He thinks he can gain the upper hand though. If you could help him out, that is.
Note: All five tomes in this task are pre-lootable. They drop from the "unseen" mobs in the living areas of the castle. There are a few rooms of them on the bottom floor and another four rooms of them on the second floor.
For each of the five tomes: You'll turn in a tome, hail Mylzital, follow a summoned creature to his brother, Xylzital (southwest of Mylzital's location), hail Xylzital, and then return to Mylzital.
Other than the unseen foes, there is no direct combat involved with this task.
Find Mylzital's tome of spider summoning and return it to him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Speak with Mylzital 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'With my tome of spider summoning, I can summon a big, scary spider to freak Xylzital out! Stand back a moment while I invoke the spell! Whana, whana, boop, bang! Wow, this is a scary spider. Follow it and see if it scares Xylzital.'
Follow the summoned spider to Xylzital and ask him if it scared him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xylzital'
Xylzital says 'Let me guess. My brother told you a big, scary spider would freak me out. Well you both will need to try harder than that. Amateurs!'
The spider didn't scare Xylzital. Return to Mylzital and let him know that he will need to think of a better prank 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'He isn't scared of spiders? What the heck? Hmm. Well why don't you get my tome of snake summoning back from the unseen foes. Surely he will have to be scared of snakes!'
Find Mylzital's tome of snake summoning and return it to him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Speak with Mylzital 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Ok, stand back while I summon this horribly scary snake! Blazaamm! Oh, my. That is indeed a scary snake. Follow it to Xylzital and see if that does the trick.'
Follow the summoned snake to Xylzital and ask him if it scared him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xylzital'
Xylzital says 'C'mon now. A snake? Really? How pathetic. You guys are the worst pranksters I have ever seen.'
The snake didn't scare Xylzital. Return to Mylzital and let him know that he will need to think of a better prank 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Ah, c'mon. Who isn't scared of snakes?! I guess we will have to try something else. Hmm. . . Why don't you get my tome of rat summoning back from the unseen foes? That just might work.'
Find Mylzital's tome of rat summoning and return it to him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Speak with Mylzital 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Ok! One disgusting rat coming up! Shooom bam boom! What a lovely rat. You know the drill. Follow it to Xylzital and let me know if that makes him cry.'
Follow the summoned rat to Xylzital and ask him if it scared him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xylzital'
Xylzital says 'Aw, a cute little rat. Are you trying to make me laugh? Well you have! Ha! Go away.'
The rat didn't scare Xylzital. Return to Mylzital and let him know that he will need to think of a better prank 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Geez. Is Xylzital scared of anything at all? Well, I am not giving up. Get my tome of bat summoning back from the unseen foes and return it to me.'
Find Mylzital's tome of bat summoning and return it to him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Speak with Mylzital 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Alright, let's summon ourselves a bat! Zoom, doom and boom! That is a freaky looking bat. Let's hope this scares him. Follow it and let me know how Xylzital reacts.'
Follow the summoned bat to Xylzital and ask him if it scared him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xylzital'
Xylzital says 'Ohhh! Bats are soooo scary! Not! You guys are a hoot!'
The bat didn't scare Xylzital. Return to Mylzital and let him know that he will need to think of a better prank 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Wow, this is getting tiresome, eh? Well I am almost out of ideas. Why don't you get my tome of rabbit summoning back from the unseen foes. I don't know anyone who is scared of a cuddly bunny, but it is worth a try.'
Find Mylzital's tome of rabbit summoning and return it to him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Speak with Mylzital 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'Ok, give me some room and let's hope I don't summon a rampaging, murderous type of bunny. Salala, bala, dooop! Aw, how cute. Look at the little bunny! Follow him to Xylzital.'
Follow the summoned rabbit to Xylzital and ask him if it scared him 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Xylzital'
Xylzital says 'Whoa, whoa, whoa! Now that's just wrong! Get that rabbit outta here! You guys win, just get it away from me!!!'
The rabbit scared the heck out of Xylzital. Return to Mylzital and let him know of your success 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Mylzital'
Mylzital says 'You're kidding me, right?! He is scared of rabbits! Oh, man. The guys back at home would love this bit of info. Thanks for helping me find his weakness. Maybe now he will think twice about pranking me in the future.'

Speak to the Nightmare Emissary after you are transported 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Nightmare Emissary'
Nightmare Emissary says 'Miragul has tired with you. Your death will be quite painful.'
_The Emissary despawns._
Defeat the sorrowful shades 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Defeat the stalking shadows 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Defeat the pools of darkness 0/3 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_You'll find the above mob types scattered throughout the zone-in area. A few of them are also in the surrounding valleys (some also see invisibility)._
Explore the burnt down housing area 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_From the zone-in, head north and then east until you come across another clearing._
Assist the lone warrior, Dariah, by defeating an undead captain 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_The undead captain is a straight melee fight. It hits for a max ~7,500 (has about 600k hitpoints)._
an undead captain has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
an undead captain's corpse collapses into a pile of bones.
Speak to Dariah Syke 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dariah Syke'
Dariah Syke says 'I appreciate the help. I was disarmed and caught off guard. We have to get out of this nightmarish dream world. I want to help you, but I'm wounded and I lost my equipment. Can you go find my pack while I tend to my wounds?'
Find Dariah's pack and return it to her 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_Her pack is a groundspawn behind a house located across from Dariah. Two mobs spawn in the area, one of which sees invisibility._
Talk to Dariah about the situation 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dariah Syke'
Dariah Syke says 'Thank you. I think we can escape this trap. I have a feeling it won't be easy though. There are undead soldiers ahead, and more shadow creatures. There may be a way to escape this 'reality' in that castle-like structure down the road and up the hill. Let's go there, and get rid of the general in charge of the undead troops. Then we can use the portal to escape.'
Locate and search the castle-structure 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_This updates as you enter the castle area in the very north of the zone. Note: None of the skeleton mobs see through Invis vs. Undead, so with IVU up, you can move around the area freely._
Defeat the Undead General 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_The Undead General hits for a max ~9,000 (has about 775k hitpoints). He's slowable and mezzable. He casts the spell "Nightmare Blade":_
Nightmare Blade: PB AE 60', Magic (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 15300 per tick
2: Decrease Attack Speed by 80%
The Undead General has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
The Undead General's corpse collapses into a pile of bones.
Search for an exit 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_This is the center room inside the castle, where you'd find the glittering portal. As you enter the room, you'll see this:_
And so we meet again. The same fools who were so willing to trespass are now so eager to escape. Did you think master would permit you to wander his sanctuary, slaughtering his kin?
The vibrant life that flows through your veins will soon bleed dry, until your body is nothing but a colorless husk. I am tasked with ending your life, and in my makers name, I will not fail.
_Note: While the Nightmare Emissary is aggro, he doesn't attack until provoked, so you enter the room and get ready._
Defeat the Nightmare Emissary 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
_Note: The task locks upon the Emissary's death._
_The Nightmare Emissary hits for a max ~9,000 (has about 775k hitpoints) and is slowable and snareable. He casts a fear spell called "Futile Escape"._
_You'll also have undead mobs spawning from the portal as the fight goes on ( **timed? or health based?**)._
_NOTE: The Emissary is NOT leashed to his spawn area, so he can pulled out of his room, away from the portal where the adds spawn._
Speak to Dariah Syke 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dariah Syke'
Dariah Syke says 'Thank you. Your help has been invaluable. And you've stopped the lich Miragul, at least for now. I just hope I can wake up from this nightmare soon. Let's get out of here. I'll be fine, just go to the portal and get out of here. I won't be far behind.'
You've made a stand against Miragul and remain victorious for now. The death of the undead figments and the Emissary has allowed Dariarh and yourself to leave. It is likely he has not finished with you yet, however. Use the portal discovered in the fountain to escape.
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Dariah Syke'
Dariah Syke says 'You had better escape while you can, stranger! Once this creature is finished with me, you will be next! We're in Miragul's trap.'
_To zone out, you can say "leave" to the glittering portal or to the cowardly shade at the zone-in._
You say, 'Hail, a glittering portal'
a glittering portal appears to allow you to exit Mirgaul's trap and return to his nightmare. Do you want to [leave]?

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a group progression task that begins with Tiseth the Thrall in Fear Itself (located within the Ring of Fire at the top of the temple)._
_You must have completed the following tasks in order to request it:_
\- Bite of the Beast
\- Follower of Fear
\- At the Altar of Fear
\- Redemption (House of Thule Upper mission)
\- Catastrophic Power (Al'Kabor mission)
\- Miragul's Vendetta (Miragul's Nightmare mission)
_Note: The Well mission "What Lies Below" also used to be required. However, a patch on December 8, 2010, eliminated this requirement._
You say, 'Hail, Tiseth the Thrall'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'With your pledge, Cazic Thule has taken notice. I can feel his power building all around us. It is finally time to [meet] our God! He will reward our efforts to spread fear in this realm, and spare us from his vengeful wrath. Followers of Thule will be welcomed into Cazic's fearful embrace, and allowed to rule alongside him in the Plane of Fear.'
You say, 'Let's meet him...'
_Without pre-requisites, you see:_
Tiseth the Thrall says 'I really need you to speak to Lilth or Elard Velsorn in the house, or perhaps the Nightmare Emissary, who lives in my nightmares.'
_With pre-requisites, you see:_
Tiseth the Thrall says 'I will gather our fellow followers, and together we will sacrifice every living creature within this realm as an offering to Cazic Thule. He cannot ignore our devotion! Are you ready to [begin]?'
You have been assigned the task 'An Offering to Thule'.
You say, 'Ready to begin!'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'We will meet again, \_\_\_\_\_, before our God!'

---

_The first 13 steps are open from the start of the task. All mob types are found in groups of six. The mobs in these groups can't be split from each other (they're aggro-linked)._
_Each group of mobs has a main mob that hits like a regular Fear mob (max ~7,500) and has a normal amount of hitpoints. In addition to the main mob, there are five lesser mobs that are usually blue-cons (though sometimes even cons), hit for much less, and have much less hitpoints._
_Fright, Dread, and Terror each hit for a max ~7,500; are slowable; and are immune to mez, root, and snare. None of the golems has any AEs or special attacks (no flurry; no rampage)._
_Note: There is an achievement associated with this task for leaving Fright, Dread, and Terror as the last remaining kills._
Wipe out all amygdalans 0/24 (Fear Itself)
Slaughter all boogeymen 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Destroy all fetid fiends 0/18 (Fear Itself)
Defeat all frightfingers 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Eliminate all glarelords 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Kill all gorgons 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Annihilate all phantasms 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Exterminate every last phoboplasm 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Eradicate the entirety of samhains 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Sacrifice all scarelings 0/12 (Fear Itself)
Kill Dread 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Kill Fright 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Kill Terror 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_NOTE: The task locks after you complete the last of any of the above steps._

---

Meet Tiseth and devotees at the base of the blood mound 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Speak with Tiseth and confront Cazic Thule 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Tiseth the Thrall'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'This is it, \_\_\_\_\_. The moment we have been preparing for. We are in the presence of a God! Cazic Thule shall recognize our efforts to spread fear, and welcome us into him realm. Come, let us confront our God and receive our humble reward!'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'Cazic Thule, God of Fear, hear us! Your loyal servants, eager to do your bidding! We await your command.'
Cazic Thule's spikes bristle, and a guttural rasp penetrates your mind. 'Death is not the only thing a mortal fears. I am an adder beneath the brush; I am a wolf beyond the gleam of campfires. I am a spy in a kingdom at war; I am a bastard in the castle of a king; I am a traitor in the ranks of an army. I am the loss of an eye or limb; I am the humiliation of courtesans; I am the scorn of a desired lover. And I am everlasting punishment.
Your task 'An Offering to Thule' has been updated.
Cazic Thule says 'How many like you have I killed, tortured, maimed? Here their blood surges with the fear of imminent death, as undoubtedly yours does. I could dismember you inch by inch, staunching your blood each time I tear off more of your body. I could flay you and splay your living flesh like a banner over my spines, every exposed nerve pierced and wailing. I am unbearable pain that does not end in death.'
Cazic Thule says 'I am the obliteration of you and all of your companions. You have fought together and bled together. Now you shall die together.'
_This opens the next step._
Kill Cazic Thule 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_He hits for a max ~8,000 and casts the spells "Cazic's Inferno" and "Fearful Force":_
Cazic's Inferno: PB AE 500', Fire (-700)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 19000
Fearful Force: Single Target, Magic (-700)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 10121
2: Decrease Movement by 200%
_Once he reaches about 50% health, Cazic Thule goes non-attackable, runs away a bit to a tower, and..._
Cazic Thule's labored words form in your mind. 'I did not think this possible, but you have proven it to me. Somehow, this plane is not my own. How long ago was it? My daughter, Terris, appeared to me and bade me walk with her. She showed to me a mansion as expansive and resplendent as the very rays of Sol. We entered, and her voice faded into memory, like chimes on the wind. I was again here, in my plane.
Cazic Thule says 'The hills soon ran with blood. Adventurers came by the thousands. I dashed their skulls against this obelisk and pulled apart their bones. They flung themselves at me, cowered, and then died in agony. But never had there been so many. And they began to look the same.'
Cazic Thule says 'I wandered this plane but found no escape. I began to see visions: A luminous horn pulsing at the edge of perception; the voice of my son, Morell, rising above my daughter's. And my strength began to wane. Longer and longer it took for me to decimate an entire regiment of soldiers.'
Cazic Thule says 'And now you have me on the cusp of death. Yes, I am now certain: My children have imprisoned me, and they weaken me unto destruction. To what end I do not know. What drives a child to harbor malice and guile against their parent? Are not dreams and nightmares bastions of fear? Does not the child learn to fight or flee from the vicious predator in the safety of dreams or nightmares? Without me, my children have no purpose.'
Cazic Thule's words come slowly. 'And what of you? What power do you have over me? What quarrel do we have? Would you walk blithely into a throng of swords and spears arrayed against you? Would you sacrifice yourself for another without hesitation, or would you thrust a companion into a rain of arrows to shield yourself from death? I can save or destroy you. You hate me, but I bring balance to your existence. Without Fear, you are all doomed.'
_At this point, Cazic Thule's health drops to 1%, and it's possible to attack him again. You can finish him off here._
Cazic Thule has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Claim your reward from the ornate chest 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You have defeated Cazic Thule within his dream realm, and are now able to further explore the House of Thule.
_When the chest spawns, an untargettable version of Terris Thule appears and a short script runs. Some spell effects appear to show her absorbing her father's power, and then she disappears._
Terris Thule stares at the corpse of her father. 'You were a fool, Cazic. Your power was instinctual, but mine is intellectual; you controlled the body, but I control the mind. And now I will have both. He cannot stop me; you cannot stop me!'
Terris Thule says 'Do you hear me, adventurers? It constantly amazes me how stupid you are! Did you think you defeated me? Did you think you had any power over nightmares? Did you think these hills and corpses were anything other than dreams? And now you have killed my father. Do not fear, though. You are my precious little puppets, always running around, killing everything that poses the slightest bit of threat to you. If you had not killed him, I would have done so myself. His time was at an end.'
Terris Thule says 'When he took me to the house, his horn was glowing brighter than ever. He said his power resonated within the walls, and near the winged statues his thoughts sprang to life: hallways that submerged into sunken caverns, gables that floated off into clouds. I touched a statue, and a banister changed to a hundred writhing adders. Morell pressed deeper into the house, but I had other plans.'
Terris Thule says 'I brought Cazic inside, rested my hand on his arm, and touched a statue. He fell into the nightmare you see now, never to awake. Morell objected, but he was more interested in his augmented powers than my designs. And besides, since when have dreams alone held more power than nightmares? How would that weakling be able to challenge me?'
Terris Thule says 'And now he will certainly have no recourse. I will draw to me the remnants of Cazic's power...'
Terris Thule screams in pain and anger as a portal opens around her. 'Is this your doing, adventurers? You have no idea what magnitude of power you are playing with! Morell, help me! Do not let them take our father!'
_A frightened follower spawns nearby as a means to zone out:_
You say, 'Hail, a frightened follower'
a frightened follower says 'Please, don't hurt me! I... I can show you the way [out] if you'll spare my life.'

# Alezra #1: Bite of the Beast

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 95 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 95
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task is the first in a series of three. It begins with Marza D`Vour in Fear Itself. She's on the ground, located a little ways north of the zone-in.
You say, 'Hail, Marza D`Vour'
Marza D`Vour says 'I haven't much energy to speak, so please listen carefully. I have been bitten by a beast whose venom is both painful and slow, yet my fear is not for myself; it is for my daughter, Alezra. She's wandering within this horrifying dream world, lost and unaware of her deceitful surroundings. Please find her, and [help] her flee this nightmare.'
You say, 'I'll help her'
Marza D`Vour says 'Please, do not let your concerns linger on me. It is only my daughter I worry about. Her safety is my dying wish. I'll cling to what little energy I have left as I await your return.'
You have been assigned the task 'Bite of the Beast'.
Marza's dying wish is to see her daughter freed from Cazic Thule's horrific dream, though it may prove to be a rather complicated task.
Speak to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Alezra D`Vour is found in a small tower a little ways east of the zone-in.
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'State your business, stranger. Are you a [follower] of Cazic Thule or are you here for some other [reason]?'
You say, 'Another reason...'
Alezra D`Vour says 'Let me guess, my mother sent you. When will that wretched hag give up! If my [faith] wasn't being tested here, I'd hunt down that pathetic excuse for a woman and finish her off myself.'
You say, 'Your faith?'
Alezra D`Vour says 'My unwavering devotion to the God of Fear himself, Cazic Thule, is always being tested. Those who prove to be worthy shall be welcomed in his powerful realm, and spared the horrific death that is an undeniable truth for those who choose to cower in fear, rather than embrace it.'
Return to Marza 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Marza D`Vour'
Marza D`Vour says 'My only daughter has become a mindless follower of Cazic Thule? My heart cannot bear to believe it! How can she not see through the deceptive haze? This wretched place is not home to the God of Fear, but rather a vivid representation of his subconscious. Only in Cazic's dreams would Alezra devote herself to a God who simply wishes to see her suffer. This dream needs to dissolve before it's too late, before I lose my daughter to Cazic's cruel hunger for death. Please, you must help. Gain the trust of Cazic's followers, so that you may meet the God of Fear and destroy him, along with this horrible dream that keeps my daughter entranced.'
Speak to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'And so you return, _____. Am I to assume this means you're ready to pledge yourself to Cazic Thule? Your devotion will be tested, be assured. Not just anyone is deemed worthy of Cazic's presence. I have a small task for you, to start with. It involves using a relatively simple device to tag wolves in the area. Once they're tagged, I'll be able to track them when they're needed for research...'
You receive a Wolf Tagging Device.
Tag wolves for tracking 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Wolves are found in the east-central part of the zone. You can do this step while invis. You have to target a wolf and right-click the tagging device. Do this for three wolves and return it to Alezra.
Return the tagging device to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Alezra D`Vour says 'You've returned unscathed? It appears you're more useful than I first thought. Now that the wolves are tagged, I can begin preparations for a subduing serum. I'll need you help gathering ingredients, but once the serum is made, I'll be able to administer it to the wolves you tagged. It's crucial that the wolves are subdued before I attempt to bring them back here for their... transformation.'
This opens up the next two steps:
Collect Docilla Leaves 0/4 (Fear Itself)
Loot Spinechiller Ichor 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Task Window Says: Gather the items Alezra needs to create a subduing serum. The docilla shrub grows best in pools of blood.
Docilla Leaves are a groundspawn in the blood rivers in the northeastern part of the zone. They appear as small plants growing in the blood.
The Ichor is a common drop off spinechiller spiders. It is not pre-lootable.
Give the Docilla Leaves to Alezra 0/4 (Fear Itself)
Give the Spinechiller Ichor to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Speak with Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'You brought me perfect specimens? I'm intrigued. I wonder if you could handle one of my pets. I call them Beasts of Fear, and they are the result of crossing wolves with the blood of Fear. Yet in order for the transformation to be complete, the creature needs to feed on a few decaying corpses. I'm sure they will be plenty near the ruins.'
Allow the Beast of Fear to feast on decaying corpses 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Task Window Says: Use the leash Alezra gave you to summon a Beast of Fear, and allow it to feed on corpses scattered around the ruins.
There are various ruined strutures in the west-central part of the zone. You can find decaying corpses around this area. Right-click the Beast of Fear Leash to update this step (again, you can do this step while invis). Respawn time is 4 minutes.
Return to Alezra with the leash 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Alezra D`Vour says 'And so the transformation begins. Soon there will be packs of these beasts roaming Cazic's domain, instilling terror and fear into all those who trespass. You should hold your head high knowing that you had a pivotal role in making this happen.'
You've helped Alezra transform an ordinary wolf into a Beast of Fear, while earning trust with those who have devoted themselves to Cazic Thule.
210 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
20 Dream Motes
The task "Follower of Fear" follows this one.Rewards:

# Alezra #2: Follower of Fear

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 95 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 95
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task is the second in a series of three. It begins with Alezra D`Vour in Fear Itself. She's found in a small tower a little ways east of the zone-in.
You must have completed the "Bite of the Beast" task before you can request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'There are many who come here, eager to join the ranks of Cazic's chosen, though not all are destined to become disciples of Fear. One must prove themselves loyal, resourceful, and remorseless if they wish to stand before our leader and be judged. You will have my recommendation, if you can withstand the [trials] I set before you.'
You say, 'Trials?'
Alezra D`Vour says 'A handful of those who pledged themselves as servants of Thule, have now dissented and are cowering away somewhere in this realm. Your first test is one of loyalty. Pledging yourself to Cazic Thule is a commitment that extends well beyond death, and those who have dissented shall be disposed of by your hand.'
You have been assigned the task 'Follower of Fear'.
To prove yourself worthy, Alezra has asked you to complete a series of trials before joining the ranks of Cazic devotees.
Dispose of the dissenters who are cowering in fear around the realm.
Xoraik was last spotted near the gorge to the northwest. Lorenai has taken refuge in a fallen bell. Vendula was seen hiding beneath the western stone bridge.
Dispose of Xoraik 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Dispose of Lorenai 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Dispose of Vendula 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Xoraik is found in a ruined building in the very northwest part of the zone.
Lorenai is found under a fallen bell in the northeast part of the zone (head due east of the southernmost point of the ring of fire).
Vendula is just south of the ring of fire (on the ground, beside the north ramp leading up to the top of the frightfinger structure).
They are all straight-forward fights, difficulty on par with trash in the zone. They're immune to stun and mez.
Speak to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'The dissenters have been disposed of? I wish I could have seen the overwhelming fear in their eyes as they met their end. You have passed the trial of loyalty. Are you ready for your next [test]?'
You say, 'I'm ready for the next test'
Alezra D`Vour says 'Resourcefulness should never be underestimated. A devotee of Thule needs to be more than just a loyal strong-arm. We need well-rounded followers, who excel in tasks of a more... agreeable nature. To test your resourcefulness, I ask you to gather rare ingredients used in crafting a slow-killing poison.'
This opens up the next three steps:
Give the Clump of Diseased Flesh to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Give the Ruptured Tear Duct to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Give the Nightmare Blood to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Task Window Says: Alezra has asked you to find rare ingredients for a slow killing poison as a test of your resourcefulness; a clump of diseased flesh from a boogeyman, a ruptured tear duct from a glarelord, and nightmare blood.
These are an uncommon drop from boogeymen, glarelords, and nightmares (respectively). They are confirmed to drop from any mobs with "boogeyman", "glarelord" and "nightmare" in their names.
They are pre-lootable.
Speak to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'To kill those who defy us simply isn't enough; we must inflict pain and suffering along with death. Your apt resourcefulness will allow us to craft many deadly poisons, which will do just that. Speak to me when you are ready to [prove] you are remorseless.'
You say, 'I'll prove it...'
Alezra D`Vour says 'There are a few who have entered this realm with the intent of doing harm to Cazic Thule. These people thrive on honesty and morals. They are weak, and their trusting nature will be their undoing. Speak to them and convince them to drink the poison you have helped create.'
Poison trapped dreamers 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Task Window Says: To show you have no remorse, Alezra has asked you to convince trusting trapped dreamers to ingest deadly poison. They should be wandering within the first level of the large temple.
The trapped dreamers are found in the temple within the ring of fire. There are a few spawnpoints on the second floor down from the top. They're typically found in dead-ends.
The regular amygdalans don't see invisibility, so unless you run into a named, you'll be fine to run around it with invis.
NOTE: These trapped dreamers have a 20-minute respawn time.
You say, 'Hail, a trapped dreamer'
a trapped dreamer says 'You've come the [help] me, yes? I've been so terrified wandering this place alone. My only wish is to return to my wife and small children.'
You say, 'Yes, I've come to help you...'
a trapped dreamer says 'So this is all a dream? This potion will rouse me from my sleep? More of a nightmare I say, but I trust you stranger. I'll drink it as I am told.'
The trapped dreamer keels over and dies.
You say, 'Hail, a trapped dreamer'
a trapped dreamer says 'At last, a friendly face! I was beginning to think I'd die down here... Can you [help] me escape?'
You say, 'Yes... I can help you escape...'
a trapped dreamer says 'A gate potion you say? Are you sure it's safe? Oh, you seem trustworthy, and taking a risk is better than the alternative I suppose.'
You've gone and killed this one too.
Return to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'Well done, _____. I don't see a trace of guilt lingering on your face. Earning my recommendation to stand before our leader, Tiseth, is no easy feat. If you wish to join our ranks, I will aid you in completing the process.'
You have passed the trials put before you, and earned Alezra's recommendation to join the ranks of Cazic's devotees.

Kill fetid fiends in the name of Cazic Thule 0/6 (Fear Itself)
Kill shiverbacks in the name of Cazic Thule 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Kill phoboplasms in the name of Cazic Thule 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Collect skeletal remains 0/4 (Fear Itself)
_This is a common drop off the above mobs._
Give the remains to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Speak to Alezra 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Alezra D`Vour'
Alezra D`Vour says 'Under the fearful gaze of Cazic Thule, I curse these remains so that those who gaze upon them will feel the terror and fear of a God. Let them serve as an offering, placed in Cazic's temple by an initiate of Thule, bathed in the blood of Fear.'
Bathe yourself in blood 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_There is a room in the lower floors of the temple with a pool of blood (location is 795, 410, 75). Stand in the blood for a few moments for the update._
Place the remains in the temple as an offering 0/1 (Fear Itself)
get on top of the table and CLICK the Bones
_Go to the top floor of the temple and place the remains on the altar in the northeastern-most room of the floor._
Present yourself to Tiseth the Thrall 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_Tiseth the Thrall is found at the very top of the temple (can only get to him from the outside, by using the ramp on the eastern side of the temple)._
You say, 'Hail, Tiseth the Thrall'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'Never before have I encountered an initiate who has shown such an affinity for fear. I believe with your pledge, we will be able to seek out Cazic Thule himself, and be welcomed into his fearful embrace!'
You completed the scared ritual by standing before Tiseth the Thrall, and were found to be worthy of Cazic's presence.
_Rewards:_
158 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
15 Dream Motes
_This leads into the group mission for Fear Itself, "An Offering to Thule"._
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

Modified: Wed Jan 29 09:00:29 2025 | | _This is one of five tasks leading up to "The Staff Retreat" and the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, "Hippity Hoppity Kill"._
_Of the five tasks, this is the only group task and the only one that takes place in its own instance. It begins with Gurroth Aberrine who is found at location 860, 1200, 60 (inside the house in the candy land area)._
_There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task._
_NOTE: Depite what the timer says, you may be limited to two hours to complete this task._
_NOTE: Unlike most instances in this expansion, all mobs in this instance (including named) DO respawn._
You say, 'Hail, Gurroth Aberrine'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Hello there. I don't suppose you've brought along any spare food for a [stranded] soul, have you?'
You say, 'Stranded?'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Yes, marooned here. I have been lost in this crazy place for days... Or is it weeks?? I found my way here, as here is where I came to find myself tolerated, not attacked by every living (or unliving) thing in the area. I have bound my wounds up as best I could, and set to work finding food in this [wretched] place.'
You say, 'Wretched?'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Yes, it is truly dismal. The folks living here are clearly out of their minds. All there is to eat is this oversized candy, which I have done until half my teeth have fallen out. I am starved for some good, solid food, but I fear I will need [help] with this as I am too weak to exert myself. I broke a bone in my hand when I fainted from hunger yesterday, and can no longer wave a sword.'
You say, 'How can I help?'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'If you could lend assistance, I could let you have something of mine which I'll no longer be needing to lug around. All I need you to do is go and kill some hares and [rabbits] as are loping around nearby, bring me back their carcasses, and I will have my first real meal in a long time.'
You say, 'Rabbits?'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Three of the speckled hares and three of the ordinary brown rabbits, what do you say? Are you [ready], willing, and able?'
You say, 'I'm ready'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Splendid, I will get the cookpot ready while you go back out there to hunt. Let me know when you'd like to [go].'
You have been assigned the task 'Gouzah Safari'.
Prevent Gurroth from dying of malnutrition by collecting the rabbit meat he needs to finish making a pot of rabbit stew.
_Say "go" to zone into the instance._
You say, 'I'd like to go...'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Good luck! Let me know if you need anything.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
_Mobs in this instance hit for a max ~8,500 (or more, if you're low on mitigation)._

---

Kill three speckled hares for Gurroth's rabbit stew 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Acquire three speckled hare carcasses 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Kill three rabbits for Gurroth's rabbit stew 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Acquire three rabbit carcasses 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
_Speckled hares spawn around the northern part of the candy land while regular rabbits spawn out in the forest._
Deliver three speckled hare carcasses to Gurroth 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Yes, this will do spendidly, there's enough speckled hare meat here to bring me back from death's door, assuming I can find a cookpot.'
Deliver three rabbit carcasses to Gurroth 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Gurroth Aberrine says 'The more rabbit meat, the better. If only I had some herbs and spices. I'll find some around here somewhere.'
Find out when Gurroth's stew will be ready 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Gurroth Aberrine'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Did... Did you hear that? That commotion outside? What is going on out there? Strange, horrible giant rabbits! What could they want? I'm still too weak to lift a weapon, could you run out there and try to... persuade them not to kill us all?'
Talk to the Grand Gouzah, whose name is Snibbles. Find out what the Gouzahs want 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_A giant rabbit, the Grand Gouzah, spawns outside the house along with several smaller gouzahs._
You say, 'Hail, Snibbles The Grand Gouzah'
Snibbles The Grand Gouzah says 'Not often are we ready and able to intervene in your mortal matters, not always are the mystical barriers which normally separate our worlds so chaotically weakened as they are in this place. We shall take this opportunity to punish you for your crimes against our inferior, less-developed brethren. We are Gouzahs. You are killers of our distant relatives. You must die! Your civilization, your creatures, your gods - all shall be destroyed in favor of our new order!'
_Snibbles and the surrounding gouzahs attack. If you're able to fade, FD, escape, or otherwise remove your aggro from the initial attack (your death also works), you can split these mobs from one another._
It behooves you to resolve the dispute with the Gouzahs in a way that permits your survival. Kill the Grand Gouzah 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Kill the attacking Gouzahs 0/6 (Morell's Castle)
_Task Window Says:_ Kill Snibbles, the Grand Gouzah, and at least eight members of his vanguard force.
_"a sorcel gouzah" casts "Sorcelling Gouzah-spray":_
Sorcelling Gouzah-spray: PB AE 30', Chromatic (-600)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 2816 (L1) to 4000 (L149)
2: Decrease Mana by 1316 (L1) to 3000 (L212) per tick
3: Decrease Hitpoints by 1316 (L1) to 12000 (L1337) per tick
_"a shade gouzah" casts "Shadowlocking Gouzah-blast":_
Shadowlocking Gouzah-blast: PB AE 0', Chromatic (-600)
1: Mesmerize (1/90)
2: ? ? ?
3: Stun (12.00 sec/90)
4: Decrease All Resists by 140
5: Increase corruption counter by 20
_"a chaos gouzah" casts "Chaos Gouzah-bolt":_
Chaos Gouzah-bolt: Single Target, Chromatic (-600)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 19816 (L1) to 30000 (L1274)
_Grizzly gouzahs and Snibbles appear to be straight melee._
Speak to the Smart Gouzah 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak to the Smart Gouzah. Perhaps a resolution can be found to this situation.
_The Smart Gouzah spawns with a few gouzahs in the southeastern corner of the candy land area._
You say, 'Hail, The Smart Gouzah'
The Smart Gouzah says 'You cannot destroy us. Killing us only banishes our life energy for a time, returning us to our home plane. As long as we possess our planar teleportation gem, we are free to return here as we please, as many times as it takes to lay waste to those who have harmed our kin. Now you will pay for your crimes against our brethren, mortal!'
_NOTE: The task locks at this point. The Smart Gouzah also attacks. It hits for a max ~9,000 (maybe more) and appears to be straight melee._
The only way to prevent Gouzahs from invading the castle is to kill the Smart Gouzah and take its teleport gem 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Deliver the Gouzah Teleportation Gem to Gurroth, then return from the Gouzah Safari 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Gurroth Aberrine says 'What's this? A 'planar teleportation gem', you say? I wouldn't know what to do with this, but I suppose I could hang on to it for you if you ever decide you need it. Nice work on capturing this from those horrid Gouzah creatures, whatever they were. They looked like they were about to cause a real problem for us! I'd hate to die hungry at the hands of giant extra-planar rabbits.'
You say, 'Hail, Gurroth Aberrine'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Hey, if it's getting too gnarly in the candy forest for you, say the word, and I'll pretend I never gave you this quest... You can [return] to your normal schedule. I still won't have a decent thing to eat, but I'll get by.'
You say, 'Return me...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Morell's Castle.
Find out if Gurroth really wants to continue making rabbit stew, now that he knows the Gouzahs are always watching 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Task Window Says:_ After the planar breach returns you to the original dimension of Morell's Castle, speak to Gurroth and see if he still wishes to continue making rabbit stew, with the threat of the Gouzahs' retribution now hanging over him.
You say, 'Hail, Gurroth Aberrine'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'I suppose I could lay off hunting rabbits around here, never know when those Gouzahs might be able to return. I'll pray that the others of their kind remain unaware of our deeds, even if they still possess the ability to come here. Here, you've done much more than you bargained for, here's a reward.'
_Rewards:_
315 platinum
Experience _(about 4 AAs worth)_
Faction with Dream Delvers
40 Dream Motes
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

# Suspiciously Sweet Treats

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is one of five tasks leading up to "The Staff Retreat" and the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, "Hippity Hoppity Kill".
It's a solo task that begins with Zairia who is found at approximate location 860, 1200, 60 (inside the house in the candyland area).
There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task.
You say, 'Hail, Zairia'
Zairia says 'What a young and vibrant _____ you are. Tell me, do you like [sweets]?'
You say, 'I like sweets!'
Zairia says 'My sisters and I are masters of confections. Children flock from distant lands just to get a tiny taste of some of the sweet treats we conjure. Of course, there is a small [price] to pay for delighting in our delectables.'
You say, 'What price?'
Zairia says 'A bite of hedgeberry pie comes at the cost of a smidgen of life, which will help this aging witch bask in the grace and beauty of youth once more! Don't give me that look. It's not like the pudgy little candy hoarders around here will miss it much. All they care about is filling their rolling bellies with sweets and [treats]!'
You say, 'Treats?'
Zairia says 'The halfling youngsters can't resist a hedgeberry pie. Unfortunately, they've learned not to trust taking treats from a haggard old witch like myself. I need someone with a fresh face to [help] me lure the little candy hoarders into my house.'
You say, 'I'll help'
Zairia  grins mischievously while tapping her fingers together with obvious delight. 'Splendid! Together, you and I will create a positively delicious hedgeberry pie, but first, I'll need help collecting some [ingredients].
You say, 'What ingredients?'
Zairia says 'Hedgeberries dangle from the branches of hedgewalkers, but those fickle treants always grumble, 'Over my dead trunk!' when I ask to pluck a few. You can arrange that, I'm sure.' Zairia licks her withered lips with a sly grin and adds, 'Don't forget the speckled rabbit meat. You'd really be surprised at its sweetness! And of course, no pie could be complete without a dash of hopes and dreams, snatched easily enough from the few daydreams that float in the area.'
You have been assigned the task 'Suspiciously Sweet Treats'.
Zairia is a master when it comes to curiously sweet treats, but she needs your help gathering a few ingredients to make a special pie, one that is sure to send anyone who eats it into a sugar craze!
- 1. Loot 4 Hedgeberries 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
- 2. Loot 2 Speckled Rabbit Meat 0/2 (Morell's Castle)
- 3. Loot 1 Dream Fragment 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
The above items are common drops from treants, speckled rabbits, and daydreams (respectively).
- 4. Give the Hedgeberries to Zairia 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
- 5. Give the Speckled Rabbit Meat to Zairia 0/2 (Morell's Castle)
- 6. Give the Dream Fragment to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
- 7. Speak to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zairia'
Zairia says 'You've done a fine job, _____. Now I'll need a full jug of enchanted water, which only spills from the falls on the castle wall. You can't miss the cascade, as it flows from a magical mural that hangs on the wall at the northern edge of the stream. The jug is rather large, and may take a while to fill. Be patient and you will be rewarded.'
- 8. Collect a jug of water from the castle wall falls 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
- 9. Wait near the wall falls while the jug fills with water 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
The above-two steps update at the north end of the forest's river, under the waterfall.
WARNING: A shellscale guard spawns on you as you get these two updates. Its difficulty is on par with regular trash mobs in the zone. It's mezzable, stunnable, hits for a max ~9,000. (You don't actually have to kill it as long as you can lose its aggro.)
- 10. Give the Jug of Enchanted Water to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Zairia says 'Many thanks. I'll whip up a pie in the blink of an eye!'
- 11. Speak to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zairia'
Zairia says 'Be cautious. The candy hoarders around here are hostile little things who protect their sweets at all costs. Find a hungry halfling and lure them with the hedgeberry pie. Once you have, the rest should be a piece of cake!'
- 12. Use the Hedgeberry Pie on a hungry halfling 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
A hungry halfling is located in the area out front of the house. Target him and right-click the pie.
- 13. Lead the candy-crazed halfling to the oven 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
He will lead himself there.
- 14. Collect the Charred Halfling Remains 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
The charred remains spawn inside the oven after the halfling toasts himself.
- 15. Give the Charred Halfling Remains to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
- 16. Speak to Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zairia'
Zairia sighs and looks at her hands, which are once again withered. 'It looks like a little halfling doesn't go a long way. I'd better get back to baking. Thanks for your generous help.
Thanks to your culinary prowess and sneaky ways, Zairia has been able to temporarily reclaim her youthful appearance.
Halfling Soul
158 platinum
Experience (about 1 AA at Level 90, depending on your AA count)
Faction
20 Dream MotesRewards:
- Dream Mote [item=90255]
- Halfling Soul [item=109186]

Entered: Mon Nov 22 06:42:01 2010
Modified: Mon Jan 27 05:26:50 2025 | | _This is one of five tasks leading up to "The Staff Retreat" and the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, "Hippity Hoppity Kill"._
_It's a solo task that begins with Griklund the Satyrist. He is found due north of the zone-in from the House of Thule's Upper Floors -- up the stairs on either side of the marionette room._
_There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task._
You say, 'Hail, Griklund the Satyrist'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Hail to you, adventurer. Strange place this is, isn't it? By studying this castle, I intend to find some clues as to what forces are at work here, and what their purpose might be. To that end, my recent conversation with a satyr named Oruff has led me to a mystery. I have become so intrigued by three satyric [riddles], that I have become obsessed with learning more about them. Alas, I am not well-equipped and these creatures have frightened away my own party of adventurers.'
You say, 'Satyric riddles?'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Yes, during our tense conversation Oruff told me of three riddles that might lead the way to three magic rings that he placed upon some of the creatures here. These magic rings, held together on the fingers of one's right hand, can help an intelligent dwarf such as myself think more clearly. I would find that very useful as I consider the origins and purpose of Morell's Castle. Unfortunately, I cannot seek more information from the other satyrs, nor am I strong enough to take the rings from the animals the riddles point to. If you are interested, I can tell you the [first] riddle.'
You say, 'What is the first one?'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'The first riddle Oruff told me goes, "I am a four-legged creature, usually gentle but can be roused to a terrible anger. I have much in common with both satyrs and horses, and I am of a very rare breed. What am I?" Obtuse and non-specific... Very little in the way of clues to narrow down the possibilities. It is said the satyrs Dugelun and Kurnax may know the answer. Let me know if you wish to hear the [second] riddle.'
You say, 'What is the second one?'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'The second riddle: "I guard the castle with great aplomb, keeping the denizens of water and dry land apart. I am of arcane orientation and I yearn to smite those who would violate the mystical sanctity of this place. What am I?" Even more meaningless than the first, though I was told that the satyrs Melnax and Eruk may know the answers. All that remains to hear is the [third] riddle, and of your interest in helping me with this quest.'
You say, 'And the third one?'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Listen to this: "I am a figment of dreams; I have a fearsome appearance, though my size is not grand. I shall defend every stone of this castle to my last fiery breath. What am I?" This describes a number of creatures, some within this very chamber. Yet one must be certain before risking one's neck in matters of logic such as this. Perhaps the satyrs Auxenbaan and Reglek can shed some light on this particular riddle. At any rate, if you think yourself enough of a daring soul to learn the answers to these riddles and bring me back the three rings of insight, I will find you [deserving] enough of a nice reward.'
You say, 'Of course I'm deserving'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'All right. Go into the camp of the satyrs, if you dare, and speak with the ones who know the riddles and will answer your questions. If you still [accept] this quest, that is.'
You say, 'I accept'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Good, good, a stalwart soul you truly are.'
You have been assigned the task 'Satyrical Riddles'.
Help Griklund the Satyrist acquire three brass rings hidden somewhere in Morell's Castle. The clues to finding these rings are put to him by Oruff the Seer, each pointing to a creature on the castle grounds which carries one of the rings.

---

_You'll need to gather three brass rings. They drop commonly from enchanted mares, dream drakes, and shellscale guards and occultists._
_They are pre-lootable._
_Each satyr you have to hail is found among the satyr tents in the west-northwest part of the forest (all around location 1800, 1100, 97)._
See if the satyr Dugelun knows the meaning of the first riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Dugelun'
Dugelun says 'The legendary First Riddle, you say? Well, friend, I happen to have worked that out for my friends here many times, they say it's rubbish. I say the answer is 'an enchanted mare' because of the line about the 'very rare breed'. Centaurs are thought to be rare, but the various breeds of enchanted equines and unicorns are rarer still!'
See if the satyr Kurnax knows the meaning of the first riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Kurnax'
Kurnax says 'Of course, Dugelun is wrong. The riddle means the centaur, not the enchanted mare. Not just any centaur, mind you, but none other than Morell Thule himself!'
Deliver the first ring to Griklund the Satyrist 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Hand in the Ring of the Enchanted Mare._
Griklund the Satyrist says 'You have found the first ring, the Ring of the Enchanted Mare! I shall reward you for your patient perseverance, after you find the other two well-hidden rings. Go now and ask Melnax and Eruk about the second riddle.'
See if the satyr Melnax knows the meaning of the second riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Melnax'
Melnax says 'The answer to the second riddle is obvious as the nose on your face, friend! What can it be but an enchanted guard of some kind. The suits of armor that are animated of spirit but have no physical wearer that can be seen, for example. Smash up one of those ornate guards, and I'll bet you find what you were looking for!'
See if the satyr Eruk knows the meaning of the second riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Eruk'
Eruk says 'The references to mysticism and arcane magic in the riddle seem to point most clearly to a shellscale occultist. Maybe if you were to hack one open you would find the ring you're looking for.'
Deliver the second ring to Griklund the Satyrist 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Griklund the Satyrist says 'The second ring! I am in awe of your relic-finding talents! Soon the secrets of this place will yield to my perceptive powers of intellect, and finally we can all begin to make some sense of this place! Go and speak with Auxenbaan and Reglek, they are the experts on the third riddle.'
_Hand in the Ring of the Shiliskin Guard._
See if the satyr Auxenbaan knows the meaning of the third riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Auxenbaan'
Auxenbaan says 'If you ask me, and you are, I will tell you that the third riddle is a masterpiece of transparent meaning. Of course the riddle points to a dream drake, and no other. The references to size and fiery breath could indicate a dream drake or a devourer, but the dream drake is clearly smaller than the dream devourer, thus it must be the dream drake.'
See if the satyr Reglek knows the meaning of the third riddle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Reglek'
Reglek says 'The third riddle is about the dream devourer, of course. The reference to 'stone' is about as clear a clue as you'll ever need, since the devourer itself is made of stone.'
Deliver the third ring to Griklund the Satyrist 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Hand in the Ring of the Dream Drake._
Griklund the Satyrist says 'The third ring! You've found them all! Yes, most unexpected that you would find all three of them for me in such a short measure of time. Let me take a look at these.'
Ask Griklund the Satyrist for your reward now that you have done his bidding 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Griklund the Satyrist'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Yes, they begin to hum and grow warm to the touch. I feel a kind of mental clarity like I have never known. I shall begin to set my mind to studying and understanding the strange workings of this place, and the neighboring realities. We must learn what is happening here, and any other mysteries Morell Thule is preparing to confront us with!'
You have successfully helped Griklund unlock the mystery of the Three Rings of Oruff. He will no doubt use the power of the rings to further unravel the remaining mysteries of the castle.
_Rewards:_
158 platinum
Experience (about 1.5 AAs worth)
Faction
20 Dream Motes

**Who:**
- [Zinahra the Haunted [ _Quest_]](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=36848)

0/1**_\*__\*__\*__\*__\*_**

Entered: Mon Nov 22 07:54:42 2010
Modified: Mon Jan 27 04:58:23 2025 | | This is one of five tasks leading up to The Staff Retreat [quest=5243] and the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, Hippity Hoppity Kill [quest=5244].
It's a solo task that begins with Zinahra the Haunted [npc=36848]. She is a siren found in the sand castle area of Morell's Castle (+445,-5930,-5 -- the southwest part of the beach)..
To get to this area from the zone-in:
\- Go through the forest and into the Shellscale water maze (entered in the northeast part of the forest).
\- Step into the whirlpool on the second level of the water maze to port to the ship.
\- Make your way to the topside of the ship.
\- Click on the ship's wheel to port to the beach area.
There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task.
You say, 'Hail, Zinahra the Haunted'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'Hello, wretch. I see you have not escaped this place either. Do you intend to [parlay] with me, or are you here to murder me as these dreadful pirates have attempted?'
You say, 'I'm here to parlay with you'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'I see. I shall study you as you attempt to gain my trust with words. I know not why I am here. I know only my songs, and my [daydreams], a growing desire to leave this place... and also a gnawing sense of dread.'
You say, 'Daydreams?'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'I find myself being lulled to sleep, as easily as I might woo these pitiful humanoids to their doom with my song. Lulled by some unseen whispering voice. Then, the daydreams come to me, unbidden. At first they were languid and beautiful, full of sensual images and colors... Alas, in recent days they have turned much more ominous. Threatening imagery of unpleasantness of all sorts; feelings of fear, of loathing, and of dread. I do not know what is happening to me, and I am not prepared to deal with this sort of [enigma]. I feel as if my earlier daydreams have been forcibly ripped away from me, leaving my imagination desolate and full of darkness.'
You say, 'Enigma?'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'Perhaps it is a cosmic riddle, or a joke played at my expense. I fear I will never be able to find out, just as I am unable to remember my own existence before waking up on this beach beneath this castle of sand. I am constantly having these disturbing visions, along with feelings that I have been trapped here a very long time, and am constantly repeating my thoughts and actions. I do not wish to be trapped; I know that is not my purpose. Learning the nature of the trap may involve your help in completing a [task].'
You say, 'What task?'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'It is a small thing I ask. Listen well! I wish to leave this place, yet I cannot. Every time I try to leave, I find myself becoming more and more confused. I simply lose the will, as if I have forgotten I ever dreamed of leaving in the first place. Instead of my plan for escape, I have strange and terrible visions... as well as seductive and elegant dreams. These are being placed in my head, no doubt, by Morell Thule. I feel his presence still, and it grows more sinister by the hour. Will you [please] help me to escape this place?'
You say, 'Yes... I will please help...'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'Good. I will promise not to enthrall you if you can interrupt the source of the power that keeps me here, and bring me some evidence of whatever it may have been. Find the tangible remains of my daydreams somewhere near the castle; they will no doubt retain some trace of what has been tampering with my will.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Few Buckets of Sand'.
Zinahra the Haunted has asked you to locate her missing daydreams.

---

1\. Locate the Daydream Patch near Morell's Castle 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
This updates in the northeastern part of the forest area.
You can port back down to the zone-in area by making your way through the sand castle.
The daydreams you need to hail are found in this area.
2\. Contemplate an idle daydream 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
an idle daydream [npc=36830] is located at /way +1900,+550,+100.
You say, 'Hail, an idle daydream'
an idle daydream says '(As your mind comes in contact with the idle daydream, you begin seeing a vision through the mind's eye of Zinahra herself. You appear to be on an island, with great cliffs and green rolling plains. A peaceful sailing ship passes by in the distance. You feel the unique personalities of the humanoids aboard. You feel compelled to sing, to draw in as many victims as possible, as fast as you can before your time runs out... Then you feel the interest of a god descend upon you. The god regards your daydream as special, unique... it must be harvested and taken somewhere else... There is agony as you watch yourself - Zinahra - being taken away from 'you'...)'
3\. Contemplate a distracting daydream 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
a distracting daydream [npc=36828] is located at /way +2100,+525,+100 a little further north than the other two, near the phantasms.
You say, 'Hail, a distracting daydream'
a distracting daydream says '(As you make contact with the distracting dream, you have a vision through Zinahra's mind's eye. You are she, travelling among humanoids in Qeynos, disguised as a simple traveler. A man catches your eye in a tavern, and you begin talking. You feel pride in your appearance, despite the disguise you know you are attractive. You decide to lull the man into a sense of false security, but as you lean forward to steal his breath, you suddenly feel your daydream being pulled out of your mind, taken away by an intensely interested godlike being. You sense a deep and abiding loss as you watch yourself - as you watch Zinahra - falling away from 'you'...)'
4\. Contemplate a vivid daydream 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
a vivid daydream [npc=36829] is located at /way +1900,+650,+100.
You say, 'Hail, a vivid daydream'
a vivid daydream says '(You touch the vivid dream, and a tableau of interesting visions appears before you, through Zinahra's mind's eye. You are with other sirens, preparing to slay a pack of humans that have dared to come too close. Their begging and pleading reach your ears, but these fail to move you. You seize the nearest one by the neck and pull him close, to feed on his fear, to cull his terror for your own increasing sense of power. And you find yourself screaming, for this horrible vision is not entirely yours. This time as your daydream is ripped away from you, you see a vision of the creatures that are helping the deranged God of Dreams harvest the fruits of your imagination... Timekeeper Sandmen, who set back the clock on all the creatures Morell Thule chooses to plunder for dreams. You gasp, as a blurry vision of the interior of a sand castle passes before you.)'
5\. Go to the Sands of the Timekeeper 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Head all the way back up to the sand castle and find a sandman timekeeper [npc=36600] in one of its northern buildings. (Location: +800,-6235,+87)
6\. Speak with the Sandman Timekeeper 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
NOTE: The timekeeper will attack you after you hail it. If, however, you do the hail while invisible, he won't attack.
You say, 'Hail, a sandman timekeeper'
a sandman timekeeper says 'You are not of the Sacred Sand. You are not counted among the number Morell has set aside for us to watch over. We must not be interfered with. You interfere; therefore, you must die.'
The sandman timekeeper attacks (difficulty on par with other trash mobs in the zone: hits for a max ~9,300).
7\. Kill three sandmen 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
The timekeeper updates this step. You'll need to kill two other sandmen.
8\. Look for a Rod of the Sandmen on the sandmen 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
9\. Bring the Rod of the Sandmen to Zinahra the Haunted 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Zinahra the Haunted says 'A unique metal rod, this? This is what was used to keep me sullen? Or, perhaps it merely focuses the power of the Sandmen on Morell Thule's behalf. No matter. As long as I hold this artifact, the Sandmen can no longer interfere with my mind, and my dreams... so I truly hope.'
10\. Acquire three buckets of Timekeeping Sand from fallen sandmen 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
11\. Deliver three buckets of Timekeeping Sand to Zinahra the Haunted 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Zinahra the Haunted says 'There aren't very many uses for this special timekeeping sand. I suppose I shall enlist the aid of the diabolists to furnish me with a magic hourglass, that I can use to protect myself from sandmen tampering with my perception of time!'
12\. Speak to to Zinahra the Haunted 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zinahra the Haunted'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'Thank you for your efforts on my behalf. No person should ever have their dreams ripped away from them. I will retain the Rod of the Sandmen in case a further use is found for it.'
You have helped the siren Zinahra determine the cause of her disturbing daydreams and visions, and provided her with several buckets of timekeeping sand and a magic artifact which she may use to concoct a defense of some sort against the entities trying to harvest her dreams.

---

_Rewards:_
158 platinum
Experience (about 1.1 AA worth at Level 90)
Your faction with Dream Delvers has been adjusted by 15.
