# Pirate's Blues

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is one of five tasks leading up to "The Staff Retreat" and the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, "Hippity Hoppity Kill".
It's a solo task that begins with Nollibene the Deckhand. He is a halfling found on the second level of the Shellscale water maze (just outside the whirlpool port to the ship).
The entrance to this area is found in the northeast part of the forest.
There are no pre-requisites for requesting this task.
You say, 'Hail, Nollibene the Deckhand'
Nollibene the Deckhand says 'Ohhh, everywhere I look... There is churning water everywhere! Friend, I am lost, and trying to get back to my ship! I am a deckhand aboard Gaitius Cloden's pirate ship, I fell into a whirlpool when the ship crashed ashore, and ended up here, wherever this is. We were tracking down a mysterious, captivating [sound] when the accident happened.'
You say, 'What sound?'
Nollibene the Deckhand says 'What was it, music? A song? A pleasant-sounding noise? I am not sure, but I know Gaitius is probably fit to be tied if the ship has been wrecked. For myself, I am not feeling strong enough yet to return through the whirlpool. I don't think I'll make it back through again without drowning. Would you be willing to [assist] Gaitius in my stead?'
You say, 'I'll assist you'
Nollibene the Deckhand says 'That would be very commendable of you. Gaitius may be just a human, but he's got a heart of gold and doesn't deserve to die wherever it is we've been stranded!'
You have been assigned the task 'Pirate's Blues'.
Go through the whirlpool, and board the pirate ship. Assist Gaitius Cloden in Nollibene's stead.
Locate the wrecked pirate ship 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Step into the whirlpool to port to the ship.
Find out if Gaitius Cloden is still aboard the vessel, and if he is willing to forgive Nollibene 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Gaitius Cloden is on the topside of the ship.
You say, 'Hail, Gaitius Cloden'
Gaitius Cloden says 'I am Gaitius Cloden, captain of the Divine Light. I gather you have met some of my miserable crew. We were charting new ocean lanes when we were blown off course by a storm. Then we encountered a strange, lovely noise, soothing and melodic. We tried to investigate its source, but soon ran aground on this island. Many of the crew have gone missing, and the rest have become insolent and disobedient. I'll tell you more, but I need your help. I would have you track down at least three of the mutineers and bring me back their heads. I saw them last standing over by the ship's wheel, when it became enchanted by 'Sailors' Darkfire'. They then vanished, and I've not seen them since.'
Kill three mutinous scallywags 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Mutinous scallywags are found all around the beach of the sand castle area (click on the ship's wheel to port there).
Take the heads of three mutinous scallywags; Cloden will need to make sure they are really dead  0/3 (Morell's Castle)
Loot three heads from the scallywags (not pre-lootable).
Bring the heads of the three mutinous scallywags to Gaitius Cloden 0/3 (Morell's Castle)
NOTE: If Gaitius Cloden is KOS when you try to interact with him, it may mean someone's failed a different task in which he's a kill target. In this case, try killing him and allowing him to respawn as a normal quest NPC.
Gaitius Cloden says 'Good. I am not a violent man, but I watched these villains push loyal members of my crew overboard into serpent-infested waters, laughing all the while. They won't be laughing any more now, will they? I hope the souls of my dead crew can rest easier now.'
Ask Gaitius Cloden about the location of the Adamantine Tube 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Gaitius Cloden'
Gaitius Cloden says 'You're doing well. Bringing me back the heads of the mutineers has had a chilling effect on the rest of my crew. I am hoping that I will soon be able to coax them into joining me, so that we may repair the ship and escape from this place... Wherever this is. However, before I can think about putting together a skeleton crew, I need you to track down the siren that brought us here. She calls herself Maiana... I know she's around here somewhere, I just can't see her. I can hear her song from time to time. The sooner you deal with her, the sooner I can break the spell she's put over my crew. Kill her, and bring me back anything she might be carrying.'
Kill Maiana the Chanteuse 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Maiana the Chanteuse is a named siren found in the northeast part of the beach. Her respawn timer is around the same as regular mobs in the zone (15-18 minutes?). She hits for a max ~9,300.
Take Maiana's Adamantine Tube 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Give the Adamantine Tube to Gaitius Cloden 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Gaitius Cloden says 'An adamantine tube, eh? No doubt a magical musical instrument of some sort. I'll keep this with me for now, until someone can think of a better use for it. In the meantime, I thank you for all you have done. If you see Nollibene, tell him I am most grateful that he sent you to help.'
Speak with Nollibene 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Nollibene the Deckhand'
Nollibene the Deckhand says 'Gaitius is alive and well? I am so glad! I was afraid when the crew began to get surly, that he might lose his life. I shall try to regain the strength to return to my ship through the whirlpool, help repair the ship, and be done with this strange, dreamlike place!'
You have done well by your new friend Nollibene, and provided aid to Gaitius Cloden, who will hopefully be more careful who he hires to be the crew on his next voyage, assuming he makes it home alive.
158 platinum
Experience (about 2 AAs worth)
Faction with Dream Delvers
20 Dream MotesRewards:
- Dream Mote [item=90255]

Entered: Mon Nov 22 22:49:09 2010
Modified: Wed Jan 29 09:23:32 2025 | | This is a solo task that leads into the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, Hippity Hoppity Kill [quest=5244]. Completion of this task also allows you to participate in the "Morell Ascendant" task in Sanctum Somnium (the end group mission in the House of Thule expansion).
In order to request this task, you must have completed the other five Morell's Castle Partisan tasks:
\- Gouzah Safari
\- Pirate's Blues
\- A Few Buckets of Sand
\- Satyrical Riddles
\- Suspicously Sweet Treats
Speak with Twereus Ulithain [npc=36793] at the zone-in of Morell's Castle.
You say, 'Hail, Twereus Ulithain'
Twereus Ulithain says 'A sinister shadow has fallen across the world, and across the planes. I have ventured long to meet the god Morell Thule, yet having sacrificed so much to finally make his acquaintance, I was shocked at what I [found].'
You say, 'What was found?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'I know Morell's nature, from the way I was taught and raised, to the divine presence I feel when I speak his name and pray to him. I have ventured into this reality at great personal cost, I have dared to seek an audience with Morell, and know his mind. There is no danger greater than a god which has become deranged, and yet this is precisely what has happened to Morell. I do not know how this came to pass, but in my brief conversation with him I did indeed sense that his essence - his personality, if you will - has been drastically [altered].'
You say, 'Altered?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'It seemed to happen days ago - or has it been weeks? It is so hard to tell, especially in this place where the line between dreams and reality fade away and become meaningless. Yet something profoundly upsetting has occurred in Morell Thule. He speaks as if he were master of more than dreams, and a corruption of his former self. He has undergone a fearful mutation of his godlike idiom, and this can only harbor ill for all of us. His [powers] have increased, and were used against me. It was only when I was filled with fear and begging for my life did he forbear from destroying me.'
You say, 'His powers?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'I have never felt so cold and sick; Morell's powers, even restrained as they were with me, are truly immense even by godlike standards. Now, it is enough for you to know that Morell has changed. Perhaps you have already pieced together some of the story... it matters not. All that matters is that he be [restored] to his original level of power, so that he does not descend into megalomania.'
You say, 'Restored?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'In my travels, I have retrieved a tome from one of the locales in time reachable through Zebuxoruk's void portals. Perhaps you have heard of them. In this tome, which comes from the distant future, methods for the alteration of godlike beings are discussed. One of these may give us the power to strip this added essence which Morell has absorbed. I believe I can construct a magical staff that can create and focus this power, using [materials] which I believe are at hand, if the rumors I've been hearing from these local creatures are true.'
You say, 'What materials?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'You will need to bring me the Rings of Insight that you gave to the dwarf Griklund. These will provide mana-focusing to the finished staff. I must also have the Gem of Planar Teleportation which was given to Gurroth Aberrine; this I can tweak to form the heart of the essence extractor. Then retrieve the Adamantine Tube from the pirate Gaitius Cloden; I shall fashion that into a cone of discharger tines. I will also make use of the Rod of the Sandmen now in the possession of Zinahra the siren-woman, and the banshee Zairia should be able to contribute a Pure Dream Fragment since the pure ones add the wrong taste to whatever kind of stew it is that she eats. If you will [help] me by bringing these items, I can travel in time to my workshop back home, and fashion the Staff of Arcane Dispossession you will be needing to restore Morell Thule to normal.'
You say, 'I will help'
Twereus Ulithain says 'I am glad there are still some adventurers of your caliber committed to the well-being of Norrath and the Planes. We need more like you. Succeed in this task, prove yourselves worthy, and perhaps you will be ready to hear the rest of my story.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Staff Retreat'.
Twereus Ulithain sees grave danger if Morell Thule retains the additional godlike essence he has absorbed. The only way to prevent him from destabilizing the Planes of Power or disrupting the pantheon will be to strip him of this added essence.
Collect the components Twereus says he needs to construct a magic staff. Ironically, you have held each of them in your hands already at some time or another, it will just be a matter of retrieving them from the people you gave them to.

---

1\. Retrieve the Rings of Insight from Griklund the Satyrist 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Griklund the Satyrist'
Griklund the Satyrist says 'Aye, I've still got the Rings of Insight, and I'm using them, too. What's that? I suppose I could let you borrow them, but I'd be obliged if you'd bring them back.'
2\. Retrieve the Teleportation Gem from Gurroth Aberrine 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Gurroth Aberrine'
Gurroth Aberrine says 'Yes, the Teleportation Gem, I still have it, why? I see. If you promise to come back sometime and bring me more rabbit meat, why not. Here it is.'
3\. Retrieve the Rod of the Sandmen from Zinahra the Haunted 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zinahra the Haunted'
Zinahra the Haunted says 'We are done examining the Rod of the Sandmen; you may have it back, but you must promise never to let the Sandmen have it.'
4\. Retrieve the Pure Dream Fragment from Zairia 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Zairia'
Zairia says 'Er, I think I have one of those. Can't use these 'pure' dream fragments in my stew, they make me sick. Here's one; help yourself to it. Now get out of my sight, you're scaring away the little ones.'
5\. Retrieve the Adamantine Tube from Gaitius Cloden 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Gaitius Cloden'
Gaitius Cloden says 'Hah, it's you again. Got tired of watching us trying to patch the hole in my ship with siren guts, have ya? Oh. Yes, I do still have the Adamantine Tube. Oh, ya want it? Well you are not gonna get it without a fight!'
Gaitius Cloden points its dagger in your direction and lets out an "arrrg!" before charging.
Gaitius Cloden attacks. He hits for a max ~8,800 and is immune to mez and stun.
NOTE: Only one person can do this step at a time, so if multiple people in your group are at this point, make sure only one person does the hail. He has a fast respawn.
6\. If you're not able to kill Gaitius after hailing him, he may reset to his non-aggro state. In this case, you'll have to repeat the task again.
7\. Defeat Gaitius Cloden 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Task Window Says:_ Unfortunately Gaitius Cloden won't give up the Adamantine Tube without a fight. Defeat him!
Gaitius Cloden has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Gaitius Cloden's corpse falls to the ground, landing directly on the dagger it once wielded.
8\. Loot the Adamantine Tube from Gaitius Cloden 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
9\. Give the Teleportation Gem to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Twereus Ulithain says 'Thank you. This Teleportation Gem will form the source of the staff's power.'
10\. Give the Rings of Insight to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Twereus Ulithain says 'Perfect. These rings will act as harmonic mana focusing components for the staff we're building.'
11\. Give the Adamantine Tube to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Twereus Ulithain says 'The Adamantine Tube, properly modified, will provide the discharger tines for the completed magic staff.'
12\. Give the Rod of the Sandmen to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Twereus Ulithain says 'The Rod of the Sandmen will form the shaft of the Staff. I'll add an appropriate insulating grip to it.'
13\. Give the Pure Dream Fragment to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
Twereus Ulithain says 'At last, the Pure Dream Fragment. A chunk of raw living magic, naturally attuned to Morell's lifeforce! I will imbue the power of this item into the staff, to repel Morell's essence even as the disfiguring essence is pulled away from him. Now I must go to my workshop - twelve hours in the past - and build the Staff of Arcane Dispossession. Then I shall return here, only a moment after I left, with the Staff in hand. It will be like I never left.'
14\. Speak with Twereus Ulithain; find out if he has finished making the Staff of Arcane Dispossession 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Twereus Ulithain'
Twereus Ulithain says 'There. The staff is ready. I am giving this to you, hoping that you are as knowledgeable and as able as you say you are. Morell will probably not allow you close enough to use this on him without a fight. But remember - please - you are not trying to destroy him. No matter how angry he makes you, you must not destroy him. One god has already been destroyed in this place, that is how this whole dilemma began!'
You have successfully gathered the components necessary to allow Twereus to build an enchanted staff that you might be able to use to restore Morell Thule to his proper level of power.
_Rewards:_
158 platinum
Experience (about 1.5 AAs worth at Level 90)
Faction with Dream Delvers
20 Dream Motes [item=90255]
You also receive a Staff of Arcane Dispossession [item=94216] (required in the second Sanctum Somnium mission, Morell Ascendant [quest=5249], as well as the raid event of the same name).
Completion of this task leads into the group progression mission for Morell's Castle, Hippity Hoppity Kill [quest=5244].
- Dream Mote [item=90255]
- Staff of Arcane Dispossession [item=94216]

You say, 'Hail, Braelin Arden'
Braelin Arden says 'You have to help me! [They] will not live much longer if you do not help.'
You say, 'Who is they?'
Braelin Arden says 'My friends! That [girl], but not really a girl, took them!'
You say, 'What girl?'
Braelin Arden says 'An evil thing she is! There is no communicating with her, no clear logic in which she [speaks]. She does things with no rhyme or reason!'
You say, 'Speaks?'
Braelin Arden says 'Imagine giving more power than you can think of, to a child! You would then know what insanity is... You would then know the whims of a [demon]!'
You say, 'A demon?'
Braelin Arden says 'Will you [help] then?'
You say, 'I'll help'
Braelin Arden says 'I thank you, as no doubt my friends will when you free them. Let me know when you are [ready] so that I may send you to that wretched girl's location.'
You have been assigned the task 'Hippity Hoppity Kill'.
Braelin Arden asks you to help him retrieve his friends who have been taken by the demon Alyss. She will not release them until she has the bunnies she so desperately wants!

---

Talk to Alyss 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_A Bellikos named Alyss is found in the southwest corner of the forest._
You say, 'Hail, Alyss'
Alyss says '[Hello]!'
You say, 'Hello...'
Alyss says 'Are you here to [play] with me?! It has been a very long time since I had someone to play with.'
You say, 'I'm not here to play'
Alyss says 'I don't think I like you. You're not going to play with me! Just like the [others]!'
You say, 'Others?'
Alyss says 'You don't need to know what happened to them. If they won't play with me, then they cannot play with anyone! That's only [fair]!'
You say, 'Fair?'
Alyss says 'Oh, so now you want to be nice. Okay, I'll tell you what. Bring me some of soft and fluffy fur of those candied [bunnies] and I'll let your friends come out and play. I do not like those nasty rabid rabbit things though. So kill them so that they do not infect the others.'
You say, 'What bunnies?'
Alyss says 'I would get them myself, but that evil witch, Haezra, is too [scary]! She'll cook me in her oven and eat me if I set foot over there!'
You say, 'Scary?'
Alyss says 'It doesn't matter what I am! And don't make fun of me! Now, bring me those little bunnies!'
_Nearby, you'll see "an unknown entity" (you don't actually have to talk to it, but if you do...):_
You say, 'Hail, an unknown entity'
an unknown entity says 'Ti si a eil! Od ton tsurt reh!'
an unknown entity says 'Uoy era gnillaf otni a part!'
_(Translation: "It is a lie! Do not trust her!"; "You are falling into a trap!")_
Retrieve 4 candied bunny furs 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Kill those foul rabid rabbits 0/10 (Morell's Castle)
_You'll find rabid rabbits and candied bunnies in the candyland area a short distance away._
_NOTE: You'll have a banshee called "Haezra" spawn when you loot a Candied Bunny Fur. She'll keep spawning in this manner until you kill her while the next step in the task is open to you (at which point she'll stop appearing)._
Defeat the Haezra 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_The Haezra spawn is triggered by the looting of a candied bunny fur. She hits for a max ~8,500._
Deliver 4 candied bunny furs to Alyss 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Alyss says 'Oooooo! Look how soft! Gimme, gimme, [gimme]!'
You say, 'Gimme'
Alyss says 'What [people]?'
You say, 'People?'
Alyss says 'I don't think I wanna play with you anymore!'
_She attacks as "Playful Alyss"._
Defeat Playful Alyss 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_At 50%, she goes inactive and says:_
Playful Alyss says 'Hahahaha! It doesn't matter what you do, you know. You will never find them, and I will have you to play with forever!'
_The four "Find..." steps open up. You'll find these NPCs at these locations (for your morellcastle.txt map file):_
P -1085.0000, -2055.0000, 95.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, tergan
P -435.0000, -2210.0000, 98.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, nalder
P 70.0000, -1600.0000, 290.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, thaelin
P 435.0000, -525.0000, 3.0000, 0, 0, 0, 1, saena
_In-game /locations:_
Tergan: 2055, 1085, 95 (forest)
Nalder: 2210, 435, 98 (forest)
Thaelin: 1600, -70, 290 (shellscale area)
Saena: 525, -435, 3 (the sand pile connected to the marionette room)
_WARNING: Four mephits spawn around each of these NPCs and attack right after you hail them. They each hit for a max ~9,000._
_You can avoid their aggro by doing the hails while invisible._
Find Tergan Norwath 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Tergan Norwath'
Tergan Norwath says 'You've come for me? Thank you! I must get back! I must get back!'
Kill Alyss' blue imps 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Find Nalder Rallin 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Nalder Rallin'
Nalder Rallin says 'I told them you'd come for me. I told them! '
Kill Alyss' green imps 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Find Thaelin Mhorna 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Thaelin Mhorna'
Thaelin Mhorna says 'It's about time! I didn't think I could wait anymore! I... Uh... I guess I'll head back home now. Thanks.'
Kill Alyss' red imps 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Find Saena Kinde 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Saena Kinde'
Saena Kinde says 'Braelin sent you, did he? I guess I should be grateful that he sent someone so... capable. I'll have to thank him when I get back. Bye now.'
Kill Alyss' orange imps 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Speak to Alyss 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_WARNING: Speaking to Alyss here will turn her into "Creepy Alyss". Also, the four NPCs around her turn into demons and attack along with her. They all summon at 100% health._
You say, 'Hail, Alyss'
Alyss says 'I'll have to thank Braelin! You're the best one yet! Most, so-called adventurers, don't last nearly as long as you have. But, it is time to go now. Let's wish our good friend here a happy goodbye!'
Defeat Creepy Alyss 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
_Creepy Alyss hits for a max ~12,500 and has a lot of hitpoints. The demons each hit for a max ~8,000. You don't have to kill the demons, however they do not despawn._
_After defeating Alyss, the unknown entity turns into "Piper Nasscul". The task locks._
Speak to Piper Nasscul 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Piper Nasscul'
Piper Nasscul says 'I tried to warn you! Her power kept me from communicatiing more clearly to you earlier. Here's a chest of things she brought with. You're welcome to anything you find inside. I will help you get out of here. Just let me know when you'd like to go [back].'
Collect your reward from Alyss' chest 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You have defeated Alyss and put an end to her games.

Modified: Wed Jan 29 10:11:56 2025 | | _This is the first of three solo tasks in a series that begins with Sage Jarith in Sanctum Somnium. He's an Erudite that wanders around the entrance area of the zone. Beware of KOS mobs in his vicinity while talking to him._
You say, 'Hail, Sage Jarith'
Sage Jarith says 'Morell has us trapped here, but I believe there is a way out. I have a [plan]. If it is going to work though, I'll need your help, as well as any other captives in this bizarre dream world.'
You say, 'What plan is that?'
Sage Jarith says 'First, we'll need to [search] this tower and make contact with the rest of the people trapped here. Next, I'll need your help gathering components for a [device] that can dispel some of Morell's magic and hopefully weaken him. Finally, we'll use the device near that locked [tower] that seems so important. I have a feeling that's where Morell is hiding.'
You say, 'What about this tower?'
Sage Jarith says 'Oh, we aren't ready for the tower yet. We'll need to [search] the area first, and then build the [device].'
You say, 'What device?'
Sage Jarith says 'We can't build the device yet, we'll need to [search] the area first.'
You say, 'Let's search it then!'
Sage Jarith says 'Good, you'd like to get started? I'll do what I can from here and prepare the spells needed. You find any other trapped individuals you can, and tell me all you can find out about the rest of this horrid place.'
You have been assigned the task 'Morell's Sanctuary'.
You've been asked to scour Morell's realm for other trapped dreamers. Find them and then convince them of their situation, and that they can escape if everyone works together.

---

_You'll want the following locations for your "somnium.txt" map file:_
P -75.0000, 80.0000, -5.0000, 0, 0, 0, 2, Saeger

_Or for in-game /locs:_
Seager: -80, 75, -5 _(balcony across from the zone-in)_
Fareth: 10, 430, -24 _(portal at this location; Fareth is in the port-up area)_
Rommen: -145, 305, -25 _(treasure halls west of the zone-in)_
Dennen: -450, 30, 35 _(en route to Morell's Tower, south of the zone-in)_
Mara: 520, 665, -21 _(down a ladder in a west-central room in the undead area)_
Cort: 10, -290, -25 _(entrance area of the castle; east from the zone-in area)_
Search the entrance hall balconies 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Seager the Locksmith 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Seager the Locksmith'
Seager the Locksmith says 'Am I glad to see a friendly face! I'm about to drop dead from fright. I can't find my way out of here, and none of my tools or keys can help if I can't find a proper passage! So you say you are with one of those magic-user types that can help us get out of here? By all means, then, let me know if I can help.'
Search the filthy, rundown village 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Fareth 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Fareth'
Fareth says 'As if my life wasn't bad enough, I tell you. I found someplace nice to go to sleep and woke up in this place. You're trying to get out of here? If you find a way, please let me know. I just can't take this anymore.'
Search the halls filled with treasure 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Rommen Steelforge 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Rommen Steelforge'
Rommen Steelforge says 'Some dark magic is at work here, I tell ya. This gold isn't real. Never seen anything like this in my life, I'll tell you that, and I've seen a lot. I may be tough, but I'm tough in business. I'm a merchant, not a fighter. So if you'd be so kind, I'd appreciate a hand out of here. If you think you've found a way, just let me know if there is anything I can do.'
Search the path to Morell's tower 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Dennen Marol 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Dennen Marol'
Dennen Marol says 'I know I shouldn't have meddled in necromancy! This plane is my punishment, I fear! They shall make me suffer before I die. What? You think you can get us out? I'd be forever grateful! My magic is at your command.'
Search the western underground maze-like dungeon entrance 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Mara O`Rane 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Mara O`Rane'
Mara O`Rane says 'I'm a big fan o` fighting, but even I'm getting a bit tired here. I can't seem to find my way out of these creepy tunnels. You say you can get us out of here? I'm at your service then. Just let me know if you need a hand. I want to go back home.'
Search the exquisite castle 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find Cort Landerson 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Cort Landerson'
Cort Landerson says 'This is unacceptable! I've never dealt with such irrational fools. Surely, we are in a bad situation. But that is no excuse to panic. The blood in my veins is noble, not that one of a frantic commoner. Ah, I see that you think you know a way out. Well if you find one, inform me. If I can help in anyway, do not hesitate to ask. Good teamwork is important, after all.'
Defeat the tower wardens that guard the way 0/2 (Sanctum Somnium)
_Any two wardens will update this step._
Return to Jarith 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Jarith'
Sage Jarith says 'Very interesting. We may be able to use their help later, thankfully it sounded as if most were cooperative. I don't think we can do this ourselves. Now, based on the information you provided me of this area, I am going to start building the device to get us all out of here. I will need your help, of course.'
You've made contact with the trapped dreamers and done what you've can to get them to work together.
_Rewards:_
158 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
20 Dream Motes

Get tools from Seager the Locksmith and give them to Jarith 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)

Get necessary potions from Rommen and give them to Jarith 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)

Jarith's actions have caused Morell to retaliate. Jarith believes that if the forces Morell has sent can be destroyed, Morell's power will significantly weaken.

---

Get the device from Jarith and bring it near the tower 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Jarith'
Sage Jarith says 'Here you are. Please don't lose or destroy it. Do you know how difficult this was to make? Good luck!'
_With "Sage's Dispel Device" in hand, walk towards Morell's tower in the southern part of the zone (nothing will happen to you; you can do this while invis)._
Defeat the tower wardens 0/3 (Sanctum Somnium)
Defeat the tower servants 0/3 (Sanctum Somnium)
Defeat the tower guardians 0/3 (Sanctum Somnium)
_Any wardens, servants, and guardians in the zone will update the above three steps._
Defeat Morell's Commander 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
_The Commander spawns outside the door to Morell's tower. He's a fairly straightforward fight. If you can handle trash in the zone, this will be simple enough._
Morell`s Commander has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Morell`s Commander's corpse lets out a death rattle and crumples to the floor.
Speak with Jarith 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Jarith'
Sage Jarith says 'As expected, you've done it. My knowledge and your power are a potent combination, my friend. Can't you feel it? Morell is quite distracted thanks to my device and your killing of a powerful creature he created. I'm sure you'll be able to access the tower now, but I must gather the others and escape here, now that we can. If you intended to face Morell, I wish you the best of luck.'
Morell's dream world is starting to shudder, and he has lost control over most of the dreamers. Jarith and the others can escape now, but Morell still resides in his tower.
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience (about 2 AAs worth)
Faction with Dream Delvers
27 Dream Motes
_Completion of this task leads into the first Sanctum Somnium mission, "Morell's Challenge"._

Modified: Sat Sep 28 07:30:14 2024 | | _This is a group mission that begins with Buck Witherspoon in Sanctum Somnium. He's a centaur located on the balcony across from the zone-in._
_You must have completed Sage Jarith's three solo tasks prior to being able to request this one._
You say, 'Hail, Buck Witherspoon'
Buck Witherspoon says 'It is time again for Morell's challenge! Are you up to it? Riches can be yours if you complete this simple [challenge]!'
You say, 'What challenge?'
Buck Witherspoon says 'It's quite straight forward really. All you have to do is race through the tower finding beacons and fighting some creatures along the way. Think you are up to [taking] the challenge?'
You say, 'I'm up for taking it on'
Buck Witherspoon says 'I knew you were a champ! When you are ready to [start], let me know!'
You have been assigned the task 'Morell's Challenge'.
You have entered Morell's Challenge, a deadly but possibly profitable race through the castle. Follow the green beacons to stay on track, but beware that some areas do not have green beacons and you will need to find your way through those locations on your own. Just make sure not to let the purple cloud of ultimate darkness catch up to you!
_NOTE: The death touch "mob" and teleporters in this task can only be seen if you have NPC spell particle effects turned on. These are in the Options-Display menu and must be turned on before you zone in._
_Say "start" to zone in._
You say, 'Ready to start...'
Buck Witherspoon says 'This is going to be so much fun!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

Speak with Buck Witherspoon to start the challenge 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
_NOTE: Make sure to follow all of Buck Witherspoon's dialogues here, otherwise the mission may get bugged._
You say, 'Hail, Buck Witherspoon'
Buck Witherspoon says 'Ah yes, you probably wish to know the rules. Your task is to race through the tower. Along the way you will battle horrible monsters and have to find different colored beacons. The green beacons along the way will show you the correct path to take. Unfortunately the green beacons don't appear in every section of the tower, so you will have to find your own way through those areas on your own. You may have to make use of teleport areas to do so or to avoid hazards. That is where most people die a most unpleasant death, but I think you have the spunk to make it through! Oh, I almost forgot one more teeny tiny [detail].'
You say, 'Tiny detail?'
Buck Witherspoon says 'Legalities and such mandate that I tell you about the cloud of ultimate death. Quite simply, it will be slowly creeping up behind you during the race. You may want to avoid it unless you want the flesh to melt off your bones. I am also legally bound to let you know that if you wish to chicken out and [leave], to let me know. If you are brave and want a chance to win some fabulous prizes however, we can begin when you are [ready].'
You say, 'Ready!'
Buck Witherspoon says 'Let the games begin!'
_Head through the treasure halls and use a teleporter to port to the castle area. The teleporter is found in one of the dead-ends of these halls._
Find and defeat Yngvar the giant 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find and defeat Otraff the centaur 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find and defeat Dreamfeeder the shade 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find and defeat a Champion of Morell, the beefeater 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
_The above-four nameds are found in the four rooms surrounding the courtyard. They each hit for a max ~14,000 and have no special abilities (no flurry, rampage, or enrage). They have a lot of hitpoints (about 1.35 million worth) and all see invisible._
_WARNING: The DT cloud may be present in this area, so you'll want to keep an eye out for that._
_After the last named is dead, a teleporter appears next to the fountain. Use it to port to the undead area._
Find the orange beacon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find the blue beacon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find the red beacon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find the purple beacon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Find the yellow beacon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
_The beacons appear as orbs on the ground and are found in different locations throughout the area. You just need to walk over them in order to update these steps._

Speak with Buck Witherspoon 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)

You say, 'Hail, Buck Witherspoon'
Buck Witherspoon says 'We have a winner! You are now one of the proud few who have survived Morell's Challenge and not ended up getting destroyed by the cloud of ultimate darkness! I promised you a reward if you lived through the challenge, so make sure to loot the treasure chest for your reward before you [leave]!'

# Morell Ascendant

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This is a group mission that begins with Twereus Ulithain in Sanctum Somnium. He's found in the northwest corner of the large, open room at the entrance.
NOTE: You'll need a Staff of Arcane Dispossession [item=94216] (received from The Staff Retreat [quest=5243] task) in order to complete this task.
In order to request this mission, you must have completed:
-- All six partisan tasks for Morell's Castle:
------ Pirate's Blues
------ Suspiciously Sweet Treats
------ Gouzah Safari
------ A Few Buckets of Sand
------ Satyrical Riddles
------ The Staff Retreat
-- Sage Jarith's three tasks:
------ Morell's Sanctuary
------ Dreamland Assault
------ The Key to the Tower
-- Morell's Challenge (the first group mission for the zone)
You say, 'Hail, Twereus Ulithain'
Twereus Ulithain says 'You have come! Have you brought the [Staff] I gave you?'
You say, 'I have the staff'
Twereus Ulithain says 'Perfect. There is still time. I can tell you more of what I know, though it isn't much. Know that this place is a kind of trap for gods. I don't know the true nature of this place, but you must be aware by now that Cazic-Thule has been defeated, or perhaps permanently destroyed, elsewhere within the confines of this... planar cloister. Whatever this place really is, it seems to be a place where [gods] can be destroyed, and their defining essences stolen by other gods.'
You say, 'How can gods be destroyed?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'There is much I do not know, but I do know that the permanent destruction of a god should not be possible. The fact that a god's very essence could be [stolen] here, says a great deal about the dangers of this place.'
You say, 'Stolen?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'I will say that my interest in Morell-Thule has led me to try and follow him and to learn what he is up to since Cazic Thule was killed. It is clear that he has changed. When Cazic Thule was defeated, his godlike essences were ripped from him and some of these were apparently absorbed by Morell. Thus, I sent you on the quest to build the Staff of Arcane Dispossession. If it is true that Morell has absorbed the godlike power of his father Cazic, then he has become too [powerful] to coexist with the rest of the pantheon.'
You say, 'Too powerful?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'This additional essence he has collected in the aftermath of the death of Cazic Thule will have to be stripped from Morell, probably by force, before it becomes too late. If Morell escapes from this place with such enhanced [powers], it could mean devastating shifts in the alignment of certain Planes, and possibly a war between the gods of the pantheon. A war that may well signal the end of life as we know it, on Norrath and elsewhere in the universe.'
You say, 'What do we do about these powers?'
Twereus Ulithain says 'Thus our course of [action] is clear, just as it must be clear to you that we are not going to destroy Morell, but merely weaken him so that the Staff can divest him of the essence he has absorbed, before it merges with his energy completely and irrevocably.'
You say, 'What's our course of action?'
Without pre-requisites:
Twereus Ulithain says 'I'm sorry, friend. You are not yet ready for the final confrontation with Morell Thule. First you must do more to understand the inner workings of the Sanctum Somnium before I risk bringing you face to face with the god of dreams.'
With pre-requisites:
Twereus Ulithain says 'When you are ready, I will [lead] you to the gazebo where Morell has confined himself. Before we go, make certain you have the Staff of Arcane Dispossession. It is the only thing that may transform him back into his original state.'
You have been assigned the task 'Morell Ascendant'.
Absorbing the power of Cazic Thule has left the god of dreams in an unstable state. Stirring with power and corruption, Morell Thule is on the brink of madness and must be stopped.
Say "lead" to zone into the instance.
You say, 'Lead the way...'
Twereus Ulithain says 'Let's not waste anymore time. I'll show you the way.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
1. Speak to Twereus Ulithain 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
2. Confront Morell Thule, God of Dreams 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
3. Defeat the dreams surrounding Morell Thule 0/6 (Sanctum Somnium)
4. Speak to Morell Thule, God of Dreams 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
5. Defeat Morell Thule, God of Dreams 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Fight Morell Thule [npc=37088] until about 45% at which point he despawns.
6. Defeat many of Morell Thule's minions 0/8 (Sanctum Somnium)
Kill the trash that spawns.
NOTE: The task locks after you complete this step.
7. Defeat Morell Thule, God of Dread 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
There will be an emote to click your Staff of Arcane Dispossession [item=94216] (around 20% to 10% health). If you don't do this, he'll start death touching.
8. Use the Staff of Arcane Dispossession on Morell Thule 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
9. Observe the strange occurrences at the gazebo 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
10. Speak to Morell Thule once he is well again 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
11. Claim your reward from the Chest of Dreams 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
Task Rewards:
210 platinum
Experience

700, 470, 50 _(inside temple; a few floors down from top)_
Sharuleth & Shade:
915, 435, 50 _(very bottom of the temple)_
Tiseth the Thrall:
810, 425, 215 _(top of the temple)_

Speak to Sherri Ganes inside the temple 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Sherri Ganes'
Sherri Ganes says 'That lunatic iskar, Tiseth the Thrall, boasts that she can get out of here. She has some sort of artifact that is strengthening the Plane of Fear's denziens. I'd love to go have a word with her, but I'm afraid we'll need your help. It's all I can do to keep these amygdalans at bay.'
Speak to Jaris Calmur inside the temple 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Jaris Calmur'
Jaris Calmur says 'There is a dark force deep inside this temple, and I'm afraid Tiseth's artifact is the key to defeating it for good. When I killed it, it just came back to life. You should be able to confront her to get the artifact. Once you do, you'll need to destroy the shade of Cazic Thule that resides here.'
Defeat amygdalan warriors 0/3 (Fear Itself)
Defeat amygdalan knights 0/3 (Fear Itself)
Interrogate Tiseth the Thrall, take the Shard, and hold onto it 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_Task Window Says:_ Interrogate Tiseth the Thrall and steal her artifact before she can do anymore damage.
_Tiseth is found at the top of the temple._
You say, 'Hail, Tiseth the Thrall'
Tiseth the Thrall says 'Stay back! The Shard of Thule's Nightmare is mine! I refuse to let this paradise end! What are you doing? Unhand me! Give me that back! The master's shade cannot be destroyed so easily, even if you steal my shard!'
_You receive a Shard of Thule's Nightmare on your cursor._
Defeat the Shade of Cazic Thule 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_Task Window Says:_ Steal the artifact. With the shard in your possession, you should be able to destroy the Shade of Cazic Thule inside the temple permanently.
_The Shade of Cazic Thule spawns at the very bottom of the temple._
_It's a Shadow Knight, hits for a max ~7,600, has a lot of hitpoints, and has a very fast respawn (within a minute or two)._
Shade of Cazic Thule has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Shade of Cazic Thule's corpse gasps for air, choking out one final breath.
Give the Shard of Thule's Nightmare to Reena 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Speak with Reena 0/1 (Fear Itself)
_Task Window Says:_ The shard you took from Tiseth has lost much of its luster. You should talk to Reena about it and give her the shard.
You say, 'Hail, Reena Kedson'
Reena Kedson says 'Excellent work! By destroying that echo of Cazic Thule, this shard you gave me has lost most of its power. I think we can destroy it completely. I don't know for certain if it will allow us to escape, but it should weaken the dark forces at work in this Plane. Which, I'm beginning to think, isn't the real Plane of Fear.'
You've destroyed the shard of Cazic Thule and have made this image of the Plane of Fear safer. Even if Reena and her friends cannot find a way out of this dream, their time trapped will be less dangerous.
_Rewards:_
210 platinum (about 3 AAs)
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers

Modified: Sun Aug 8 14:41:48 2021 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Rashan Spiritweaver in Fear Itself. He is in an NPC camp right next to the zone-in area._
You say, 'Hail, Rashan Spiritweaver'
Rashan Spiritweaver seems to be lost in a chant as he leans over the faintly breathing woman before him. A seasoned adventurer would recognize his incantations as being an attempt to cleanse [poison].
You say, 'What kind of poison did this?'
Rashan Spiritweaver stops as he's awoken from his trance and looks up at \_\_\_\_\_. 'Spider venom, specifically. She took ill from the venom of the spinechillers. It keeps [transforming] and moving.' He lowers his head.
You say, 'Transforming?'
Rashan Spiritweaver closes his eyes from the pain, but a tear soaks the fur beneath them. 'On entry into her system, the venom keeps morphing, making it difficult to treat. Just as I think I have saved her, it appears it has merely changed constitution.' He looks back up at \_\_\_\_\_ and inquires, 'The only hope for her are pure samples of the venom that has infected her, those that have not yet been injected in flesh. I could study it and try to find a way to stop the transformations or anticipate the next forms. It's a lot to ask with how dire the situation is here, but would you [retrieve some] to save her?'
You say, 'I will retrieve some'
Rashan Spiritweaver places a hand on your arm and nods. 'Thank you... May your ancestors guide you, friend. Return safe.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Antidote Within'.
A transforming poison was found to be slowly tearing apart Iyana T`vre and Rashan Spiritweaver has been doing all he can to help her as she suffers. However, what he cannot do while he tends to her is obtain some samples of pre-injection venom from the spinechiller spiders to examine them more completely. He has asked for you to do so in his place.

---

Loot a few spinechiller venom samples 0/4 (Fear Itself)

_Note: These are not pre-lootable._
Return the samples to Rashan 0/1 (Fear Itself)
Rashan Spiritweaver says, 'You couldn't have come a moment too soon. Every moment without the antidote, is a moment closer to her death. I'll get started looking at these samples immediately. Thank you.'

This is a solo kill task that begins with Hildran Heartguard in Fear Itself. He is found in an NPC camp on the eastern zone boundary in the northeast part of the zone.
You say, 'Hail, Hildran Heartguard'
Hildran Heartguard says 'For once, I'm truly [scared], _____.  What is going on here?'
You say, 'Why are you scared?'
Hildran Heartguard says 'The place of Fear is terrorizing today.  We've been here before, fought many times against the beings here, but I'm not quite sure how we're in the position we are today.  We've lost many friends on the battlefield, including our cleric, and it may be looking like a complete loss.  The [undead] have been insistant on taking our camp, leaving us trying to fend them off when we can.  Of course, there is also my personal [phobias] begining to play out here, causing an unwary grip on my sword.  I had to switch to my two-hander for the sake of stability.'
You say, 'What about the undead?'
Hildran Heartguard says 'The forces of undead have unfortunately been a bit too active here.  There are few camps left with others in tatters, and the undead seem to want to add another notch in that respect.  If communication wasn't down between us, I'd try to rally support to [get rid of] the creeps.'
You say, 'I'll get rid of them'
Hildran Heartguard says 'Would you [do it] for us?  Any chance to further the survival of the group would be appreciative.'
You say, 'I'll do it'
Hildran Heartguard says 'Thank you very much.  We do appreciate it.  Find the turmoil toads and worry wraiths.  They seem to be the most problematic lately.'
You have been assigned the task 'Encroaching Undeath'.
Camp warrior Hildran Heartguard is afraid the undead are coming up on the camp, and there are few camps left to defend. He's asked that you rid the area of turmoil toads and worry wraiths for the safety of the camp.
Kill a few worry wraiths 0/3 (Fear Itself)
Kill a few turmoil toads 0/3 (Fear Itself)
Report back to Hildran Heartguard 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Hildran Heartguard'
Hildran Heartguard smiles and gives you a pat on the back. 'You have no idea how much this means to us.  Thank you, adventurer.'

This is a solo kill task that begins with Morae Windwatcher in Fear Itself. She is found in an NPC camp on the eastern zone boundary in the northeast part of the zone.
You say, 'Hail, Morae Windwatcher'
Morae Windwatcher says 'I'm sorry. You've probably lost friends here in the Plane of Fear, as well.  Do you come from another camp?  No matter; you are welcome here.  I've been observing [the area], to try to determine a way out, or to re-establish communication with anyone else.'
You say, 'What's going on in the area?'
Morae Windwatcher says 'I fear problems with the [undead] that are encroaching on our camp.  There is also a [group of creatures] that have blocked us from progressing here.  Your assistance would be so great.'
You say, 'What group of creatures?'
Morae Windwatcher frowns. 'Gorgons, thought bleeders, nightmares...  They overwhelm us.  We're unable to move forward.  It would greatly help us if you're able to break them up.  If you run across any, if it's not too much to ask, please [wipe them out]?'
You say, 'I'll wipe them out'
Morae Windwatcher says 'You're too kind to a druid.  My thanks.  May the Mother protect you.'
You have been assigned the task 'Overwhelming Odds'.
Morae Windwatcher feels that several denizens of the Plane of Fear have blocked the path of their group, and requests you assistance.
Kill a few gorgons 0/5 (Fear Itself)
Kill a few thought bleeders 0/2 (Fear Itself)
Kill a few nightmares 0/3 (Fear Itself)
Gorgons are found most commonly in the northwest part of the zone while thought bleeders are found most commonly in the east-northeast. Nightmares can be found in most areas of the zone.
Report back to Morae 0/1 (Fear Itself)
You say, 'Hail, Morae Windwatcher'
Morae Windwatcher bows her head gently.  'Thank you, adventurer.  Your selflessness is appreciated well beyond what we can offer.  Just... Thank you so much.'

You say, 'A piece of paper?'
You pick up and flatten out what appears to be a good-bye letter to his family, which never made it to them.  Some of the words cannot be made out due to being covered by a thick black liquid similar to the consistancy of the lurkers about.  However, looking back to the body, you also notice the [claw marks] covering his extremities.
You say, 'Claw marks?'
The marks on the body seem as though they've been created by an animal, although experience of making it into the nightmare this far, it had to have been a mephit.  Void of other emotions in the coldness of the Palace, this piece of paper shows significant life.  Could it be a [calling]?
You say, 'A calling?'
Looking back to the paper once more, you're overcome with the need to enact revenge on those who slaughtered this man.
You have been assigned the task 'A Shred of Emotion'.
A maimed erudite body on the floor of the palace appears more tattered than most, and a note crunched within his grasp caught your attention. An overwhelming sense of revenge washing over, you're now driven to kill shadow mephits and shadowy lurkers within the palace so that he may rest.
Slaughter a few shadowy lurkers 0/5 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Slaughter a few shadow mephits 0/5 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Check on the maimed erudite 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, a maimed erudite'
The body of the maimed erudite remains there, cold, but there is a sense of peace at having fought for his honor.
Experience (about 1 AA worth)

Modified: Sat Feb 27 13:56:19 2021 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Ziedan Gazel in Miragul's Nightmare. He's located at about 200, 460, 35 (head west from the first clearing, then north to the next clearing)._
You say, 'Hail, Ziedan Gazel'
Ziedan Gazel says 'I am lost and nearly out of hope, friend. Whoever you are, I cannot be sure you are real. So many of us have died here. Only myself and my retainer Utto have survived these inimical shadow creatures, yet I fear we may [never] see home again.'
You say, 'Never?'
Ziedan Gazel says 'We have slain dozens of the creatures but more always come to harass and taunt us until our minds can take no more. We are physically and spiritually exhausted, and now I can feel the tendrils of something dark and sinister plucking at our souls, testing them for ripeness as one would a fruit. Eagerly and hungrily does this [presence] watch us, even as we speak.'
You say, 'What presence?'
Ziedan Gazel says 'I know nothing; I can only feel it around us. The only way to drive it away is to destroy its shapes, its minions which infest this place. If you intend to wander where you will, you may find it beneficial to destroy the [golems and shadows] first, or perhaps you will concentrate on the sorrowful [shades and pools] of darkness. It doesn't matter. All of them must be destroyed.'
You say, 'I'll get some of the golems and shadows...'
Ziedan Gazel says 'Thank you. If you kill enough of them, we just might be able to make our escape.'
You have been assigned the task 'Shades of Black'.
You have agreed to help Ziedan Gazel by destroying some of the threatening shadow creatures which are likely to kill him and his friend Utto before they can make their escape from this place.

You have successfully driven back the shadow creatures and given your allies Ziedan and Utto a chance to survive, for at least a little while longer.

This is a solo kill task that begins with Utto the Haggard in Miragul's Nightmare. He's an ogre located at about 200, 460, 35 (head west from the first clearing, then north to the next clearing).
You say, 'Hail, Utto the Haggard'
Utto the Haggard says 'Great adventures and battles not prepare Utto for hardships like these. Utto suffers much [pain], knowing friends all dead except Ziedan.'
You say, 'Pain?'
Utto the Haggard says 'Utto weary. Utto see too many friends die in palace, killed by things Utto not understand. Attackers without shape, or substance. Unseen foes and forgotten guardians; trapped souls in thrall to whatever controls this place. You fight these things, destroy them for Utto, Utto be [grateful].'
You say, 'Grateful?'
Utto the Haggard says 'Good. Utto like you already. Utto can kill anything Utto understands. Utto not understand unseen things, ghost things. Destroy these. . . make the bad memories of Utto's dying friends screaming not bother Utto so much.'
You have been assigned the task 'An Unseen Presence'.
You have agreed to help Utto the Haggard get revenge on the ghastly unseen forces that killed his friends.
Kill five unseen foes or warriors 0/5 (Miragul's Nightmare)
Kill five forgotten guardians 0/5 (Miragul's Nightmare)
The above mob types are found in the castle area.
Speak to Utto the Haggard after you have dealt with the menaces 0/1 (Miragul's Nightmare)
You say, 'Hail, Utto the Haggard'
Utto the Haggard says 'You good friend, you avenge Utto's friends. I take heart, knowing souls of friends rest easier!'
You have successfully destroyed enough of the phantom threats to have avenged some of Utto's fallen comrades. Perhaps futile in the long run, but the gesture will help to keep Utto's morale where it should be.

# Controlling the Population

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90

This is a solo kill task that begins with Havrin in Morell's Castle. He's a satyr located at the entrance to the shellscale area in the northeast part of the forest.
Note: Two roaming mobs (including a see invis daydream) path into the room he's in.
You say, 'Hail, Havrin'
Havrin says 'Hail and well met, adventurer.  Enjoying your romp through the stuff dreams are made of?  Well, I do have some things to [keep you occupied], if you so wish.'
You say, 'Yes, keep me occupied...'
Havrin says 'I figured you might enjoy that.  There are two issues of note that you might find relaxing as you dream, away from the slaughter of the waking world.  The native flora and fauna are [out of control] in population.  Unfortunately, you could say the same for the [shellscales], which are intent on encroaching on our area.'
You say, 'Out of control?'
Havrin says 'The water serpents have taken over the river, and the hedge walkers killing the green life in their path.  If you could bring their populations [down to size], this may help keep the area healthy and vibrant.'
You say, 'I'll bring them down to size'
Havrin says 'Great, friend.  I appreciate your assistance in the matter.  I know people of your type do not wish to sit idle.'
You have been assigned the task 'Controlling the Population'.
Kill the water serpents overpopulating the river 0/8 (Morell's Castle)
Serpents in the river and water serpents in the area preceding the water maze update this step.
Kill the hedgewalkers causing havoc in the forest 0/4 (Morell's Castle)
Report back to Havrin 0/1 (Morell's Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Havrin'
Havrin says, 'You've done a lot for us, friend.  For now, the populations seem fine, but will not be permanent.  Please let us know if there's anything we can do for you in the meantime.'
Task rewards:
Experience (about 1 AA worth)

Modified: Sun Jan 26 23:04:03 2025 | | _This is a solo kill task that begins with Alisia Sandweaver in Morell's Castle. She's located at the zone-in._
You say, 'Hail, Alisia Sandweaver'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'Dreamer, you've come [too far]. I advise you turn back.'
You say, 'Too far?'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'You've touched the edge of dreams themselves. The [mysteries] of the mind are better not left unraveled.'
You say, 'What mysteries?'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'Why does one dream far differently than another? Why can some remember their dreams in the waking world, and some believe they do not dream at all? Why are hopes and dreams so closely matched? They are better unanswered than to be stumbled upon in the [grains] of sand ahead.'
You say, 'Grains?'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'You are very persistent. Sand is part of the life of all things, from how it is created to how we interact with it. Hundreds, thousands, millions of years ago, it may have made up a being, or a large rock, and finely sifted to such miniscule granules over time. Then such can be transformed by man to sculptures, to glass, or within that glass, even more sand to display the passing of time. Sand is comforting, sand is how many beings are born of Norrath, and how we all [rest].'
You say, 'It helps us rest?'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'You're unaware of that mystery of sand? With your insistence on remaining and inquiring about these mysteries, then I shall send you on a [quest] of knowledge if you wish.'
You say, 'Send me on a quest...'
Alisia Sandweaver says 'The sandmen hold your answer, but they will not give it up easily. Fight them for it, then bring it here, and I shall explain some more on the significance of it.'
You have been assigned the task 'Bring Me a Dream'.

---

Kill the sandmen and search for what they hold 0/7 (Morell's Castle)
Loot Pristine White Sand from the fallen sandmen 0/5 (Morell's Castle)
_There are three sandman spawns in the zone-in area, east of the marionette room. There are many more in the sand castle._
_To get to the sand castle, you have to go through to the northeast part of the forest and into the shellscale area, up and to the second level of the water maze and use the whirlpool to port to the ship. Then from atop the ship, click the ship's wheel to port to the beach area._
_Note: These are not pre-lootable._
Bring the sand back to Alisia Sandweaver for more information 0/5 (Morell's Castle)
Alisia Sandweaver muses over the samples you've given her, her lips twitching into a curl as she brings a small portion of the sand above your head, sprinkling it over you. 'With luck, you will remember this as a dream now. Sleep, and dream, so that the mysteries remain mysteries.'
_Task rewards:_
Experience (about 1 AA worth)
Minor Faction with Dream Delvers

You say, 'Hail, Mrug the Gunner'
Mrug the Gunner says 'Mrug not happy.  Mrug left out of da [booty].  Mrug 'posed to have one and quarter share of booty, but got no booty.'
You say, 'What booty?'
Mrug the Gunner says 'Other pirates hide booty from Mrug, say Mrug too dumb for booty.  But Mrug is good gun.  Mrug earn booty.  Mrug now just want dem [dead], so dey can't use da booty.'

You say, 'I'll kill dem'

You have been assigned the task 'A Fair Share'.
Mrug the Gunner is sad he's out of his booty earned, and at this point doesn't care if he gets his share or not, but just wants the taunting pirates keeping his booty to die.

You have been assigned the task 'Let Them Beg'.

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task begins with Salvonius in Sanctum Somnium. He's found on the balcony on the south side of the zone-in area._
You say, 'Hail, Salvonius'
Salvonius whispers to you, 'Hey, you look like the industrious type. How would you like to perform a small [task] for me?'
You say, 'What task?'
Salvonius says 'Down in Morell's treasure trove, all sorts of wondrous things can be found. In fact, I have had my eye on a few treasures down there for quite some time. If you were able to [procure] some of them for me, I would compensate you greatly.'
You say, 'I'll procure them!'
Salvonius says 'I can't rightly go marching in there myself and be caught pilfering them, so what better than a meddling adventurer? And if you are caught, I can just say I never met you. Sure, it will be risky, but like I said, you will be rewarded for your efforts. What do you [say]?'
You say, 'I say let's do this'
Salvonius says 'Good. I knew you would be up to it. Otherwise, I would have you thrown into the dungeon. Now, let's keep this on the down-low. If anyone asks, we have never seen each other. Head into the trove and obtain the items I seek. They should be lying amongst the piles of gold. I shall await your return. Oh, and be careful. I wouldn't want any of the treasure to get damaged.'
You have been assigned the task 'Stash and Go'.
Salvonius has had his eye on some valuable items in Morell's treasure trove and would like you to steal them for him.

---

_The kill steps and groundspawns in this task are all found in the treasure halls in the west part of the zone (which, incidentally, are full of see-invis leprechauns)._

# Me Gold!

**Level:** 90 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 90
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with Seamus Greendream in Sanctum Somnium. He's a leprechaun found in the southern part of the treasure halls.
You say, 'Hail, Seamus Greendream'
Seamus Greendream says 'Me gold! Someone took me pot of gold! Was it you? No, you are not crafty enough to find me gold. We must find the culprit and get me pot of gold [back]!'
You say, 'Back from...?'
Seamus Greendream says 'Now I remember! I was having a wee bit of mead the other day, when a dirty beggar came up and asked me for some change. I told him no of course, and he got a bit irate. Next thing I know, I wake up with a bit of a headache and me pot of gold was gone! I bet that [beggar] took me gold when I was passed out from drinking too much mead!'
You say, 'A beggar?'
Seamus Greendream says 'He was a real crazy fellow. You have to [find] him and get me gold!'
You have been assigned the task 'Me Gold!'.
Seamus Greendream has had his pot of gold stolen. He believes one of the mad beggars in Sanctum Somnium took it.
Find Seamus' pot of gold and return it to him 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
These drop commonly from mad beggars (in the undead halls as well as the ruined village) and deranged beggars (only in the ruined village).
There are eight different versions of "A Pot of Gold" and only one of them will work for the turn-in. They are lore, so you can loot a bunch of them before attempting a turn-in.
Upon a failed turn-in:
Seamus Greendream says 'This isn't my gold? That beggar must have stolen this from someone else!'
Upon a successful turn-in:
Seamus Greendream says 'Yes! Me gold! Thank you!'
Speak with Seamus 0/1 (Sanctum Somnium)
You say, 'Hail, Seamus Greendream'
Seamus Greendream says 'You have done well. I don't normally give mortals anything, but you can have this since you helped me out.'
158 platinum
Experience
Faction with Dream Delvers
20 Dream MotesRewards:
- Dream Mote [item=90255]
